            BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = profile,
        })
        local avatarFrame = create("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = profile,
        })
        addCorner(avatarFrame, 17)
        addGlowStroke(avatarFrame, Theme.Accent)
        local avatarImg = create("ImageLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
            Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(player.UserId) .. "&w=150&h=150", Parent = avatarFrame,
        })
        addCorner(avatarImg, 17)
        label(profile, tostring(player.DisplayName), 12, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Theme.Text, {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 9), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        })
        label(profile, "Script User • @" .. tostring(player.Name), 9, Enum.Font.Gotham, Theme.SubText, {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 0, 14), Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 26), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
        })

        -- Thanh bên (danh sách tab, cuộn được)
        local sidebar = create("Frame", {
            Name = "Sidebar", Size = UDim2.new(0, SIDEBAR_W, 1, -(TOPBAR_H + PROFILE_H)),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, TOPBAR_H + PROFILE_H), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
            BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = main,
        })
        create("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -1, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = sidebar,
        })
        local navScroll = create("ScrollingFrame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8), Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, Parent = sidebar,
        })
        create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Parent = navScroll })
        win._navScroll = navScroll

        -- Khu vực trang
        local pagesContainer = create("Frame", {
            Name = "Pages", Size = UDim2.new(1, -SIDEBAR_W, 1, -(TOPBAR_H + PROFILE_H)),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, SIDEBAR_W, 0, TOPBAR_H + PROFILE_H), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = main,
        })
        local pageTitle = label(pagesContainer, "", 14, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Theme.Text, {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 6), NoLoc = true,
        })
        local pageSub = label(pagesContainer, win.Author, 9, Enum.Font.Gotham, Theme.SubText, {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 24),
        })
        local pageHolder = create("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -44), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ClipsDescendants = true, Parent = pagesContainer,
        })
        win._pageHolder = pageHolder

        -- thanh sáng dọc trượt tới tab được chọn
        local glow = create("Frame", {
            Name = "Glow", Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 22), BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Visible = false, ZIndex = 5, Parent = sidebar,
        })
        addCorner(glow, 2)
        local function glowTarget(tab)
            local ok, y = pcall(function()
                return tab._navBtn.AbsolutePosition.Y - sidebar.AbsolutePosition.Y + (tab._navBtn.AbsoluteSize.Y - 22) / 2
            end)
            if ok then return y end
            return 6
        end
        local glowY = nil
        win._placeGlow = function()
            if win._current then
                glow.Visible = true
                glowY = glowTarget(win._current)
                glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, glowY)
            end
        end
        -- mỗi khung hình: thanh sáng trượt mượt tới vị trí THỰC của nút tab đang chọn (đi theo cả khi cuộn thanh bên)
        win._glowStep = function(dt)
            if not win._current then return end
            local target = glowTarget(win._current)
            if glowY == nil then glowY = target end
            glowY = glowY + (target - glowY) * math.min(1, dt * 16)
            glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, glowY)
            local h = sidebar.AbsoluteSize.Y
            glow.Visible = (target > -4 and target < h - 10) -- tab bị cuộn ra ngoài thì ẩn thanh sáng
        end

        -- mọi thứ trong trang cần làm mờ: chữ, nền, viền, ảnh (nhớ độ mờ gốc để trả lại đúng)
        local function fadeTargets(root)
            local list = {}
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then table.insert(list, { d, "TextTransparency" }) end
                if d:IsA("ImageLabel") or d:IsA("ImageButton") then table.insert(list, { d, "ImageTransparency" }) end
                if d:IsA("GuiObject") then table.insert(list, { d, "BackgroundTransparency" }) end
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") then table.insert(list, { d, "Transparency" }) end
            end
            return list
        end
        local function storeOrig(list)
            for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                local ok, v = pcall(function() return e[1][e[2]] end)
                e[3] = ok and v or 0
            end
        end
        local function setAll(list, useOrig, value)
            for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                pcall(function() e[1][e[2]] = useOrig and e[3] or value end)
            end
        end
        local function tweenAll(list, toOrig, value, t)
            for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                tween(e[1], { [e[2]] = toOrig and e[3] or value }, t, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
            end
        end

        local function applyNav(tab)
            for _, t in ipairs(win._tabs) do
                local active = (t == tab)
                t._navBtn.BackgroundTransparency = active and 0.8 or 1
                t._navLabel.TextColor3 = active and Theme.Accent or Theme.SubText
                t._indicator.Visible = false
            end
        end

        win._switching = false
        win._selectInstant = function(tab)
            if win._switching or win._current == tab then return end
            if win._current then win._current._page.Visible = false end
            tab._page.Visible = true
            win._current = tab
            applyNav(tab)
            Loc.bind(pageTitle, "Text", tab.Title)
            glow.Visible = true
        end
        win._select = function(tab)
            if win._switching or win._current == tab then return end
            local old = win._current
            -- tab đầu tiên: hiện ngay không hiệu ứng
            if not old then
                tab._page.Visible = true
                win._current = tab
                applyNav(tab)
                Loc.bind(pageTitle, "Text", tab.Title)
                return
            end
            win._switching = true -- chốt khóa: chống bấm nhanh nhiều tab
            win._current = tab
            applyNav(tab)
            glow.Visible = true
            Loc.bind(pageTitle, "Text", tab.Title)
            task.spawn(function()
                -- Giai đoạn 1: tab cũ tan biến (0.5s)
                local oldList = fadeTargets(old._page)
                storeOrig(oldList)
                tweenAll(oldList, false, 1, 0.5)
                task.wait(0.5)
                old._page.Visible = false
                setAll(oldList, true) -- trả độ mờ gốc (trang đã ẩn nên không thấy)
                -- Giai đoạn 2: khoảng lặng (2s)
                task.wait(2)
                -- Giai đoạn 3: tab mới hiện ra (1.2s, Linear)
                local newList = fadeTargets(tab._page)
                storeOrig(newList)
                setAll(newList, false, 1)
                tab._page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                tab._page.Visible = true
                tweenAll(newList, true, nil, 1.2)
                task.wait(1.25)
                setAll(newList, true)
                win._switching = false
            end)
        end

        -- Kéo thả cửa sổ bằng thanh trên
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
        topBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = i.Position
                startPos = main.Position
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = i.Position - dragStart
                main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)

        -- Kéo thả nút toggle (phải kéo quá 15px mới tính là kéo, tránh bấm nhầm trên mobile)
        local tDragging, tStart, tPos, tMoved = false, nil, nil, false
        toggleBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                tDragging = true
                tStart = input.Position
                tPos = toggleBtn.Position
                tMoved = false
            end
        end)
        toggleBtn.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if tDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = input.Position - tStart
                if d.Magnitude > 15 then
                    tMoved = true
                    toggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(tPos.X.Scale, tPos.X.Offset + d.X, tPos.Y.Scale, tPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end
        end)
        toggleBtn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if tDragging and not tMoved then win:Toggle() end
                tDragging = false
            end
        end)
        toggleBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(toggleBtn, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48) }, 0.15) end)
        toggleBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(toggleBtn, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(45, 45) }, 0.15) end)

        -- ================== CẤU HÌNH: Aur_Meow Config / ValuedScript.txt ==================
        win.ToggleBtn = toggleBtn
        local ROOT = win.Folder
        local VALUED_FILE = ROOT .. "/ValuedScript.txt"
        local MUSIC_DIR = ROOT .. "/Files_Music"

        local function ensureFolders()
            pcall(function()
                if makefolder and isfolder then
                    if not isfolder(ROOT) then makefolder(ROOT) end
                    if not isfolder(MUSIC_DIR) then makefolder(MUSIC_DIR) end
                end
            end)
        end
        ensureFolders()

        -- loại bỏ lưu trữ cũ của các phiên bản trước
        pcall(function()
            for _, old in ipairs({ "EvadeFarm", "Mẹ mày béo" }) do
                if isfolder and delfolder and isfolder(old) then pcall(delfolder, old) end
            end
        end)

        local function q(str) return '"' .. tostring(str):gsub("\\", "\\\\"):gsub('"', '\\"') .. '"' end

        -- mỗi chức năng là một khối riêng, cách nhau 1 dòng trống
        local function serializeItem(item)
            local ok, v = pcall(item.obj._get)
            if not ok then return nil end
            local head = "local Tab://" .. item.tab .. " | " .. item.kind
            local name = tostring(item.name):gsub('"', '\\"')
            if item.kind == "Toggle" then
                return { head, '- Name_Toggle: "' .. name .. '"', "- ActivateValue = " .. tostring(v == true), "- Directory-Configs = AutoSaves" }
            elseif item.kind == "Dropdown" then
                local chosen = ""
                if type(v) == "table" then
                    local parts = {}
                    for _, x in ipairs(v) do table.insert(parts, q(x)) end
                    chosen = table.concat(parts, ", ")
                elseif v ~= nil then
                    chosen = q(v)
                else
                    chosen = '""'
                end
                return { head, '- Name_Dropdown: "' .. name .. '"', "- TheChosenValue = (" .. chosen .. ")", "-  ActivateType:Dropdown://Configs", "- Directory-Configs = AutoSaves" }
            elseif item.kind == "Slider" then
                return { head, '- Name_Slider: "' .. name .. '"', '- Value {"' .. tostring(tonumber(v) or 0) .. '")', "- Directory-Configs = AutoSaves" }
            elseif item.kind == "Colorpicker" then
                if type(v) == "table" and v.r then
                    local c = string.format("Color3.fromRGB(%d, %d, %d)", math.floor(v.r * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(v.g * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(v.b * 255 + 0.5))
                    return { head, '- Name_Colorpicker: "' .. name .. '"', "- Value = " .. c, "- Directory-Configs = AutoSaves" }
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        function win:SerializeValues()
            local out = {}
            for _, tab in ipairs(self._tabs) do
                for _, item in ipairs(self._items) do
                    if item.tab == tab.Title then
                        local block = serializeItem(item)
                        if block then
                            for _, l in ipairs(block) do table.insert(out, l) end
                            table.insert(out, "")
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return table.concat(out, "\n")
        end

        function win:SaveValues()
            pcall(function()
                ensureFolders()
                writefile(VALUED_FILE, self:SerializeValues())
            end)
        end

        -- đọc ValuedScript.txt -> danh sách { tab, kind, name, raw }
        local function parseValued(text)
            local entries, cur = {}, nil
            local function finish()
                if cur and cur.name and cur.raw ~= nil then table.insert(entries, cur) end
                cur = nil
            end
            for line in (text .. "\n"):gmatch("(.-)\r?\n") do
                local tabName, kind = line:match("^local Tab://(.-) | (%a+)%s*$")
                if tabName then
                    finish()
                    cur = { tab = tabName, kind = kind }
                elseif cur then
                    local nm = line:match('^%- Name_%a+: "(.*)"%s*$')
                    if nm then
                        cur.name = (nm:gsub('\\"', '"'))
                    else
                        local av = line:match("^%- ActivateValue = (%a+)")
                        local cv = line:match("^%- TheChosenValue = %((.*)%)%s*$")
                        local sv = line:match('^%- Value {"(.-)"%)')
                        local colv = line:match("^%- Value = (Color3.-)%s*$")
                        if av then cur.raw = av
                        elseif cv then cur.raw = cv
                        elseif sv then cur.raw = sv
                        elseif colv then cur.raw = colv end
                    end
                end
            end
            finish()
            return entries
        end

        local function convertRaw(kind, raw, multi)
            if kind == "Toggle" then return raw == "true" end
            if kind == "Slider" then return tonumber(raw) end
            if kind == "Dropdown" then
                local list = {}
                for item in raw:gmatch('"(.-)"') do table.insert(list, (item:gsub('\\"', '"'):gsub("\\\\", "\\"))) end
                if multi then return list end
                if list[1] == nil or list[1] == "" then return nil end
                return list[1]
            end
            if kind == "Colorpicker" then
                local r, g, b = raw:match("Color3%.fromRGB%((%d+),%s*(%d+),%s*(%d+)%)")
                if r then return { r = tonumber(r) / 255, g = tonumber(g) / 255, b = tonumber(b) / 255 } end
            end
            return nil
        end

        -- ép các chức năng về trạng thái đã lưu (callback chạy như bật/chọn thật)
        function win:LoadValues()
            local ok, text = pcall(function()
                if isfile and readfile and isfile(VALUED_FILE) then return readfile(VALUED_FILE) end
                return nil
            end)
            self._loading = true
            if ok and type(text) == "string" and text ~= "" then
                local entries = parseValued(text)
                -- gom theo khoá (tab|loại|tên|lần xuất hiện) để xử lý tên trùng
                local seenFile, byKey = {}, {}
                for _, e in ipairs(entries) do
                    local base = e.tab .. "|" .. e.kind .. "|" .. e.name
                    seenFile[base] = (seenFile[base] or 0) + 1
                    byKey[base .. "|" .. seenFile[base]] = e
                end
                local function find(item)
                    local base = item.tab .. "|" .. item.kind .. "|" .. item.name
                    item._occ = item._occ
                    return base
                end
                -- Auto configs tắt trong file => không ép các chức năng khác
                local autoOff = false
                for _, e in ipairs(entries) do
                    if e.kind == "Toggle" and e.name == "Auto configs" and e.raw == "false" then autoOff = true end
                end
                local seenItem = {}
                for _, item in ipairs(self._items) do
                    local base = find(item)
                    seenItem[base] = (seenItem[base] or 0) + 1
                    local e = byKey[base .. "|" .. seenItem[base]]
                    if e then
                        local isAuto = (item.kind == "Toggle" and item.name == "Auto configs")
                        if not autoOff or isAuto then
                            local v = convertRaw(item.kind, e.raw, item.obj._multi)
                            if v ~= nil and item.obj._load then
                                if isAuto and autoOff then
                                    pcall(function() item.obj:Set(false, true) end)
                                    self._autoConfigs = false
                                else
                                    pcall(item.obj._load, v)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if autoOff then self._autoConfigs = false end
            end
            self._loading = false
            self._cfgReady = true
            if self._autoConfigs then self:SaveValues() end
        end

        function win:SetAutoConfigs(on)
            self._autoConfigs = (on == true)
            if self._cfgReady then self:SaveValues() end -- ghi trạng thái cuối cùng (kể cả khi tắt)
        end

        local saveQueued = false
        win._markDirty = function()
            if win._autoConfigs and win._cfgReady and not win._loading and not saveQueued then
                saveQueued = true
                task.delay(1.5, function()
                    saveQueued = false
                    if win._autoConfigs then win:SaveValues() end
                end)
            end
        end

        -- tương thích cách gọi cũ: ConfigManager:CreateConfig(name) -> :Load() / :Save()
        win.ConfigManager = {
            CreateConfig = function(_, name)
                local cfg = { Name = tostring(name or "Config") }
                function cfg:Save() if win._autoConfigs then win:SaveValues() end end
                function cfg:Load() win:LoadValues() end
                win._config = cfg
                return cfg
            end,
        }

        -- ================== THEME + ẢNH NỀN ==================
        local bgImage = create("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "BgImage", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop,
            Image = "", ImageTransparency = 0.55, ZIndex = 0, Visible = false, Parent = main,
        })
        addCorner(bgImage, 16)

        local THEME_PROPS = { "BackgroundColor3", "TextColor3", "ImageColor3", "PlaceholderColor3", "ScrollBarImageColor3" }
        local function ckey(c)
            return string.format("%d,%d,%d", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        end
        local function remap(root, map)
            if not root then return end
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                for _, prop in ipairs(THEME_PROPS) do
                    local ok, c = pcall(function() return d[prop] end)
                    if ok and typeof(c) == "Color3" then
                        local n = map[ckey(c)]
                        if n then
                            local tw = tween(d, { [prop] = n }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                            if not tw then pcall(function() d[prop] = n end) end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if d:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    local n = map[ckey(d.Color)]
                    if n then
                        local tw = tween(d, { Color = n }, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                        if not tw then pcall(function() d.Color = n end) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        function win:ApplyTheme(name)
            local t = Lib.Themes[name]
            if not t then return false end
            local map = {}
            for _, k in ipairs({ "Panel", "Panel2", "Accent", "Accent2", "SubText" }) do
                local old = Theme[k]
                local new = t[k]
                if old and new then map[ckey(old)] = new end
            end
            remap(self.Gui, map)
            remap(notifyGui, map)
            for _, k in ipairs({ "Panel", "Panel2", "Accent", "Accent2", "SubText" }) do Theme[k] = t[k] end
            ThemeFx.Speed = t.Speed or 1
            ThemeFx.Pulse = t.Pulse or 0.15
            if self._refreshAura then self._refreshAura() end
            self._themeName = name
            return true
        end

        -- dùng ID ảnh Roblox làm hình nền cho UI ("" = xóa ảnh nền)
        function win:ApplyImage(id)
            id = tostring(id or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
            if id == "" then
                bgImage.Visible = false
                bgImage.Image = ""
                main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                return true
            end
            local num = id:match("(%d+)")
            if not num then return false end
            bgImage.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. num
            bgImage.Visible = true
            main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
            return true
        end

        -- ================== KIỂM SOÁT UI ==================
        win._lastTap = 0

        -- Ẩn UI (không phá hủy): chạm 3 lần vào vùng góc trái màn hình hoặc nhấn RightShift để hiện lại
        function win:HideUI()
            if self._hidden or self._destroyed then return end
            self._hidden = true
            self:Close(true)
            toggleBtn.Visible = false
            self._lastTap = 0
            Lib:Notify({ Title = "Ẩn UI", Content = "Chạm 2 lần vào màn hình (hoặc nhấn RightShift) để hiện lại.", Duration = 5 })
        end

        function win:ShowUI()
            if self._destroyed then return end
            self._hidden = false
            toggleBtn.Visible = true
            self:Open()
        end

        function win:DestroyUI()
            if self._destroyed then return end
            self._destroyed = true
            pcall(function() self:SaveValues() end)
            pcall(function() self.Gui:Destroy() end)
            pcall(function() if notifyGui then notifyGui:Destroy() end end)
            if win._uisConn then pcall(function() win._uisConn:Disconnect() end) end
            if win._auraConn then pcall(function() win._auraConn:Disconnect() end) end
        end

        -- Phát hiện chạm màn hình (giống Anti-AFK): đang ẩn mà chạm 2 lần liên tiếp bất kỳ đâu thì hiện UI
        win._uisConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
            if win._destroyed then return end
            -- phím tắt riêng cho người dùng bàn phím (chỉ khi đang ở chế độ PC)
            if Lib.IsPC() and not gpe and not win._hidden then
                local kc = input.KeyCode
                local typing = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() ~= nil
                if (kc == Enum.KeyCode.Slash or (kc == Enum.KeyCode.F and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl))) and not typing then
                    if win._openSearch then win._openSearch() end
                    return
                elseif kc == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and win._closeSearch then
                    win._closeSearch()
                elseif (kc == Enum.KeyCode.Up or kc == Enum.KeyCode.Down) and win._open and not typing and not win._switching then
                    local idx = 1
                    for i, t in ipairs(win._tabs) do if t == win._current then idx = i end end
                    idx = idx + ((kc == Enum.KeyCode.Down) and 1 or -1)
                    if win._tabs[idx] then win._select(win._tabs[idx]) end
                    return
                end
            end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift and not gpe then
                if win._hidden then win:ShowUI() else win:HideUI() end
                return
            end
            if win._hidden and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then
                local now = os.clock()
                if now - win._lastTap <= 0.5 then
                    win._lastTap = 0
                    win:ShowUI()
                else
                    win._lastTap = now
                end
            end
        end)

        -- Màn hình chào + tự mở UI (luôn có đường dọn dẹp riêng, không bao giờ kẹt màn hình)
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local splash = create("Frame", {
                    Name = "Splash", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 5, 10),
                    BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 500, Parent = gui,
                })
                local welcome = create("TextLabel", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -30), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Welcome " .. tostring(player.DisplayName), TextColor3 = Theme.Accent,
                    TextSize = 26, Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold, ZIndex = 501, Parent = splash,
                })
                local loading = create("TextLabel", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 20), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = win.Title .. " Loading...", TextColor3 = Theme.SubText,
                    TextSize = 14, Font = Enum.Font.Gotham, ZIndex = 501, Parent = splash,
                })
                task.delay(1.6, function()
                    tween(welcome, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
                    tween(loading, { TextTransparency = 1 }, 0.3)
                    tween(splash, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, 0.4)
                end)
                task.delay(2.3, function() pcall(function() splash:Destroy() end) end)
            end)
        end)
        task.delay(2.0, function()
            toggleBtn.Visible = true
            win:Open()
        end)
        task.delay(3.2, function()
            Lib:Notify({
                Title = "Thiết bị",
                Content = Lib.IsPC() and "Đã phát hiện bàn phím/chuột: dùng phím của máy tính, các nút giả lập cho điện thoại đã được ẩn."
                    or "Đã phát hiện thiết bị cảm ứng: dùng các nút giả lập trên màn hình.",
                Duration = 5,
            })
        end)

        return win
    end

    return Lib
end)()

local ForceResetMovement = function()
    pcall(function()
        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player or not player.Character then return end
        local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hum then
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum.Sit = false
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
        if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end
        for _, v in ipairs(player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then v.Anchored = false end
        end
    end)
end

do
    local clientName = "Unknown"
    pcall(function()
        if type(identifyexecutor) == "function" then
            local n = identifyexecutor()
            if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "" then clientName = n end
        end
    end)
    local base = ""
    local candidates = { "", "Workspace/" }
    -- Arceus X: ưu tiên ghi vào thư mục Workspace của client
    if string.find(string.lower(clientName), "arceus", 1, true) then candidates = { "Workspace/", "" } end
    for _, b in ipairs(candidates) do
        local usable = true
        if b ~= "" and not (type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder("Workspace")) then usable = false end
        if usable then
            local probe = b .. "AurMeow_probe.txt"
            local okW = pcall(function() writefile(probe, "1") end)
            if okW and type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(probe) then
                pcall(function() if type(delfile) == "function" then delfile(probe) end end)
                base = b
                break
            end
        end
    end
    _G.EvadeHub_ClientName = clientName
    _G.EvadeHub_ConfigRoot = base .. "Aur_Meow Configs"
end

_G.EvadeHub_AntisTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Antis & Automatic", Icon = "shield" })

local Config = {
    AvoidNextbot = false,
    AvoidNextbotRange = 40,
    AvoidNextbotMode = "Players",
}

local AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = nil
local AvoidNextbot_IsHiding = false
local AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations = {}
local AvoidNextbot_LastSafeCheck = 0
local AvoidNextbot_OnWallPos = nil

local function AvoidNextbot_GetHRP()
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not player.Character then return nil end
    return player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(pos, radius)
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetAttribute("Team") == "Nextbot" then
            local hrp = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or obj:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or obj:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            if hrp and (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude < radius then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function AvoidNextbot_FindClosestBot(hrp)
    local closestBot, closestDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetAttribute("Team") == "Nextbot" then
            local botRoot = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or obj:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or obj:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            if botRoot then
                local d = (hrp.Position - botRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if d < closestDist then
                    closestDist = d
                    closestBot = { Model = obj, Position = botRoot.Position, Distance = d }
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closestBot, closestDist
end

local function AvoidNextbot_GetPlayersTarget(botPos)
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local bestTarget, maxDist = nil, -1
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
            local pHrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if pHrp then
                local pDist = (botPos - pHrp.Position).Magnitude
                if pDist > maxDist then
                    maxDist = pDist
                    bestTarget = pHrp.Position
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if bestTarget then return bestTarget + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) end
    return nil
end

local function AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocations(centerPos)
    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character }

    local safeList = {}
    local attempts = 0
    while #safeList < 10 and attempts < 100 do
        attempts = attempts + 1
        local angle = math.rad(math.random(0, 360))
        local dist = math.random(30, 300)
        local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * dist, 0, math.sin(angle) * dist)
        local checkPos = centerPos + offset
        local hit = workspace:Raycast(checkPos + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0), Vector3.new(0, -100, 0), rayParams)
        if hit and hit.Instance and hit.Instance.CanCollide then
            local groundPos = hit.Position
            if not AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(groundPos, 20) then
                table.insert(safeList, groundPos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            end
        end
    end
    return safeList
end

local function AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocationsOnWall(wall)
    if not wall or not wall.Parent then return {} end
    local wallPart = wall:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if not wallPart then return {} end

    local wallPos = wallPart.Position
    local wallSize = wallPart.Size

    local safeList = {}
    local gridN = 8
    for i = -gridN, gridN do
        for j = -gridN, gridN do
            local offsetX = (i / gridN) * (wallSize.X / 2 - 5)
            local offsetZ = (j / gridN) * (wallSize.Z / 2 - 5)
            local candidatePos = Vector3.new(
                wallPos.X + offsetX,
                wallPos.Y + 2,
                wallPos.Z + offsetZ
            )
            if not AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(candidatePos, 20) then
                table.insert(safeList, candidatePos)
            end
        end
    end
    return safeList
end

local function AvoidNextbot_ApplyTeleport(botPos, hrp)
    local mode = Config.AvoidNextbotMode
    local targetPos = nil

    if mode == "Players" then
        targetPos = AvoidNextbot_GetPlayersTarget(botPos)
        if not targetPos then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
            return
        end
        if not AvoidNextbot_IsHiding then AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = hrp.Position end
    elseif mode == "Wall_Safe" then
        if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then
            if CreateWall then CreateWall() end
            task.wait(0.3)
            if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then return end
        end
        if not AvoidNextbot_IsHiding then
            AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = hrp.Position
        end
        if #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations == 0 then
            AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations = AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocationsOnWall(wallModel)
        end
        if #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations > 0 then
            local bestIdx, bestDist = 1, -1
            for idx, loc in ipairs(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations) do
                local d = (loc - botPos).Magnitude
                if d > bestDist then bestDist = d; bestIdx = idx end
            end
            targetPos = AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations[bestIdx]
            table.remove(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, bestIdx)
        end
        AvoidNextbot_IsHiding = true
    elseif mode == "Designated location" then
        if not AvoidNextbot_IsHiding then AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = hrp.Position end
        if #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations == 0 then
            AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations = AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocations(hrp.Position)
        end
        if #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations > 0 then
            local bestIdx, bestDist = 1, -1
            for idx, loc in ipairs(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations) do
                local d = (loc - botPos).Magnitude
                if d > bestDist then bestDist = d; bestIdx = idx end
            end
            targetPos = AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations[bestIdx]
            table.remove(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, bestIdx)
        else
            targetPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
        end
    end

    if targetPos then
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    end
end

local function AvoidNextbot_RestorePosition()
    if AvoidNextbot_IsHiding and AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos then
        local hrp = AvoidNextbot_GetHRP()
        if hrp then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
    end
    AvoidNextbot_IsHiding = false
    AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = nil
    AvoidNextbot_OnWallPos = nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if not Config.AvoidNextbot then continue end
        if Config.AvoidNextbotMode == "Players" then continue end
        if #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations < 5 then
            if Config.AvoidNextbotMode == "Wall_Safe" then
                if wallModel and wallModel.Parent then
                    local newLocs = AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocationsOnWall(wallModel)
                    for _, loc in ipairs(newLocs) do
                        if not AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(loc, 20) then
                            table.insert(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, loc)
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif Config.AvoidNextbotMode == "Designated location" then
                local hrp = AvoidNextbot_GetHRP()
                if hrp then
                    local newLocs = AvoidNextbot_FindSafeLocations(hrp.Position)
                    for _, loc in ipairs(newLocs) do
                        if not AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(loc, 20) then
                            table.insert(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, loc)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for i = #AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, 1, -1 do
            if AvoidNextbot_IsBotNearPosition(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations[i], 20) then
                table.remove(AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations, i)
            end
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if not Config.AvoidNextbot then
            if AvoidNextbot_IsHiding then AvoidNextbot_RestorePosition() end
            continue
        end
        local hrp = AvoidNextbot_GetHRP()
        if not hrp then continue end
        local closestBot, dist = AvoidNextbot_FindClosestBot(hrp)
        if not closestBot then
            if AvoidNextbot_IsHiding then
                AvoidNextbot_RestorePosition()
            end
            continue
        end
        if dist <= Config.AvoidNextbotRange then
            AvoidNextbot_ApplyTeleport(closestBot.Position, hrp)
            task.wait(0.3)
        else
            if AvoidNextbot_IsHiding and Config.AvoidNextbotMode ~= "Players" then
                AvoidNextbot_RestorePosition()
            end
        end
    end
end)

workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
    if child.Name == "Game" then
        AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations = {}
        AvoidNextbot_IsHiding = false
        AvoidNextbot_OriginalPos = nil
        AvoidNextbot_OnWallPos = nil
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- ===== SECTION SPEED + JUMP (trên cùng tab Character) =======
-- ============================================================
;(function()
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    _G.EvadeHub_Move = _G.EvadeHub_Move or {}
    local M = _G.EvadeHub_Move
    for _, c in pairs(M.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    M.conns = {}
    M.speed, M.jumpPower, M.jumpBoost = 16, 50, false

    local function CurrentHumanoid()
        local ch = LP.Character
        return ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    end

    local speedSection = _G.EvadeHub_CFrameTab:Section({ Title = "Speed + Jump", Opened = true })

    speedSection:Input({
        Title = "Movement Speed",
        Desc = "Nhập tốc độ cho CFrame Speed Boost (không giới hạn, mặc định 16)",
        Value = "16",
        Placeholder = "Nhập số...",
        Flag = "MovementSpeed",
        Callback = function(text)
            local n = tonumber(text)
            if n then M.speed = n end
        end,
    })

    speedSection:Toggle({
        Title = "CFrame Speed Boost",
        Desc = "Dịch CFrame theo hướng đi mỗi frame (không đụng WalkSpeed)",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "CFrameSpeedBoost",
        Callback = function(v)
            if M.conns.cframe then M.conns.cframe:Disconnect(); M.conns.cframe = nil end -- không chồng vòng lặp khi bật/tắt nhiều lần
            if v then
                M.conns.cframe = RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    local char = LP.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if char and hum and hrp and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
                        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + hum.MoveDirection * math.max(M.speed, 1) * 0.08
                    end
                end)
            end
        end,
    })

    speedSection:Input({
        Title = "Jump Power Multiplier",
        Desc = "Nhập JumpPower (không giới hạn, mặc định 50); chỉ có tác dụng khi Jump Boost Mode bật",
        Value = "50",
        Placeholder = "Nhập số...",
        Flag = "JumpPowerValue",
        Callback = function(text)
            local n = tonumber(text)
            if n then
                M.jumpPower = n
                local hum = CurrentHumanoid()
                if hum then hum.JumpPower = n end
            end
        end,
    })

    speedSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Jump Boost Mode",
        Desc = "Bật = độ cao nhảy tính theo JumpPower (UseJumpPower), tắt = theo JumpHeight",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "JumpBoostMode",
        Callback = function(v)
            M.jumpBoost = v
            local hum = CurrentHumanoid()
            if hum then hum.UseJumpPower = v end
        end,
    })

    -- Áp dụng lại cho nhân vật mới sau khi hồi sinh (khi Jump Boost Mode đang bật)
    M.conns.charAdded = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if hum and M.jumpBoost then
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = M.jumpPower
        end
    end)
end)()

local avoidSection = characterTab:Section({ Title = "Avoid Next-Bot", Opened = true })

avoidSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Avoid Next-Bot",
    Desc = "Tự động né khi Nextbot lại gần",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "AvoidNextbot",
    Callback = function(s)
        Config.AvoidNextbot = s
        if s then
            if Config.AvoidNextbotMode == "Wall_Safe" then
                if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then
                    if CreateWall then CreateWall() end
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🤖 Avoid Next-Bot", Content = "✔️ Đã bật - Mode: " .. Config.AvoidNextbotMode, Duration = 3 })
            end)
        else
            AvoidNextbot_RestorePosition()
            AvoidNextbot_SafeLocations = {}
            pcall(function()
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🤖 Avoid Next-Bot", Content = "❌ Đã tắt", Duration = 3 })
            end)
        end
    end,
})

-- ============================================================
-- ===== SECTION VOTE + TIME ==================================
-- ============================================================
local Timer = {
    gui = nil,
    bgAnim = nil,
    loopTask = nil,
    isDragging = false,
    startPos = nil,
    startPosUI = nil,
}

local function removeTimer()
    if Timer.bgAnim then Timer.bgAnim:Disconnect(); Timer.bgAnim = nil end
    if Timer.gui then pcall(function() Timer.gui.sg:Destroy() end); Timer.gui = nil end
    Timer.loopTask = nil
    Timer.isDragging = false
end

local function createTimer()
    if Timer.gui then return end
    local playerGui = game.Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    
    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name = "LegacyTimer_GG_EVADE_Lol"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
    sg.DisplayOrder = 2
    sg.Parent = playerGui
    
    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
    container.Name = "TimerContainer"
    container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.25, 0)
    container.Size = UDim2.new(0.18, 0, 0.11, 0)
    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    container.Parent = sg
    
    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    bg.Parent = container
    local bgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    bgCorner.Parent = bg
    local bgStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    bgStroke.Thickness = 3
    bgStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) -- Base
    bgStroke.Parent = bg
    
    -- Gradient Trắng -> Xanh dương -> Trắng
    local strokeGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    strokeGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new{
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), -- Trắng
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 140, 255)), -- Xanh dương
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), -- Trắng
    }
    strokeGrad.Parent = bgStroke

    local statusText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    statusText.Name = "StatusText"
    statusText.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    statusText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.3, 0)
    statusText.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.35, 0)
    statusText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    statusText.Text = "WAITING"
    statusText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    statusText.TextScaled = true
    statusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    statusText.Parent = container

    local countdownText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    countdownText.Name = "CountdownText"
    countdownText.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    countdownText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.7, 0)
    countdownText.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0.45, 0)
    countdownText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    countdownText.Text = "??:??"
    countdownText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    countdownText.TextScaled = true
    countdownText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    countdownText.Parent = container
    
    Timer.gui = { sg = sg, container = container, status = statusText, count = countdownText, strokeGrad = strokeGrad }

    container.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            Timer.isDragging = true
            Timer.startPos = input.Position
            Timer.startPosUI = container.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    Timer.isDragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    container.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if Timer.isDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - Timer.startPos
            container.Position = UDim2.new(
                Timer.startPosUI.X.Scale, Timer.startPosUI.X.Offset + delta.X,
                Timer.startPosUI.Y.Scale, Timer.startPosUI.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)

    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui_ = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while Timer.gui do
            local remaining = nil
            pcall(function()
                if playerGui_ then
                    local shared = playerGui_:FindFirstChild("Shared")
                    if shared then
                        local popups = shared:FindFirstChild("Popups")
                        if popups then
                            local vote = popups:FindFirstChild("Vote")
                            if vote then
                                local countdown = vote:FindFirstChild("Countdown")
                                if countdown then
                                    local rem = countdown:FindFirstChild("Remaining")
                                    if rem then remaining = rem end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            
            if remaining then
                local secondsStr = remaining.Text:match("(%d+)")
                local timeLeft = tonumber(secondsStr) or 0
                local m = math.floor(timeLeft / 60)
                local s = timeLeft % 60
                Timer.gui.count.Text = string.format("%d:%02d", m, s)
                Timer.gui.status.Text = "ROUND ACTIVE"
                Timer.gui.status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

                -- Chuyển đỏ khi còn 30s
                if timeLeft <= 30 then
                    Timer.gui.count.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                else
                    Timer.gui.count.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                end
            else
                Timer.gui.count.Text = "??:??"
                Timer.gui.count.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                Timer.gui.status.Text = "WAITING"
                Timer.gui.status.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)

    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    Timer.bgAnim = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not Timer.gui then return end
        -- Xoay viền Gradient liên tục
        Timer.gui.strokeGrad.Rotation = (Timer.gui.strokeGrad.Rotation + 90 * dt) % 360
    end)
end

local timerSection = _G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Section({ Title = "Display Real-time", Opened = true, Order = 500, NoDivider = true })
timerSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Display Time Round",
    Desc = "Show 3D countdown HUD",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "RoundTimer",
    Callback = function(s)
        if s then createTimer() else removeTimer() end
    end,
})

})

-- ============================================================
-- ===== SECTION ESP ==========================================
-- ============================================================
-- FIX: khai báo trước để vòng lặp ESP bên dưới thấy được (trước đây khai báo muộn nên bị nil)
local EventItems, SpecialEvent
local GetValidEventItemNames, GetValidSpecialItemNames

local espPlayersEnabled = false
local espBotsEnabled = false
local espItemsEnabled = false
local notifyAllEnabled = false

local espPlayerObjects = {}
local espBotObjects = {}
local espItemObjects = {}

-- Màu ESP do colorpicker chọn (dùng _G để không thêm biến local ở cấp ngoài cùng)
_G.EvadeHub_ESPColor = {
    player = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
    bot = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
    item = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),
}

local notifiedPlayers = {}
local notifiedBots = {}
local notifiedItems = {}

-- Hàm tạo Label chung
local function createLabel(parent, text, color)
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "ESP_Billboard"
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 20) -- Kích thước nhỏ gọn
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.MaxDistance = 1000
    billboard.Adornee = parent
    billboard.Parent = parent

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text
    label.TextColor3 = color
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = billboard

    return billboard, label
end

-- ESP Players
local function clearPlayerESP()
    for _, obj in pairs(espPlayerObjects) do
        if obj.highlight then obj.highlight:Destroy() end
        if obj.billboard then obj.billboard:Destroy() end
    end
    espPlayerObjects = {}
end

local function createPlayerESP(targetPlayer)
    if espPlayerObjects[targetPlayer] then return end
    if not targetPlayer.Character then return end
    local char = targetPlayer.Character
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not head then return end

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.FillColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player
    highlight.OutlineColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.6
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee = char
    highlight.Parent = workspace

    local billboard, label = createLabel(head, targetPlayer.Name, _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player)
    espPlayerObjects[targetPlayer] = { highlight = highlight, billboard = billboard, label = label, char = char }
end

-- ESP Bots
local function clearBotESP()
    for _, obj in pairs(espBotObjects) do
        if obj.highlight then obj.highlight:Destroy() end
        if obj.billboard then obj.billboard:Destroy() end
    end
    espBotObjects = {}
end

local function createBotESP(botModel)
    if espBotObjects[botModel] then return end
    local botRoot = botModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or botModel:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or botModel:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not botRoot then return end
    local botHead = botModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or botRoot

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.FillColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.bot
    highlight.OutlineColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.bot
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.6
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee = botModel
    highlight.Parent = workspace

    local billboard, label = createLabel(botHead, botModel.Name, _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.bot)
    espBotObjects[botModel] = { highlight = highlight, billboard = billboard, label = label, root = botRoot }
end


-- Update Loops Players
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.5) do
        if espPlayersEnabled then
            for _, plr in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer then
                    local old = espPlayerObjects[plr]
                    if old and (old.char ~= plr.Character or not old.billboard or not old.billboard.Parent or not old.highlight or not old.highlight.Parent) then
                        if old.highlight then pcall(function() old.highlight:Destroy() end) end
                        if old.billboard then pcall(function() old.billboard:Destroy() end) end
                        espPlayerObjects[plr] = nil
                    end
                    if plr.Character and not espPlayerObjects[plr] then
                        createPlayerESP(plr)
                    end
                    if espPlayerObjects[plr] and espPlayerObjects[plr].char then
                        local obj = espPlayerObjects[plr]
                        local hum = obj.char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local hrp = obj.char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local myHrp = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        
                        if hrp and myHrp and obj.label then
                            local dist = math.floor((hrp.Position - myHrp.Position).Magnitude)
                            obj.label.Text = string.format("%s | %dm", plr.Name, dist)
                        elseif obj.label then
                            obj.label.Text = plr.Name
                        end

                        if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then
                            if obj.label then obj.label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0) end
                            if obj.highlight then
                                obj.highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
                                obj.highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
                            end
                        else
                            if obj.label then obj.label.TextColor3 = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player end
                            if obj.highlight then
                                obj.highlight.OutlineColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player
                                obj.highlight.FillColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.player
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for plr, obj in pairs(espPlayerObjects) do
                if not plr or not plr.Parent or not plr.Character or not plr.Character.Parent or plr.Character ~= obj.char then
                    if obj.highlight then pcall(function() obj.highlight:Destroy() end) end
                    if obj.billboard then pcall(function() obj.billboard:Destroy() end) end
                    espPlayerObjects[plr] = nil
                end
            end
        else
            if next(espPlayerObjects) then clearPlayerESP() end
        end
    end
end)

-- Update Loops Bots
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.5) do
        if espBotsEnabled then
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetAttribute("Team") == "Nextbot" then
                    if not espBotObjects[obj] then
                        createBotESP(obj)
                    end
                    if espBotObjects[obj] and espBotObjects[obj].root then
                        local data = espBotObjects[obj]
                        local myHrp = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if myHrp and data.label then
                            local dist = math.floor((data.root.Position - myHrp.Position).Magnitude)
                            data.label.Text = string.format("%s | %dm", obj.Name, dist)
                        elseif data.label then
                            data.label.Text = obj.Name
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for bot, obj in pairs(espBotObjects) do
                if not bot or not bot.Parent or not obj.root or not obj.root.Parent or not obj.billboard or not obj.billboard.Parent then
                    if obj.highlight then pcall(function() obj.highlight:Destroy() end) end
                    if obj.billboard then pcall(function() obj.billboard:Destroy() end) end
                    espBotObjects[bot] = nil
                end
            end
        else
            if next(espBotObjects) then clearBotESP() end
        end
    end
end)

-- ESP Event Items (FULL FIXED)
local function clearItemESP()
    for part, obj in pairs(espItemObjects) do
        if obj.highlight then pcall(function() obj.highlight:Destroy() end) end
        if obj.billboard then pcall(function() obj.billboard:Destroy() end) end
    end
    table.clear(espItemObjects)
end

local function createItemESP(itemPart, itemName)
    if not itemPart or not itemPart.Parent then return end
    if espItemObjects[itemPart] then return end

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "ESP_EventItem"
    highlight.FillColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.item
    highlight.OutlineColor = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.item
    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.55
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee = itemPart
    highlight.Parent = workspace

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "ESP_EventItem_Billboard"
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 20)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.MaxDistance = 1000
    billboard.Adornee = itemPart
    billboard.Parent = itemPart

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = itemName
    label.TextColor3 = _G.EvadeHub_ESPColor.item
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextScaled = true
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = billboard

    espItemObjects[itemPart] = {
        highlight = highlight,
        billboard = billboard,
        label = label,
        name = itemName
    }
end

-- Loop chính: Scan + Update tên + khoảng cách cho EventItems
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.25)

        if not espItemsEnabled then
            if next(espItemObjects) then
                clearItemESP()
            end
            continue
        end

        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local myChar = player.Character
        local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        local evtNames = GetValidEventItemNames()
        local spcNames = GetValidSpecialItemNames()

        local matchList = {}
        for _, name in ipairs(evtNames) do
            matchList[string.lower(name)] = name
        end
        for _, name in ipairs(spcNames) do
            matchList[string.lower(name)] = name
        end

        -- Dọn những item đã biến mất
        for part, obj in pairs(espItemObjects) do
            if not part or not part.Parent or not obj.billboard or not obj.billboard.Parent or not obj.highlight or not obj.highlight.Parent then
                if obj.highlight then pcall(function() obj.highlight:Destroy() end) end
                if obj.billboard then pcall(function() obj.billboard:Destroy() end) end
                espItemObjects[part] = nil
            end
        end

        -- Scan toàn bộ workspace
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) and not (myChar and obj:IsDescendantOf(myChar)) then
                local lowerName = string.lower(obj.Name)
                local matchedName = matchList[lowerName]

                -- Fuzzy match (chứa tên)
                if not matchedName then
                    for key, realName in pairs(matchList) do
                        if string.find(lowerName, key, 1, true) then
                            matchedName = realName
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end

                if matchedName then
                    local part = obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if part and part.Parent then
                        if not espItemObjects[part] then
                            createItemESP(part, matchedName)
                        end

                        -- Cập nhật tên + khoảng cách
                        local data = espItemObjects[part]
                        if data and data.label and data.label.Parent then
                            if myHrp then
                                local dist = math.floor((myHrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude)
                                data.label.Text = string.format("%s | %dm", matchedName, dist)
                            else
                                data.label.Text = matchedName
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Notify All
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if notifyAllEnabled then
            local myHrp = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            -- Check Players Down
            for _, plr in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hum and hrp and hum.Health <= 0 then
                        if not notifiedPlayers[plr] then
                            notifiedPlayers[plr] = true
                            local pos = hrp.Position
                            local dist = myHrp and math.floor((myHrp.Position - pos).Magnitude) or 0
                            WindUI:Notify({
                                Title = "💀 Người chơi bị hạ gục",
                                Content = string.format("%s\nCách bạn: %dm\nVị trí: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", plr.Name, dist, pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z),
                                Duration = 5
                            })
                        end
                    else
                        notifiedPlayers[plr] = nil
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Check Bots
            if myHrp then
                for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetAttribute("Team") == "Nextbot" then
                        local botRoot = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or obj:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or obj:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if botRoot then
                            local dist = (myHrp.Position - botRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist <= 45 then
                                if not notifiedBots[obj] then
                                    notifiedBots[obj] = true
                                    local pos = botRoot.Position
                                    WindUI:Notify({
                                        Title = "⚠️ Nextbot đang tới!",
                                        Content = string.format("%s\nCách bạn: %.1fm\nVị trí: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", obj.Name, dist, pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z),
                                        Duration = 5
                                    })
                                end
                            else
                                notifiedBots[obj] = nil
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            -- Check Event Items
            if myHrp then
                local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local pc = player.Character
                local evtNames, spcNames = GetValidEventItemNames(), GetValidSpecialItemNames()
                local exactEventNames, exactSpecialNames = {}, {}
                for _, n in ipairs(evtNames) do exactEventNames[string.lower(n)] = n end
                for _, n in ipairs(spcNames) do exactSpecialNames[string.lower(n)] = n end

                for _, v in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("Model")) and not (pc and v:IsDescendantOf(pc)) then
                        local lowerName = string.lower(v.Name)
                        local matchedName = nil
                        if exactEventNames[lowerName] then matchedName = exactEventNames[lowerName]
                        elseif exactSpecialNames[lowerName] then matchedName = exactSpecialNames[lowerName] end

                        if matchedName then
                            local part = v:IsA("BasePart") and v or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                            if part and part.Parent then
                                local dist = (myHrp.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist <= 50 then -- Bán kính 50 studs cho vật phẩm
                                    if not notifiedItems[part] then
                                        notifiedItems[part] = true
                                        local pos = part.Position
                                        WindUI:Notify({
                                            Title = "🎁 Vật phẩm sự kiện!",
                                            Content = string.format("%s\nCách bạn: %.1fm\nVị trí: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", matchedName, dist, pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z),
                                            Duration = 4
                                        })
                                    end
                                else
                                    notifiedItems[part] = nil
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            notifiedPlayers = {}
            notifiedBots = {}
            notifiedItems = {}
        end
    end
end)
