-- ============================================================
-- ===== SECTION JUMPS + INFINITE (dưới cùng tab Character) ===
-- ============================================================
;(function()
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local LP = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    _G.EvadeHub_Jump = _G.EvadeHub_Jump or {}
    local J = _G.EvadeHub_Jump
    -- dọn kết nối + vòng lặp của lần chạy trước (nếu script chạy lại)
    for _, c in pairs(J.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    J.conns = {}
    J.bhop, J.inf, J.holding = false, false, false
    J.gen = (J.gen or 0) + 1
    local myGen = J.gen

    -- Jump Holder có 2 cơ chế khác nhau (khác hẳn Infinite Jump = nhảy vô hạn, không giới hạn):
    --  * GIỮ nút nhảy  = tự động nhảy (bunny hop: chạm đất là nhảy lại)
    --  * BẤM 2 LẦN nút nhảy = nhảy 2 lần (lần bấm thứ 2 khi đang ở trên không tạo thêm 1 cú nhảy);
    --    hồi lại mỗi lần chạm đất, nên mỗi lần chạm đất chỉ được thêm 1 cú nhảy
    local DOUBLE_WINDOW = 0.5 -- 2 lần bấm cách nhau tối đa (giây)
    J.lastPress = 0
    J.usedDouble = false

    local function OnJumpPress()
        local now = os.clock()
        J.holding = true
        if J.bhop and (now - J.lastPress) <= DOUBLE_WINDOW and not J.usedDouble then
            local ch = LP.Character
            local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
                J.usedDouble = true
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) -- cú nhảy thứ 2 giữa không trung
                -- đặt luôn vận tốc nhảy lên để chắc chắn bật lên (một số game bỏ qua ChangeState khi đang rơi)
                local hrpJ = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrpJ then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait()
                        local vel = hrpJ.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                        local jv = hum.UseJumpPower and hum.JumpPower or math.sqrt(2 * workspace.Gravity * hum.JumpHeight)
                        hrpJ.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.max(jv, 50), vel.Z)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        J.lastPress = now
    end

    local function OnJumpRelease()
        J.holding = false
    end

    -- Hook input giữ nút nhảy (PC: Space / tay cầm: ButtonA)
    J.conns.began = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if gpe then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA then
            OnJumpPress()
        end
    end)
    -- InputEnded không kiểm tra gpe để cờ không bị kẹt true
    J.conns.ended = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA then
            OnJumpRelease()
        end
    end)

    -- Hook nút Jump trên mobile: PlayerGui.TouchGui.TouchControlFrame.JumpButton
    local function HookMobileJumpButton()
        local pg = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        if not pg then return end
        local touchGui = pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
        local frame = touchGui and touchGui:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
        local btn = frame and frame:FindFirstChild("JumpButton")
        if not btn then return end
        if J.conns.jbBegan then J.conns.jbBegan:Disconnect() end
        if J.conns.jbEnded then J.conns.jbEnded:Disconnect() end
        J.conns.jbBegan = btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                OnJumpPress()
            end
        end)
        J.conns.jbEnded = btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                OnJumpRelease()
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Jump Holder (bunny hop): vừa tiếp đất (Landed) + đang giữ nút + đang bật -> nhảy lại ngay
    local function HookBhop(character)
        if not character then return end
        local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if hum then
            if J.conns.state then J.conns.state:Disconnect(); J.conns.state = nil end -- tránh nhân đôi listener sau mỗi lần respawn
            J.conns.state = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed then
                    J.usedDouble = false -- chạm đất: hồi lại lượt nhảy thứ 2
                    if J.holding and J.bhop then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
                end
            end)
        end
        task.defer(HookMobileJumpButton)
    end
    if LP.Character then task.spawn(HookBhop, LP.Character) end
    J.conns.charAdded = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookBhop)

    -- Infinite Jump: ép nhảy 20 lần/giây khi đang giữ nút (kể cả giữa không trung)
    task.spawn(function()
        while J.gen == myGen do
            task.wait(0.05)
            if J.inf and J.holding and LP.Character then
                local hum = LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ===== Auto Jumps (Gui): bật toggle = tạo GUI toggle, tắt toggle = phá hủy GUI =====
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local AJ = { gui = nil, conns = {}, enabled = false }

    local function DestroyAutoJumpGui()
        AJ.enabled = false
        for _, c in pairs(AJ.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        AJ.conns = {}
        if AJ.gui then pcall(function() AJ.gui:Destroy() end); AJ.gui = nil end
    end
    -- nếu script chạy lại thì dọn GUI của lần trước
    if _G.EvadeHub_AutoJumpDestroy then pcall(_G.EvadeHub_AutoJumpDestroy) end
    _G.EvadeHub_AutoJumpDestroy = DestroyAutoJumpGui

    local function CreateAutoJumpGui()
        DestroyAutoJumpGui()
        local playerGui = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        if not playerGui then return end
        local oldGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoJumpGui")
        if oldGui then oldGui:Destroy() end

        local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        ScreenGui.Name = "AutoJumpGui"
        ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        ScreenGui.Parent = playerGui
        AJ.gui = ScreenGui

        local ToggleFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        ToggleFrame.Name = "AutoJumpToggle"
        ToggleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 0, 40)
        ToggleFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
        ToggleFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        ToggleFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ToggleFrame.Active = true
        ToggleFrame.Parent = ScreenGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", ToggleFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
        Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Label.Text = "Auto Jump"
        Label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        Label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        Label.TextSize = 14
        Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Label.Parent = ToggleFrame

        -- Nền công tắc (nhấp nháy đỏ/đen khi tắt, xanh/đen khi bật)
        local Switch = Instance.new("Frame")
        Switch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
        Switch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -54, 0.5, -11)
        Switch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        Switch.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Switch.Active = true
        Switch.Parent = ToggleFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", Switch).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local Knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        Knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
        Knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        Knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Knob.ZIndex = 2
        Knob.Parent = Switch
        Instance.new("UICorner", Knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        -- Nút bấm Toggle (chỉ nằm bên phải, không chặn kéo thả)
        local ClickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        ClickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 1, 0)
        ClickBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 0)
        ClickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ClickBtn.Text = ""
        ClickBtn.ZIndex = 3
        ClickBtn.Parent = ToggleFrame

        -- Kéo thả GUI
        local dragging = false
        local dragInput, dragStart, startPos
        ToggleFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = ToggleFrame.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        ToggleFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragInput = input
            end
        end)
        AJ.conns.drag = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if input == dragInput and dragging then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                ToggleFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end)

        -- Hiệu ứng nhấp nháy màu
        local flashTime = 0
        local originalColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        local blackColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        AJ.conns.flash = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            flashTime = flashTime + dt
            if math.floor(flashTime * 2.5) % 2 == 0 then
                Switch.BackgroundColor3 = originalColor
            else
                Switch.BackgroundColor3 = blackColor
            end
        end)

        -- Bhop lõi: vừa tiếp đất và đang bật thì nhảy lại
        local function HookBhop(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if hum then
                if AJ.conns.state then AJ.conns.state:Disconnect(); AJ.conns.state = nil end
                AJ.conns.state = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                    if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed and AJ.enabled then
                        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        if LP.Character then task.spawn(HookBhop, LP.Character) end
        AJ.conns.charAdded = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookBhop)

        -- Bật / tắt
        local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local function UpdateToggleVisual()
            local targetPos = AJ.enabled and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
            TweenService:Create(Knob, tweenInfo, {Position = targetPos}):Play()
            originalColor = AJ.enabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        end

        ClickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            AJ.enabled = not AJ.enabled
            UpdateToggleVisual()
            -- Bật lên và đang đứng dưới đất -> ép nhảy ngay
            if AJ.enabled then
                local char = LP.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if hum and (hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics) then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end
            end
        end)

        UpdateToggleVisual() -- trạng thái ban đầu: tắt
    end

    -- ===== Auto Jumps² (Gui): bật toggle = tạo GUI Auto Jumps² (có panel ⚙️ chỉnh tốc độ), tắt toggle = phá hủy GUI =====
    local AJ2 = { gui = nil, conns = {}, cleanup = nil }

    local function DestroyAutoJumps2Gui()
        if AJ2.cleanup then pcall(AJ2.cleanup); AJ2.cleanup = nil end -- hủy BodyVelocity + trả tốc độ gốc
        for _, c in pairs(AJ2.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        AJ2.conns = {}
        if AJ2.gui then pcall(function() AJ2.gui:Destroy() end); AJ2.gui = nil end
    end
    -- nếu script chạy lại thì dọn GUI của lần trước
    if _G.EvadeHub_AutoJumps2Destroy then pcall(_G.EvadeHub_AutoJumps2Destroy) end
    _G.EvadeHub_AutoJumps2Destroy = DestroyAutoJumps2Gui

    local function CreateAutoJumps2Gui()
        DestroyAutoJumps2Gui()
        local playerGui = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        if not playerGui then return end
        local oldGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("AutoJumpsGui")
        if oldGui then oldGui:Destroy() end

        --// State
        local State = {
            FrontJumpEnabled = false,
            PushEnabled = true,  -- Toggle "Lực đẩy character" trong panel ⚙️
            AutoJumpEnabled = true, -- Toggle "Tự động nhảy" trong panel ⚙️ (độc lập với lực đẩy)
        }
        local savedSpeed = 16
        local originalWalkSpeed = nil
        local currentBodyVelocity = nil
        local bodyVelocityRoot = nil
        local lastJumpTime = 0

        --// ==================== UI: TOGGLE ====================
        local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        ScreenGui.Name = "AutoJumpsGui"
        ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        ScreenGui.Parent = playerGui
        AJ2.gui = ScreenGui

        local ToggleFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        ToggleFrame.Name = "AutoJumpsToggle"
        ToggleFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 40)
        ToggleFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 20)
        ToggleFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        ToggleFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ToggleFrame.Active = true
        ToggleFrame.Parent = ScreenGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", ToggleFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local Label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        Label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 1, 0)
        Label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Label.Text = "Auto Jumps²"
        Label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        Label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        Label.TextSize = 13
        Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Label.Parent = ToggleFrame

        local Switch = Instance.new("Frame")
        Switch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
        Switch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -104, 0.5, -11)
        Switch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        Switch.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Switch.Active = true
        Switch.Parent = ToggleFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", Switch).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local Knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        Knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        Knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
        Knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        Knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Knob.ZIndex = 2
        Knob.Parent = Switch
        Instance.new("UICorner", Knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local ClickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        ClickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 1, 0)
        ClickBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -105, 0, 0)
        ClickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ClickBtn.Text = ""
        ClickBtn.ZIndex = 3
        ClickBtn.Parent = ToggleFrame

        local SettingsBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        SettingsBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
        SettingsBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -45, 0, 0)
        SettingsBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
        SettingsBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        SettingsBtn.Text = "⚙️"
        SettingsBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        SettingsBtn.TextSize = 20
        SettingsBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        SettingsBtn.ZIndex = 3
        SettingsBtn.Parent = ToggleFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", SettingsBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        --// ==================== PANEL ⚙️ ====================
        local SettingsPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
        SettingsPanel.Name = "SettingsPanel"
        SettingsPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 380)
        SettingsPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 20)
        SettingsPanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
        SettingsPanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        SettingsPanel.Active = true
        SettingsPanel.Visible = false
        SettingsPanel.Parent = ScreenGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", SettingsPanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

        local PanelStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        PanelStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
        PanelStroke.Thickness = 1
        PanelStroke.Parent = SettingsPanel

        local PanelTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        PanelTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 40)
        PanelTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 5)
        PanelTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        PanelTitle.Text = "⚙️ CHỈNH TỐC ĐỘ"
        PanelTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 50)
        PanelTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        PanelTitle.TextSize = 16
        PanelTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        PanelTitle.Parent = SettingsPanel

        local CloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        CloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28)
        CloseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 10)
        CloseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
        CloseBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        CloseBtn.Text = "✕"
        CloseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        CloseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        CloseBtn.TextSize = 14

        PushStatus.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham

        --// ==================== TOGGLE "TỰ ĐỘNG NHẢY" TRONG PANEL ====================
        local AutoJumpLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        AutoJumpLabel.Parent = SettingsPanel

        local AutoJumpSwitch = Instance.new("Frame")
        AutoJumpSwitch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
        AutoJumpSwitch.Position = UDim2.new(1, -59, 0, 289)
        AutoJumpSwitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        AutoJumpSwitch.BorderSizePixel = 0
        AutoJumpSwitch.Parent = SettingsPanel
        Instance.new("UICorner", AutoJumpSwitch).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local AutoJumpKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
        AutoJumpKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
        AutoJumpKnob.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9)
        AutoJumpKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        AutoJumpKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        AutoJumpKnob.ZIndex = 2
        AutoJumpKnob.Parent = AutoJumpSwitch
        Instance.new("UICorner", AutoJumpKnob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local AutoJumpClickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        AutoJumpClickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
        AutoJumpClickBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        AutoJumpClickBtn.Text = ""
        AutoJumpClickBtn.ZIndex = 3
        AutoJumpClickBtn.Parent = AutoJumpSwitch

        local AutoJumpStatus = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        AutoJumpStatus.Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 20)
        AutoJumpStatus.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 315)
        AutoJumpStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        AutoJumpStatus.Text = "Đang bật — tự động nhảy"
        AutoJumpStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        AutoJumpStatus.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        AutoJumpStatus.TextSize = 12
        AutoJumpStatus.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        AutoJumpStatus.Parent = SettingsPanel

        --// ==================== KÉO THẢ GUI ====================
        local function MakeDraggable(frame)
            local dragging = false
            local dragInput, dragStart, startPos
            frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = frame.Position
                    input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragInput = input
                end
            end)
            AJ2.conns[#AJ2.conns + 1] = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input == dragInput and dragging then
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                        startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                        startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
        end
        MakeDraggable(ToggleFrame)
        MakeDraggable(SettingsPanel)

        --// ==================== HELPER ====================
        local function GetHumanoid()
            local char = LP.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end
        local function GetRoot()
            local char = LP.Character
            return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end

        --// ==================== PANEL LOGIC ====================
        local function UpdateSettingsButtonColor()
            if SettingsPanel.Visible then
                SettingsBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 50, 200)
            else
                SettingsBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
            end
        end

        local function OpenPanel()
            local hum = GetHumanoid()
            if hum then
                InputBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(hum.WalkSpeed))
                StatusLabel.Text = "Tốc độ: " .. tostring(math.floor(hum.WalkSpeed))
            else
                StatusLabel.Text = "Tốc độ: " .. tostring(math.floor(savedSpeed))
            end
            StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            SettingsPanel.Visible = true
            UpdateSettingsButtonColor()
        end

        local function ClosePanel()
            SettingsPanel.Visible = false
            UpdateSettingsButtonColor()
        end

        SettingsBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if not SettingsPanel.Visible then
                TweenService:Create(SettingsBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60) }):Play()
            end
        end)
        SettingsBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(UpdateSettingsButtonColor)
        SettingsBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if SettingsPanel.Visible then ClosePanel() else OpenPanel() end
        end)
        CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ClosePanel)

        local function ApplySpeed()
            local val = tonumber(InputBox.Text)
            if not val then
                StatusLabel.Text = "⚠️ Số không hợp lệ"
                StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                return
            end
            val = math.clamp(val, 1, 500)
            savedSpeed = val
            InputBox.Text = tostring(math.floor(val))
            local hum = GetHumanoid()
            if hum then
                hum.WalkSpeed = val
                StatusLabel.Text = "✅ Tốc độ: " .. tostring(math.floor(val))
                StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            else
                StatusLabel.Text = "⚠️ Chưa có nhân vật"
                StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
            end
        end
        ApplyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ApplySpeed)
        InputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
            if enterPressed then ApplySpeed() end
        end)
        ApplyBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(ApplyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 150, 255) }):Play()
        end)
        ApplyBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(ApplyBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 130, 255) }):Play()
        end)

        --// ==================== VELOCITY (khai báo trước handler để hủy được BodyVelocity) ====================
        local FRONT_JUMP_SPEED = 50
        local JUMP_COOLDOWN = 0.15

        local function DestroyVelocity()
            if currentBodyVelocity then
                currentBodyVelocity:Destroy()
                currentBodyVelocity = nil
                bodyVelocityRoot = nil
            end
        end

        --// ==================== LOGIC TOGGLE "LỰC ĐẨY CHARACTER" ====================
        local pushTweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local function UpdatePushVisual()
            local targetPos = State.PushEnabled and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
            TweenService:Create(PushKnob, pushTweenInfo, { Position = targetPos }):Play()
            if State.PushEnabled then
                PushSwitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
                PushStatus.Text = "Đang bật — nhân vật luôn được đẩy về phía trước"
                PushStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            else
                PushSwitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                PushStatus.Text = "Đang tắt — không đẩy"
                PushStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
            end
        end
        PushClickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            State.PushEnabled = not State.PushEnabled
            UpdatePushVisual()
            if not State.PushEnabled then DestroyVelocity() end
        end)

        -- Tự động nhảy: chỉ bật/tắt việc tự nhảy, KHÔNG ảnh hưởng lực đẩy (lực đẩy vẫn chạy khi nhân vật nhảy/ở trên không)
        local function UpdateAutoJumpVisual()
            TweenService:Create(AutoJumpKnob, pushTweenInfo, { Position = State.AutoJumpEnabled and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9) }):Play()
            if State.AutoJumpEnabled then
                AutoJumpSwitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
                AutoJumpStatus.Text = "Đang bật — tự động nhảy"
                AutoJumpStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            else
                AutoJumpSwitch.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                AutoJumpStatus.Text = "Đang tắt — không tự nhảy"
                AutoJumpStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
            end
        end
        AutoJumpClickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            State.AutoJumpEnabled = not State.AutoJumpEnabled
            UpdateAutoJumpVisual()
        end)

        --// ==================== HIỆU ỨNG NHẤP NHÁY TOGGLE CHÍNH ====================
        local flashTime = 0
        local originalColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        local blackColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        AJ2.conns.flash = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            flashTime = flashTime + dt
            if math.floor(flashTime * 2.5) % 2 == 0 then
                Switch.BackgroundColor3 = originalColor
            else
                Switch.BackgroundColor3 = blackColor
            end
        end)

        --// ==================== FRONTJUMP LOGIC ====================
        local function GetMoveDirection(root)
            if not root then return Vector3.zero end
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local dir = Vector3.zero
            if cam then
                local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                dir = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
            end
            if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 then
                local charLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
                dir = Vector3.new(charLook.X, 0, charLook.Z)
            end
            if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 then dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
            return dir.Unit
        end

        local function IsGrounded(hum, root)
            if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then return true end
            if root then
                local params = RaycastParams.new()
                params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LP.Character }
                local result = workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -4, 0), params)
                if result then return true end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function IsAirState(state)
            return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying
        end

        local function IsBlockedState(state)
            return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing
                or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated
        end

        local function CreateVelocity(root)
            if currentBodyVelocity and bodyVelocityRoot == root and currentBodyVelocity.Parent then
                return currentBodyVelocity
            end
            if currentBodyVelocity then
                currentBodyVelocity:Destroy()
                currentBodyVelocity = nil
            end
            local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
            bv.P = 10000
            bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            bv.Parent = root
            currentBodyVelocity = bv
            bodyVelocityRoot = root
            return bv
        end

        local function PerformFrontJump(hum, root)
            local dir = GetMoveDirection(root)
            local currentVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            hum.Jump = true
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            -- Chỉ đẩy nếu PushEnabled = true
            if State.PushEnabled then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED, currentVel.Y, dir.Z * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED)
                local bv = CreateVelocity(root)
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED, 0, dir.Z * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED)
            end
            lastJumpTime = tick()
        end

        local function TryFrontJump()
            if not State.FrontJumpEnabled then return end
            local hum = GetHumanoid()
            local root = GetRoot()
            if not hum or not root then return end
            if hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if IsBlockedState(state) then return end
            -- Đang bay -> chỉ đẩy nếu PushEnabled = true
            if IsAirState(state) then
                if State.PushEnabled then
                    local dir = GetMoveDirection(root)
                    local bv = CreateVelocity(root)
                    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED, 0, dir.Z * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED)
                end
                return
            end
            if not State.AutoJumpEnabled then
                -- tắt Tự động nhảy: không tự nhảy, nhưng lực đẩy vẫn chạy bình thường (kể cả khi đứng trên đất)
                if State.PushEnabled then
                    local dir = GetMoveDirection(root)
                    local bv = CreateVelocity(root)
                    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED, 0, dir.Z * FRONT_JUMP_SPEED)
                end
                return
            end
            if not IsGrounded(hum, root) then return end
            if tick() - lastJumpTime < JUMP_COOLDOWN then return end
            PerformFrontJump(hum, root)
        end

        local function HookStateChanged(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not hum then return end
            if AJ2.conns.state then AJ2.conns.state:Disconnect(); AJ2.conns.state = nil end
            AJ2.conns.state = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if not State.FrontJumpEnabled then return end
                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
                    or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then
                    lastJumpTime = 0
                end
            end)
        end

        AJ2.conns.heartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if State.FrontJumpEnabled then TryFrontJump() end
        end)

        --// ==================== XỬ LÝ BẬT / TẮT TOGGLE CHÍNH ====================
        local tweenInfo2 = TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local function UpdateToggleVisual()
            local targetPos = State.FrontJumpEnabled and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
            TweenService:Create(Knob, tweenInfo2, { Position = targetPos }):Play()
            originalColor = State.FrontJumpEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        end

        ClickBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            State.FrontJumpEnabled = not State.FrontJumpEnabled
            UpdateToggleVisual()
            if State.FrontJumpEnabled then
                lastJumpTime = 0
            else
                DestroyVelocity()
            end
        end)

        --// ==================== CHARACTER RESPAWN ====================
        local function OnCharacterAdded(character)
            HookStateChanged(character)
            task.delay(1, function()
                local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = savedSpeed end
            end)
        end

        if LP.Character then
            HookStateChanged(LP.Character)
            local hum = GetHumanoid()
            if hum then savedSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed; originalWalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed end
        end
        AJ2.conns.charAdded = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)

        -- Khi tắt toggle trong UI: hủy BodyVelocity và trả tốc độ gốc
        AJ2.cleanup = function()
            DestroyVelocity()
            State.FrontJumpEnabled = false
            local hum = GetHumanoid()
            if hum and originalWalkSpeed then hum.WalkSpeed = originalWalkSpeed end
        end

        -- Khởi tạo
        UpdateToggleVisual()
        UpdateSettingsButtonColor()
    end

    local jumpSection = _G.EvadeHub_CFrameTab:Section({ Title = "Jumps + Infinite", Opened = true })
    jumpSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Infinite Jump",
        Desc = "Giữ nút nhảy là nhảy liên tục, kể cả giữa không trung",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "InfiniteJump",
        Callback = function(v) J.inf = v end,
    })
    jumpSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Jump Holder",
        Desc = "Giữ nút nhảy = nhảy tự động (bunny hop). Bấm 2 lần nút nhảy = nhảy 2 lần, hồi lại mỗi lần chạm đất",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "JumpHolder",
        Callback = function(v) J.bhop = v end,
    })
    jumpSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Jumps (Gui)",
        Desc = "Bật = tạo GUI công tắc Auto Jump (kéo thả được), tắt = xóa GUI",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "AutoJumpsGui",
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                task.spawn(CreateAutoJumpGui)
            else
                DestroyAutoJumpGui()
            end
        end,
    })
    jumpSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Auto Jumps² (Gui)",
        Desc = "Bật = tạo GUI Auto Jumps² (kèm panel ⚙️ chỉnh tốc độ + lực đẩy), tắt = xóa GUI",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "AutoJumps2Gui",
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                task.spawn(CreateAutoJumps2Gui)
            else
                DestroyAutoJumps2Gui()
            end
        end,
    })
end)()

-- ============================================================
-- ===== CÁC HÀM XỬ LÝ FILE ===================================
-- ============================================================
local function EnsureFolder()
    if type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" then
        if not isfolder(FOLDER_NAME) then pcall(function() makefolder(FOLDER_NAME) end) end
    end
end

local function SaveClickLocation(x, y)
    EnsureFolder()
    pcall(function()
        writefile(LOCATION_FILE, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({ clickX = x, clickY = y }))
    end)
end

local function LoadClickLocation()
    if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(LOCATION_FILE) then
        local success, content = pcall(readfile, LOCATION_FILE)
        if success and content then
            local okJ, data = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(content) end)
            if okJ and type(data) == "table" and tonumber(data.clickX) and tonumber(data.clickY) then
                return tonumber(data.clickX), tonumber(data.clickY)
            end
        end
    end
    return nil, nil
end

local function DeleteClickLocation()
    if type(delfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(LOCATION_FILE) then
        pcall(function() delfile(LOCATION_FILE) end)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function SaveExecuteFile()
    EnsureFolder()
    pcall(function() writefile(EXECUTE_FILE, AUTO_EXECUTE_CMD) end)
end

-- ===== AUTO EXECUTE: ghi code raw vào AutoExecute.txt + queue_on_teleport + thư mục autoexec của client =====
_G.EvadeHub_ApplyAutoExecute = function(state)
    local AUTOEXEC_DIRS = { "autoexec", "Autoexecute", "autoexecute", "AutoExec", "../autoexec", "../Autoexecute", "../autoexecute" }
    EnsureFolder()
    if state then
        SaveExecuteFile() -- AutoExecute.txt chứa đoạn code raw khởi động script
        if type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" then
            pcall(function() queue_on_teleport(AUTO_EXECUTE_CMD) end) -- chạy lại sau khi chuyển server/rejoin
        end
        -- thư mục autoexec của client (chạy cả khi mở lại game): chỉ ghi nếu thư mục đó có sẵn
        for _, dir in ipairs(AUTOEXEC_DIRS) do
            if type(isfolder) == "function" then
                local okF, exists = pcall(isfolder, dir)
                if okF and exists then
                    pcall(function() writefile(dir .. "/Aur_Meow_AutoExecute.lua", AUTO_EXECUTE_CMD) end)
                end
            end
        end
    else
        pcall(function() if type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" then queue_on_teleport("") end end)
        pcall(function() if type(delfile) == "function" and isfile(EXECUTE_FILE) then delfile(EXECUTE_FILE) end end)
        for _, dir in ipairs(AUTOEXEC_DIRS) do
            pcall(function()
                local f = dir .. "/Aur_Meow_AutoExecute.lua"
                if type(delfile) == "function" and isfile(f) then delfile(f) end
            end)
        end
    end
end
_G.EvadeHub_ApplyAutoExecute(true) -- mặc định bật: tạo AutoExecute.txt ngay khi script chạy (cấu hình cũ sẽ chỉnh lại sau)

-- ===== LOGIC CHECK LOBBY ====================================
-- ============================================================
local TARGET_XYZ = Vector3.new(122.50, 36.99, -170.65)
local LOBBY_DISTANCE = 300
local currentIsInLobby = false

local function UpdateLobbyStatus()
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local char = player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then 
        currentIsInLobby = true 
        return 
    end

    local dist = (hrp.Position - TARGET_XYZ).Magnitude
    currentIsInLobby = (dist <= LOBBY_DISTANCE)
end

task.spawn(function() while task.wait(0.2) do UpdateLobbyStatus() end end)

-- ============================================================
-- ===== ANTI-AFK SYSTEM + AUTO REJOIN 18p45s =================
-- ============================================================
local antiAfkEnabled = false
local lastUserActionTime = tick()
local antiAfkDelayThreshold = 30
local antiAfkLoopRunning = false
local isSimulatingInput = false

local antiAfkRejoinEnabled = false
local antiAfkRejoinTimer = 0

local function GetActualAfkTime() return tick() - lastUserActionTime end

local function SimulateKeyPress(keyCode)
    pcall(function()
        isSimulatingInput = true
        local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode, false, game)
        task.wait(0.1)
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game)
        task.wait(0.2)
        isSimulatingInput = false
    end)
end

local function DoAntiAfkAction()
    -- Chỉ còn thao tác bàn phím mở/đóng bảng xếp hạng (Tab) trong Evade
    SimulateKeyPress(Enum.KeyCode.Tab); task.wait(0.5); SimulateKeyPress(Enum.KeyCode.Tab)
end

local function StartAntiAfkLoop()
    if antiAfkLoopRunning or not antiAfkEnabled then return end
    antiAfkLoopRunning = true
    WindUI:Notify({ Title = "📊 Anti AFK", Content = "✔️ Đã kích hoạt", Duration = 5 })
    task.spawn(function()
        while antiAfkEnabled and GetActualAfkTime() >= antiAfkDelayThreshold do
            task.wait(math.random(1, 10))
            if not antiAfkEnabled or GetActualAfkTime() < antiAfkDelayThreshold then break end
            DoAntiAfkAction()
        end
        antiAfkLoopRunning = false
    end)
end

-- (Đã bỏ bộ đếm độc lập của Auto Rejoin: giờ Auto Rejoin chỉ chạy theo "Counter AFK" của farm,
--  xem khối "AUTO REJOIN THEO COUNTER AFK" trong vòng lặp REALTIME UPDATE TEXT bên dưới)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        if antiAfkEnabled and GetActualAfkTime() >= antiAfkDelayThreshold and not antiAfkLoopRunning then StartAntiAfkLoop() end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- ===== ĐẾM FPS / CPU / PING / GPU ===========================
-- ============================================================
local currentFps, currentCpu, currentPing, currentGpu = 60, "N/A", "N/A", "N/A"

task.spawn(function()
    local frameCount, lastTime = 0, tick()
    game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        frameCount = frameCount + 1
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastTime >= 1 then
            currentFps = math.floor(frameCount / (now - lastTime) + 0.5)
            frameCount, lastTime = 0, now
            currentCpu = math.clamp(math.floor((1 - (currentFps / 60)) * 100), 0, 100) .. "%"
            currentGpu = math.clamp(math.floor((1 - (currentFps / 60)) * 80), 0, 100) .. "%"
        end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
        pcall(function()
            local ping = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            currentPing = math.floor(ping) .. "ms"
        end)
    end
end)

    "Souls", "Wisp", "Spellbook", "none",
    "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none",
    "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none", "none"

local uiFarmEventText, uiFarmLvText, uiFarmTokenText
local itemCollectedCounts = {}

local function Notify(title, content, duration)
    pcall(function() WindUI:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = duration or 3 }) end)
end

FormatTime = function(seconds)
    return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(seconds / 3600), math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60), math.floor(seconds % 60))
end

local function SetFarmEventStatus(text) farmEventStatusText = text end
