-- ============================================================
-- ===== HÀM XỬ LÝ ITEM =======================================
-- ============================================================
local function GetValidItemNames(list)
    local names = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(list) do
        if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name ~= "none" and name ~= "nil" then table.insert(names, name) end
    end
    return names
end

GetValidEventItemNames = function() return GetValidItemNames(EventItems) end
GetValidSpecialItemNames = function() return GetValidItemNames(SpecialEvent) end

local function HasAnyValidEventItem() return #GetValidEventItemNames() > 0 end
local function HasAnyValidSpecialItem() return #GetValidSpecialItemNames() > 0 end

local function FormatTargetItemsDisplay()
    local evtNames = GetValidEventItemNames()
    local spcNames = GetValidSpecialItemNames()
    local allNames = {}
    for _, v in ipairs(evtNames) do table.insert(allNames, v) end
    for _, v in ipairs(spcNames) do table.insert(allNames, v) end
    if #allNames == 0 then return "—" end
    return table.concat(allNames, ", ")
end

local function InitItemCollectedCounts()
    itemCollectedCounts = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(EventItems) do
        if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name ~= "none" and name ~= "nil" then
            itemCollectedCounts[name] = 0
        end
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(SpecialEvent) do
        if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name ~= "none" and name ~= "nil" then
            itemCollectedCounts[name] = 0
        end
    end
end
InitItemCollectedCounts()

local function IncrementItemCount(itemName)
    if itemCollectedCounts[itemName] then itemCollectedCounts[itemName] = itemCollectedCounts[itemName] + 1 end
end

local function FormatScanEventItemsDisplay()
    local lines, lineNum = {}, 1
    for _, name in ipairs(EventItems) do
        if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name ~= "none" and name ~= "nil" then
            table.insert(lines, string.format("%d. %s | EventItems [%s]", lineNum, name, string.format("%02d", itemCollectedCounts[name] or 0)))
            lineNum = lineNum + 1
        end
    end
    for _, name in ipairs(SpecialEvent) do
        if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" and name ~= "none" and name ~= "nil" then
            table.insert(lines, string.format("%d. %s | SpecialEvent [%s]", lineNum, name, string.format("%02d", itemCollectedCounts[name] or 0)))
            lineNum = lineNum + 1
        end
    end
    return #lines == 0 and "❌ Không có item nào" or table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

local function getAllItems()
    local items, player = {}, game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player then return items end
    local pc = player.Character
    local evtNames, spcNames = GetValidEventItemNames(), GetValidSpecialItemNames()
    if #evtNames == 0 and #spcNames == 0 then return items end

    local exactEventNames = {}
    local exactSpecialNames = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(evtNames) do exactEventNames[string.lower(n)] = n end
    for _, n in ipairs(spcNames) do exactSpecialNames[string.lower(n)] = n end

    local desc = workspace:GetDescendants()
    for i = 1, #desc do
        local v = desc[i]
        if (v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("Model")) and not (pc and v:IsDescendantOf(pc)) then
            local rawName = v.Name
            local lowerName = string.lower(rawName)

            local matchedName, isSpecial = nil, false
            if exactEventNames[lowerName] then
                matchedName = exactEventNames[lowerName]
                isSpecial = false
            elseif exactSpecialNames[lowerName] then
                matchedName = exactSpecialNames[lowerName]
                isSpecial = true
            end

            if not matchedName then
                local baseName = string.match(lowerName, "^(%a+)%s*%(?%d*%)?$")
                if baseName then
                    if exactEventNames[baseName] then
                        matchedName = exactEventNames[baseName]
                        isSpecial = false
                    elseif exactSpecialNames[baseName] then
                        matchedName = exactSpecialNames[baseName]
                        isSpecial = true
                    end
                end
            end

            if matchedName then
                local part = v:IsA("BasePart") and v or v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if part and part.Parent then
                    table.insert(items, {
                        Object = part,
                        Name = matchedName,
                        RawName = rawName,
                        Position = part.Position,
                        IsSpecial = isSpecial
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return items
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== ESP (TOKEN & EVENT ITEM) =============================
-- ============================================================
local function ClearTargetESP()
    if targetEspConn then pcall(function() targetEspConn:Disconnect() end) targetEspConn = nil end
    if targetEspHighlight then pcall(function() targetEspHighlight:Destroy() end) targetEspHighlight = nil end
    if targetEspGui then pcall(function() targetEspGui:Destroy() end) targetEspGui = nil end
    targetEspLabel, targetEspPlayer, targetEspMode = nil, nil, "idle"
end

local function CreateTargetESP(targetPlayer)
    ClearTargetESP()
    if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then return end
    local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character
    local head = targetChar:FindFirstChild("Head") or targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not head then return end
    targetEspPlayer, targetEspMode = targetPlayer, "blink"

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "EvadeHubESP_Token"
    highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.6, 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee, highlight.Parent = targetChar, workspace
    targetEspHighlight = highlight

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "EvadeHubName"
    billboard.Size, billboard.StudsOffset = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 40), Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop, billboard.LightInfluence, billboard.MaxDistance = true, 0, 1000
    billboard.Adornee, billboard.Parent = head, head
    targetEspGui = billboard

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size, label.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), 1
    label.Text, label.TextColor3 = targetPlayer.Name, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency, label.TextStrokeColor3 = 0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextScaled, label.Font = true, Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = billboard
    targetEspLabel = label

    targetEspConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not targetEspHighlight or not targetEspHighlight.Parent then return end
        if targetEspMode == "success" then
            targetEspHighlight.FillColor, targetEspHighlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 0), Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
            targetEspHighlight.FillTransparency = 0.4
            if targetEspLabel and targetEspLabel.Parent then
                targetEspLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
                targetEspLabel.Text = "✅ ĐÃ CỨU - " .. (targetEspPlayer and targetEspPlayer.Name or "")
            end
            return
        end
        local t = (math.sin(tick() * 8) + 1) / 2
        targetEspHighlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0), t)
        targetEspHighlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), t)
        if targetEspLabel and targetEspLabel.Parent then targetEspLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), t) end
        if targetEspPlayer and targetEspPlayer.Character then
            local newChar = targetEspPlayer.Character
            if targetEspHighlight and targetEspHighlight.Adornee ~= newChar then targetEspHighlight.Adornee = newChar end
            local newHead = newChar:FindFirstChild("Head") or newChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if newHead and targetEspGui and targetEspGui.Parent ~= newHead then targetEspGui.Adornee, targetEspGui.Parent = newHead, newHead end
        end
    end)
end

local function MarkTargetESPSuccess()
    if targetEspMode ~= "blink" then return end
    targetEspMode = "success"
    task.spawn(function() task.wait(1.2); ClearTargetESP() end)
end

local function ClearEventItemESP()
    if eventItemEspConn then pcall(function() eventItemEspConn:Disconnect() end) eventItemEspConn = nil end
    if eventItemEspHighlight then pcall(function() eventItemEspHighlight:Destroy() end) eventItemEspHighlight = nil end
    if eventItemEspGui then pcall(function() eventItemEspGui:Destroy() end) eventItemEspGui = nil end
    eventItemEspLabel, eventItemEspTarget = nil, nil
end

local function CreateEventItemESP(itemPart, itemName)
    ClearEventItemESP()
    if not itemPart or not itemPart.Parent then return end
    eventItemEspTarget = itemPart

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "EvadeHubESP_EventItem"
    highlight.FillColor, highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0), Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)
    highlight.FillTransparency, highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.5, 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee, highlight.Parent = itemPart, workspace
    eventItemEspHighlight = highlight

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "EvadeHubItemName"
    billboard.Size, billboard.StudsOffset = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 40), Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop, billboard.LightInfluence, billboard.MaxDistance = true, 0, 1000
    billboard.Adornee, billboard.Parent = itemPart, itemPart
    eventItemEspGui = billboard

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size, label.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), 1
    label.Text, label.TextColor3 = itemName, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency, label.TextStrokeColor3 = 0, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextScaled, label.Font = true, Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.Parent = billboard
    eventItemEspLabel = label

    local hue = 0
    eventItemEspConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        hue = (hue + dt * 0.5) % 1
        local c1, c2 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 1, 1), Color3.fromHSV((hue + 0.5) % 1, 1, 1)
        if eventItemEspHighlight and eventItemEspHighlight.Parent then eventItemEspHighlight.FillColor, eventItemEspHighlight.OutlineColor = c1, c2 end
        if eventItemEspLabel and eventItemEspLabel.Parent then eventItemEspLabel.TextColor3 = c1 end
        if eventItemEspTarget and eventItemEspTarget.Parent and eventItemEspHighlight.Adornee ~= eventItemEspTarget then eventItemEspHighlight.Adornee = eventItemEspTarget end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== SAVED POSITION & XUNG ĐỘT ============================
-- ============================================================
local function SaveCurrentPosition(mode)
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not player.Character then return end
    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    if mode == "event" and not savedPosEventSet then savedPosEvent, savedPosEventSet = hrp.Position, true
    elseif mode == "lv" and not savedPosLvSet then savedPosLv, savedPosLvSet = hrp.Position, true
    elseif mode == "token" and not savedPosTokenSet then savedPosToken, savedPosTokenSet = hrp.Position, true end
end

local function TeleportToSavedPosition(mode)
    local savedPos = (mode == "event" and savedPosEvent) or (mode == "lv" and savedPosLv) or (mode == "token" and savedPosToken)
    if not savedPos then return end
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if player and player.Character then
        local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(savedPos) end
    end
end

local function IsAtSavedPosition(mode)
    local savedPos = (mode == "event" and savedPosEvent) or (mode == "lv" and savedPosLv) or (mode == "token" and savedPosToken)
    if not savedPos then return true end
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end
    return (hrp.Position - savedPos).Magnitude <= POSITION_TOLERANCE
end

local function ClearSavedPosition(mode)
    if mode == "event" then savedPosEvent, savedPosEventSet = nil, false
    elseif mode == "lv" then savedPosLv, savedPosLvSet = nil, false
    elseif mode == "token" then savedPosToken, savedPosTokenSet = nil, false end
end

local function CountActiveFarms() return (isFarming and 1 or 0) + (isFarmingLv and 1 or 0) + (isRevivingActive and 1 or 0) end
local function UpdateFarmConflict()
    if CountActiveFarms() >= 2 then
        isFarmBlocked = true
        if not conflictNotified then conflictNotified = true; Notify("❗ Xung đột hệ thống", "⚠️ Xin vui lòng, loại bỏ 1 toggle farm", 5) end
    else
        if not isCharacterDead then isFarmBlocked = false end
        conflictNotified = false
    end
end
local function IsModeBlocked() return isFarmBlocked or isCharacterDead end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== CHARACTER DEAD HANDLER ===============================
-- ============================================================
local function GetActiveMode()
    if isFarming then return "event" elseif isFarmingLv then return "lv" elseif isRevivingActive then return "token" else return nil end
end

local function OnCharacterDeadOrDowned()
    if isCharacterDead then return end
    isCharacterDead, isFarmBlocked = true, true
    if isFarming then SetFarmEventStatus("🕑 Character Dead/Downed") end
    if isFarmingLv then SetFarmLvStatus("🕑 Character Dead/Downed") end
    if isRevivingActive then SetFarmTokenStatus("🕑 Character Dead/Downed") end

    local mode = GetActiveMode()
    task.spawn(function()
        local pendingRevive = true
        while pendingRevive do
            local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
            if player and player.Character then
                local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    -- HỒI SINH: giữ chặn farm (isFarmBlocked) cho tới khi đã teleport lên tường VÀ xác nhận đúng vị trí
                    isCharacterDead = false
                    task.wait(0.1)

                    -- Teleport lên tường rồi so sánh XYZ với (500, 500, 500); chưa tới thì teleport lại, đủ rồi mới farm tiếp
                    local function GoToWallVerified(tpFn)
                        if _G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall then return true end
                        for _ = 1, 20 do
                            pcall(tpFn)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            local ch = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                            local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if root then
                                local p = root.Position
                                -- X, Y, Z đều đạt từ 500 trở lên (X, Z cho sai số 2 stud) thì chấp nhận
                                if p.X >= WALL_POSITION.X - 2 and p.Y >= WALL_POSITION.Y and p.Z >= WALL_POSITION.Z - 2 then
                                    return true
                                end
                            end
                            local h2 = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if h2 and h2.Health <= 0 then return false end -- lại bị hạ giữa chừng
                        end
                        return false
                    end

                    if isFarming then
                        if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then SetFarmEventStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); if CreateWall then CreateWall() end; task.wait(0.2) end
                        SetFarmEventStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe")
                        local ok = GoToWallVerified(function() if TeleportToWall then TeleportToWall() end end)
                        SetFarmEventStatus(ok and "✅ Đã lên tường - farm tiếp" or "⚠️ Chưa lên được tường")
                    end
                    if isFarmingLv then
                        if not wallModelLv or not wallModelLv.Parent then SetFarmLvStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); if CreateWallLv then CreateWallLv() end; task.wait(0.2) end
                        SetFarmLvStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe")
                        local ok = GoToWallVerified(function() if TeleportToWallLv then TeleportToWallLv() end end)
                        SetFarmLvStatus(ok and "✅ Đã lên tường - farm tiếp" or "⚠️ Chưa lên được tường")
                    end
                    if isRevivingActive then
                        if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then SetFarmTokenStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); if CreateWall then CreateWall() end; task.wait(0.2) end
                        SetFarmTokenStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe")
                        local ok = GoToWallVerified(function() if TeleportToWall then TeleportToWall() end end)
                        SetFarmTokenStatus(ok and "✅ Đã lên tường - farm tiếp" or "⚠️ Chưa lên được tường")
                    end

                    -- Xong: mở chặn farm (UpdateFarmConflict vẫn giữ chặn nếu đang bật >= 2 farm cùng lúc)
                    UpdateFarmConflict()
                    pendingRevive = false
                    break
                else
                    if mode and not IsAtSavedPosition(mode) then TeleportToSavedPosition(mode) end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
            else task.wait(0.2) end
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local wasDead = false
    while task.wait(0.3) do
        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player or not player.Character then
            if not wasDead then wasDead = true; OnCharacterDeadOrDowned() end
        else
            local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local isDead = hum.Health <= 0
                if isDead and not wasDead then wasDead = true; OnCharacterDeadOrDowned()
                elseif not isDead and wasDead then wasDead = false; isCharacterDead = false end
            end
        end
    end
end)

task.spawn(function() while task.wait(0.1) do playerCountNow = #game.Players:GetPlayers() end end)

-- ============================================================
-- ===== USER INPUT & SPAM E ==================================
-- ============================================================
_G.FarmLvUserActive, _G.FarmTokenUserActive, _G.FarmEventUserActive = false, false, false
local function MarkUserActive()
    if isSimulatingInput then return end
    _G.FarmLvUserActive, _G.FarmTokenUserActive, _G.FarmEventUserActive = true, true, true
    lastUserActionTime = tick()
    if antiAfkLoopRunning then
        antiAfkLoopRunning = false
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "📊 Anti AFK", Content = "❌ Đã tắt anti-afk", Duration = 3 })
    end
end

do
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i, g) if g or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then return end MarkUserActive() end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(_, g) if g then return end MarkUserActive() end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(_, g) if g then return end MarkUserActive() end)
    UIS.TouchStarted:Connect(MarkUserActive); UIS.TouchMoved:Connect(MarkUserActive); UIS.TouchEnded:Connect(MarkUserActive)
    UIS.WindowFocused:Connect(MarkUserActive); UIS.TextBoxFocused:Connect(MarkUserActive)
    UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then MarkUserActive() end end)
end

local function PressKeyEOnce()
    pcall(function()
        local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
    end)
end

local function StartSpamE()
    if spamEActive then return end
    spamEActive = true
    if spamEConn then pcall(function() spamEConn:Disconnect() end) end
    spamEConn = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function() if spamEActive then PressKeyEOnce() end end)
end

local function StopSpamE()
    spamEActive = false
    if spamEConn then pcall(function() spamEConn:Disconnect() end) spamEConn = nil end
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== WALL & TELEPORT FUNCTIONS ============================
-- ============================================================
CreateWall = function()
    if _G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall then return nil end
    pcall(function() if wallModel then wallModel:Destroy() wallModel = nil end end)
    wallModel = Instance.new("Model"); wallModel.Name = "FarmWall"
    local wall = Instance.new("Part")
    wall.Size, wall.Position = Vector3.new(200, 1, 200), WALL_POSITION
    wall.Anchored, wall.CanCollide, wall.Transparency, wall.BrickColor, wall.Material = true, true, 0, BrickColor.new("White"), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    wall.Parent, wallModel.Parent = wallModel, workspace
    return wallModel
end

CreateWallLv = function()
    if _G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall then return nil end
    pcall(function() if wallModelLv then wallModelLv:Destroy() wallModelLv = nil end end)
    wallModelLv = Instance.new("Model"); wallModelLv.Name = "FarmWallLv"
    local wall = Instance.new("Part")
    wall.Size, wall.Position = Vector3.new(200, 1, 200), WALL_POSITION
    wall.Anchored, wall.CanCollide, wall.Transparency, wall.BrickColor, wall.Material = true, true, 0, BrickColor.new("White"), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    wall.Parent, wallModelLv.Parent = wallModelLv, workspace
    return wallModelLv
end

TeleportWithEmote = function(position, cframe, isWall, isLv)
    if not isWall then isCollecting = true end
    if isLv then if isTeleportingLv then return end; isTeleportingLv = true
    else if isTeleporting then return end; isTeleporting = true end
    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not player.Character then
        if isLv then isTeleportingLv = false else isTeleporting = false end
        isCollecting = false; return
    end
    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then
        if isLv then isTeleportingLv = false else isTeleporting = false end
        isCollecting = false; return
    end
    pcall(function() if cframe then hrp.CFrame = cframe else hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(position) end end)
    task.wait(0.05)
    if isLv then isTeleportingLv = false else isTeleporting = false end
    if isWall then isCollecting = false end
end

TeleportToWall = function()
    if not wallModel then return end
    TeleportWithEmote(Vector3.new(wallModel:GetPivot().Position.X, wallModel:GetPivot().Position.Y + 1, wallModel:GetPivot().Position.Z), nil, true, false)
    task.wait(TELEPORT_TO_WALL_DELAY)
end

TeleportToWallLv = function()
    if not wallModelLv then return end
    TeleportWithEmote(Vector3.new(wallModelLv:GetPivot().Position.X, wallModelLv:GetPivot().Position.Y + 1, wallModelLv:GetPivot().Position.Z), nil, true, true)
    task.wait(TELEPORT_TO_WALL_DELAY)
end

TeleportToWallSafe = function()
    if isTeleportingToSafe then return end
    isTeleportingToSafe = true
    if not wallModel then CreateWall() task.wait(0.5) end
    TeleportToWall(); isTeleportingToSafe = false
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== DOWNED PLAYER & RESCUE ===============================
-- ============================================================
local function FindDownedPlayer()
    local Players, lp = game:GetService("Players"), game.Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp then return nil end
    local closest, closestDist = nil, math.huge
    local myPos = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= lp then
            local char = player.Character
            if char then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and (hum.Health <= 0 or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) then
                    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if root then
                        if myPos then
                            local d = (myPos - root.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < closestDist then closestDist = d; closest = player end
                        else if not closest then closest = player end end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

local function RescueModeWait(target, rootPart)
    if not target or not target.Character or not rootPart then return false end
    local lp2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    TeleportWithEmote(rootPart.Position, nil, false, false)
    local isRevived = false
    while not isRevived and isRevivingActive do
        if isCharacterDead or not target or not target.Character then break end
        local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health > 0 then isRevived = true; break end
        local curRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not curRoot then break end
        local lpRoot = lp2.Character and lp2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if lpRoot and (lpRoot.Position - curRoot.Position).Magnitude > 5 then TeleportWithEmote(curRoot.Position + (lpRoot.Position - curRoot.Position).Unit * 3, nil, false, false) end
        if isRevivingActive then PressKeyEOnce() end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return isRevived
end

local function RescueModeFast(target)
    if not target or not target.Character then return false end
    local isRevived, loopCount, MAX_LOOPS = false, 0, 50
    while not isRevived and isRevivingActive and loopCount < MAX_LOOPS do
        if isCharacterDead or not target or not target.Character then break end
        loopCount = loopCount + 1
        local hum = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum or hum.Health > 0 then isRevived = true; break end
        local curRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not curRoot then break end
        StartSpamE(); TeleportWithEmote(curRoot.Position, nil, false, false); task.wait(0.2); StopSpamE()
        if not wallModel then CreateWall() task.wait(0.3) end
        SetFarmTokenStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWallSafe(); task.wait(0.15)
        if not target or not target.Character then break end
        local hum2 = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum2 or hum2.Health > 0 then isRevived = true; break end
    end
    StopSpamE(); return isRevived
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== REVIVE LOOP & FARM LOOPS =============================
-- ============================================================
local function RevivePlayersLoop()
    if reviveLoopRunning then return end
    reviveLoopRunning = true

    SetFarmTokenStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); CreateWall(); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmTokenStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWall(); task.wait(1)
    isOnWallAndWaiting = true

    while isRevivingActive do
        if isCharacterDead then task.wait(0.5)
        elseif isOnWallAndWaiting then
            if IsModeBlocked() then task.wait(1)
            else
                SetFarmTokenStatus("🔎 Downed player - Đang quét")
                local target = FindDownedPlayer()
                if target then
                    isOnWallAndWaiting = false
                    local rootPart = target.Character and (target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or target.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart"))
                    if rootPart then
                        CreateTargetESP(target)
                        tokenCurrentTargetName = "@" .. target.Name
                        tokenCurrentTargetPos = rootPart.Position
                        if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            tokenCurrentTargetDist = (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude
                        end
                        reviveTarget = target
                        SetFarmTokenStatus("👤 Revive player - @" .. target.Name)

                        local isRevived = (farmMode == "Cứu nhanh") and RescueModeFast(target) or RescueModeWait(target, rootPart)

                        if isRevived then
                            reviveSuccessCount = reviveSuccessCount + 1
                            tokenStatSuccess = tokenStatSuccess + 1
                            SetFarmTokenStatus("✅ Revived successfully - " .. target.Name)
                            MarkTargetESPSuccess(); task.wait(2)
                        else
                            tokenStatFail = tokenStatFail + 1
                            SetFarmTokenStatus("❌ Revived Failure - " .. (target and target.Name or ""))
                            ClearTargetESP()
                        end

                        ClearTargetESP(); StopSpamE()
                        if not isCharacterDead then SetFarmTokenStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWallSafe() end
                        task.wait(1); isOnWallAndWaiting = true
                        tokenCurrentTargetName, tokenCurrentTargetPos = "—", nil
                    end
                else
                    SetFarmTokenStatus("🔎 Downed player - Đang quét"); task.wait(1)
                end
            end
        else task.wait(0.1) end
    end
    reviveLoopRunning, isOnWallAndWaiting = false, false
end

local function StartRevivePlayers()
    if isRevivingActive then return end
    isRevivingActive = true
    reviveTarget, isOnWallAndWaiting, reviveSuccessCount = nil, false, 0
    tokenStatSuccess, tokenStatFail, tokenStatDead = 0, 0, 0
    tokenCurrentTargetName, tokenCurrentTargetPos = "—", nil
    SaveCurrentPosition("token"); UpdateFarmConflict()

    SetFarmTokenStatus("⏳ Đang khởi động"); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmTokenStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); CreateWall(); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmTokenStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWall(); task.wait(0.5)

    task.spawn(RevivePlayersLoop)
end

local function StopRevivePlayers()
    isRevivingActive, reviveLoopRunning, reviveTarget, isOnWallAndWaiting = false, false, nil, false
    UpdateFarmConflict(); StopSpamE(); ClearTargetESP(); ForceResetMovement()
    if savedPosToken and savedPosTokenSet then
        task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3); TeleportToSavedPosition("token"); task.wait(0.3); ClearSavedPosition("token") end)
    end
end

-- ===== FARM EVENT LOOP =====
local function FarmEventLoop()
    if not isFarming or IsModeBlocked() then 
        task.wait(1)
        if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
        return 
    end

    if not (HasAnyValidEventItem() or HasAnyValidSpecialItem()) then 
        SetFarmEventStatus("🔴 Dừng... - Hết event")
        task.wait(1)
        if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
        return 
    end

    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local char = player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not char or not hrp then 
        SetFarmEventStatus("🟠 Đang xử lý... - Chờ spawn")
        task.wait(0.3)
        if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
        return 
    end
    if isCollecting then 
        task.wait(0.1)
        if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
        return 
    end

    SetFarmEventStatus("🔎 Scan Items - Đang quét")
    local items = getAllItems()
    farmEventTargetName = FormatTargetItemsDisplay()

    if #items == 0 then
        farmEventTargetPos = nil
        ClearEventItemESP()
        task.wait(0.4)
        if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
        return
    end

    local playerPos = hrp.Position
    table.sort(items, function(a, b) 
        return (playerPos - a.Position).Magnitude < (playerPos - b.Position).Magnitude 
    end)

    local target = items[1]
    farmEventTargetPos, farmEventTargetDist = target.Position, (playerPos - target.Position).Magnitude

    CreateEventItemESP(target.Object, target.RawName or target.Name)
    isCollecting = true

    if target.IsSpecial then
        SetFarmEventStatus("⚡ Special Chase - " .. (target.RawName or target.Name))

        local function CollectItem(itemObject)
            pcall(function()
                local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local collectId = itemObject.Parent:GetAttribute("Id") or itemObject:GetAttribute("Id") or "a19ac91bff904b7385e826fd6a23dc01"
                if RS and RS.Events and RS.Events.Collectibles then 
                    RS.Events.Collectibles.Invoke:InvokeServer(player, collectId, "Collect") 
                end
            end)
        end

        local isVanished = false
        local collectSuccess = false

        while not collectSuccess do
            if not target.Object or not target.Object.Parent then
                isVanished = true
                collectSuccess = true
                break
            end
            if isCharacterDead then break end

            local currentHRP = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not currentHRP then task.wait(0.05); continue end
            
            currentHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(target.Object.Position)
            currentHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

            CollectItem(target.Object)
            
            SetFarmEventStatus("⚡ Special Chase - Đang teleport liên tục")
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if isVanished then
            IncrementItemCount(target.Name)
            SetFarmEventStatus("✅ Special collected - " .. target.Name)
        end

        if not isCharacterDead then
            SetFarmEventStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe")
            TeleportToWallSafe()
        end

        isCollecting = false
        ClearEventItemESP()
        task.wait(0.1)

    else
        SetFarmEventStatus("👤 Đang teleporting " .. (target.RawName or target.Name))
        TeleportWithEmote(target.Position, target.Object.CFrame, false, false)
        task.wait(0.1)

        pcall(function()
            local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local collectId = target.Object.Parent:GetAttribute("Id") or target.Object:GetAttribute("Id") or "a19ac91bff904b7385e826fd6a23dc01"
            if RS and RS.Events and RS.Events.Collectibles then 
                RS.Events.Collectibles.Invoke:InvokeServer(player, collectId, "Collect") 
            end
        end)

        local checkTimeout = 0
        local MAX_CHECK_TIMEOUT = 10
        while checkTimeout < MAX_CHECK_TIMEOUT do
            if not target.Object or not target.Object.Parent then
                break
            end
            if isCharacterDead then break end
            local currentHRP = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if currentHRP and target.Object and target.Object.Parent then
                currentHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(target.Object.Position)
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
            checkTimeout = checkTimeout + 0.05
        end

        IncrementItemCount(target.Name)

        isCollecting = false
        ClearEventItemESP()

        if not isCharacterDead then
            SetFarmEventStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe")
            TeleportToWallSafe()
        end
    end

    if isFarming then FarmEventLoop() end
end

local function StartFarmEvent()
    if not (HasAnyValidEventItem() or HasAnyValidSpecialItem()) then 
        Notify("❌ Không còn event", "Không có event để farm!", 4)
        if farmEventToggle then farmEventToggle:Set(false) end
        return 
    end
    if isFarming then return end
    isFarming, isCollecting = true, false
    InitItemCollectedCounts(); SaveCurrentPosition("event"); UpdateFarmConflict()

    SetFarmEventStatus("⏳ Đang khởi động"); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmEventStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); CreateWall(); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmEventStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWall(); task.wait(0.3)

    task.spawn(FarmEventLoop)
end

local function StopFarmEvent()
    isFarming, isCollecting = false, false
    UpdateFarmConflict(); pcall(function() if wallModel then wallModel:Destroy() wallModel = nil end end); ForceResetMovement()
    ClearEventItemESP()
    if savedPosEvent and savedPosEventSet then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3); TeleportToSavedPosition("event"); task.wait(0.3); ClearSavedPosition("event") end) end
end

local function FarmLvLoop()
    if not isFarmingLv or IsModeBlocked() then task.wait(1); if isFarmingLv then FarmLvLoop() end; return end
    if not wallModelLv then SetFarmLvStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); CreateWallLv(); task.wait(2) end
    SetFarmLvStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWallLv()
    SetFarmLvStatus("🟢 Đang chạy - Xử lý thao tác")
    while isFarmingLv do
        task.wait(1)
        if not isFarmingLv then break end
        if not IsModeBlocked() then SetFarmLvStatus("🟢 Đang chạy - Xử lý thao tác") end
    end
end

local function StartFarmLv()
    if isFarmingLv then return end
    isFarmingLv = true; SaveCurrentPosition("lv"); UpdateFarmConflict()
    SetFarmLvStatus("⏳ Đang khởi động"); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmLvStatus("🔨 Đang tạo wall_safe"); CreateWallLv(); task.wait(2)
    SetFarmLvStatus("❗ Teleporting - Wall_Safe"); TeleportToWallLv(); task.wait(0.5)
    task.spawn(FarmLvLoop)
end

local function StopFarmLv()
    isFarmingLv = false; UpdateFarmConflict(); pcall(function() if wallModelLv then wallModelLv:Destroy() wallModelLv = nil end end); ForceResetMovement()
    if savedPosLv and savedPosLvSet then task.spawn(function() task.wait(0.3); TeleportToSavedPosition("lv"); task.wait(0.3); ClearSavedPosition("lv") end) end
end

local function ResetMiningStatistics()
    tokenStatSuccess, tokenStatFail, tokenStatDead = 0, 0, 0
    tokenCurrentTargetName, tokenCurrentTargetPos, tokenCurrentTargetDist = "—", nil, 0
    farmEventTargetName, farmEventTargetPos, farmEventTargetDist = "—", nil, 0
    farmEventAfkTimer, farmLvAfkTimer, farmTokenAfkTimer = 0, 0, 0
    farmEventStatusText, farmLvStatusText, farmTokenStatusText = "⏳ Đang khởi động", "⏳ Đang khởi động", "⏳ Đang khởi động"
    InitItemCollectedCounts()
    if uiFarmEventText then uiFarmEventText:SetDesc("...") end
    if uiFarmLvText then uiFarmLvText:SetDesc("...") end
    if uiFarmTokenText then uiFarmTokenText:SetDesc("...") end
    Notify("🔄 Reset thành công", "Đã reset toàn bộ số liệu", 3)
end

local function StartAutoJoin()
    local clickX, clickY = LoadClickLocation()
    if not clickX or not clickY then
        Notify("❌ Location Click", "Bạn chưa setup để click 😡\nHãy ấn nút 'Set location click' trước!", 6)
        return false
    end
    if autoJoinConn then pcall(function() autoJoinConn:Disconnect() end) end
    autoJoinEnabled = true
    local lastClickTime = 0
    Notify("✔️ Location Click", "Đã phát hiện kích hoạt auto join game", 4)
    autoJoinConn = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not autoJoinEnabled then return end
        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local char = player.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local isAlive = hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0
        local dist = hrp and (hrp.Position - TARGET_XYZ).Magnitude or 99999
        local isInLobby = (dist <= LOBBY_DISTANCE) or (not isAlive)
        if isInLobby then
            local currentTime = tick()
            if currentTime - lastClickTime > 1.5 then
                lastClickTime = currentTime
                if not _G.LobbyNotified then
                    _G.LobbyNotified = true
                    Notify("🔎 Lobby Detect", "Phát hiện đang ở lobby đang thao tác", 3)
                end
                local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                local camPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                local lookDirection = (TARGET_XYZ - camPos).Unit
                camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, camPos + lookDirection)
                isCameraForced = true
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    if not autoJoinEnabled then return end
                    local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(clickX, clickY, 0, true, game, 0)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(clickX, clickY, 0, false, game, 0)
                end)
            end
        else
            if isCameraForced then
                isCameraForced = false
                pcall(function() camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end)
            end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

local function StopAutoJoin()
    autoJoinEnabled = false
    if autoJoinConn then pcall(function() autoJoinConn:Disconnect() end); autoJoinConn = nil end
    isCameraForced = false
    _G.LobbyNotified = false
    pcall(function()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ===== UI TAB FARM ==========================================
-- ============================================================
local comboSection = farmTab:Section({ Title = "Danh sách tổ hợp farm", Opened = true })
uiFarmEventText = comboSection:Paragraph({ Title = "🎁 Farm Event", Desc = "..." })
uiFarmLvText = comboSection:Paragraph({ Title = "📊 Farm Lv", Desc = "..." })
uiFarmTokenText = comboSection:Paragraph({ Title = "💸 Farm Token", Desc = "..." })
comboSection:Button({ Title = "Reset Mining Statistics", Desc = "Reset toàn bộ", Callback = function() ResetMiningStatistics() end })

local extraSection = _G.EvadeHub_AntisTab:Section({ Title = "Automatic", Opened = true })

local autoJoinToggle = extraSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Join (Lobby)",
    Desc = "Tự động vào trận (dùng toạ độ đã ghim)",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "AutoJoin",
    Callback = function(state)
        if state then
            local success = StartAutoJoin()
            if not success and autoJoinToggle then
                autoJoinToggle:Set(false)
            end
        else
            StopAutoJoin()
        end
    end,
})

extraSection:Button({
    Title = "Set location click",
    Desc = "Bấm để mở GUI ghim toạ độ click (có nút Test)",
    Callback = function()
        if DeleteClickLocation() then
            Notify("🗑️ Đã xoá toạ độ cũ", "Toạ độ click cũ đã được xoá, hãy ghim lại!", 3)
        end

        local playerGui = game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not playerGui then return end

        screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        screenGui.Parent = playerGui

        mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
        mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 210)
        mainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
        mainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 3
        mainFrame.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        mainFrame.Parent = screenGui

        infoText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 70)
        infoText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 5)
        infoText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        infoText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        infoText.Font = Enum.Font.Code
        infoText.TextSize = 14
        infoText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        infoText.Text = "BƯỚC 1: Click vào vị trí nút\n'Tham gia trò chơi' trên màn hình..."
        infoText.Parent = mainFrame

        local clickBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        clickBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 40)
        clickBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 80)
        clickBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100)
        clickBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        clickBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        clickBtn.TextSize = 18
        clickBtn.Text = "CHƯA GHIM"
        clickBtn.Parent = mainFrame

        local testBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        testBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, -15, 0, 40)
        testBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 130)
        testBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
        testBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        testBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        testBtn.TextSize = 16
        testBtn.Text = "TEST CLICK"
        testBtn.Parent = mainFrame

        local saveBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        saveBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, -15, 0, 40)
        saveBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.52, 5, 0, 130)
        saveBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 80)
        saveBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        saveBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        saveBtn.TextSize = 16
        saveBtn.Text = "LƯU & ĐÓNG"
        saveBtn.Parent = mainFrame

        local savedWorldPos = nil
        local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

        raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

            local str = string.format("%.2f", n)
            str = str:gsub("0+$", "")
            str = str:gsub("%.$", "")
            return str
        end

            if gameProcessed then return end
            if not savedX and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local mouseLoc = UIS:GetMouseLocation()

                savedX = mouseLoc.X
                savedY = mouseLoc.Y

                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
                    raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character}
                end

                local ray = Camera:ViewportPointToRay(savedX, savedY)
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 5000, raycastParams)

                if hit then
                    savedWorldPos = hit.Position
                else
                    savedWorldPos = ray.Origin + ray.Direction * 500
                end

                infoText.Text = string.format("ĐÃ GHIM:\nScreen: X=%.1f, Y=%.1f\nWorld: X=%s, Y=%s, Z=%s",
                    savedX, savedY,

                clickBtn.Text = "ĐÃ GHIM (Click để đổi)"

                Notify("⚠️ Chưa ghim", "Hãy click vào màn hình để ghim toạ độ trước!", 3)
                return
            end

            if savedWorldPos then
                local camPos = Camera.CFrame.Position
                local lookDirection = (savedWorldPos - camPos).Unit
                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, camPos + lookDirection)
            end

            task.wait(0.15)

            local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(savedX, savedY, 0, true, game, 0)

            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(savedX, savedY, 0, false, game, 0)

            Notify("🖱️ Test Click", "Đã click tại X=" .. savedX .. ", Y=" .. savedY, 3)
        end)

            if savedX and savedY then

                screenGui:Destroy()
            else

            end
        end)
    end,
})

-- Do not use walls: khi đang farm thì tự bật né Next-Bot theo kiểu "Designated location" (chỗ đã quét để đứng), hết farm thì trả lại
;(function()
    local saved = nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.5)
            pcall(function()
                local farming = (isFarming or isFarmingLv or isRevivingActive) and true or false
                local want = (_G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall == true) and farming
                if want and not saved then
                    saved = { Config.AvoidNextbot, Config.AvoidNextbotMode }
                    Config.AvoidNextbot = true
                    Config.AvoidNextbotMode = "Designated location"
                elseif (not want) and saved then
                    Config.AvoidNextbot = saved[1]
                    Config.AvoidNextbotMode = saved[2]
                    saved = nil
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end)()

-- ===== Safety Walls / Do not use walls: tự lên tường (hoặc đứng chỗ an toàn) sau khi game đưa về map =====
;(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LPl = Players.LocalPlayer
    local busy = false

    local function root()
        local c = LPl.Character
        return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function onWall(pos)
        return pos.X >= WALL_POSITION.X - 2 and pos.Y >= WALL_POSITION.Y and pos.Z >= WALL_POSITION.Z - 2
    end
    local function farming()
        return (isFarming or isFarmingLv or isRevivingActive) and true or false
    end

    -- Safety Walls: teleport tức thời liên tục (có delay 0.15s, không lag) tới khi lên được tường; đã ở trên tường thì không teleport
    local function ensureOnWall()
        if busy then return end
        busy = true
        for _ = 1, 40 do
            local hrp = root()
            if not hrp then break end
            if onWall(hrp.Position) then break end
            if not wallModel or not wallModel.Parent then pcall(function() if CreateWall then CreateWall() end end) end
            pcall(function()
                if isFarmingLv and not isFarming and not isRevivingActive then
                    if TeleportToWallLv then TeleportToWallLv() end
                else
                    if TeleportToWall then TeleportToWall() end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
        busy = false
    end

    -- Do not use walls: teleport tức thời tới vị trí NGẪU NHIÊN có thể đứng
    -- (không chỗ kẹt/bị che, không gần điểm spawn); thử tối đa 25 chỗ, có delay giữa các lần
    local function randomStandable()
        if busy then return end
        busy = true
        local hrp = root()
        if hrp then
            local spawns = {}
            for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if o:IsA("SpawnLocation") then table.insert(spawns, o.Position) end
            end
            local params = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LPl.Character }
            local origin = hrp.Position
            for _ = 1, 25 do
                local rx = origin.X + math.random(-120, 120)
                local rz = origin.Z + math.random(-120, 120)
                local hit = workspace:Raycast(Vector3.new(rx, origin.Y + 200, rz), Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), params)
                if hit and hit.Instance and hit.Instance.CanCollide and hit.Normal.Y > 0.9 and hit.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water then
                    local pos = hit.Position
                    local nearSpawn = false
                    for _, sp in ipairs(spawns) do
                        if (sp - pos).Magnitude < 60 then nearSpawn = true break end
                    end
                    -- chỗ trống phía trên (đủ chỗ đứng, không bị kẹt trong vật thể)
                    local blocked = workspace:Raycast(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), Vector3.new(0, 7, 0), params)
                    if not nearSpawn and not blocked then
                        pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)) end)
                        break
                    end
                end
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end
        busy = false
    end

    -- Safety Walls: phát hiện game đưa về map (đang ở tường -> bị đưa đi xa) rồi quá 4s vẫn chưa lên lại tường
    local lastOn, lastPos, jumpAt = false, nil, nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)
            pcall(function()
                local hrp = root()
                if not hrp or not farming() or _G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall then
                    lastPos, jumpAt = nil, nil
                    return
                end
                local pos = hrp.Position
                local on = onWall(pos)
                if lastPos and lastOn and not on and (pos - lastPos).Magnitude > 150 then jumpAt = os.clock() end
                if on then jumpAt = nil end
                if jumpAt and os.clock() - jumpAt >= 4 and not on and not isFarmBlocked and not isCharacterDead then
                    jumpAt = nil
                    task.spawn(ensureOnWall)
                end
                lastOn, lastPos = on, pos
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- mỗi lần hồi sinh / vào map mới: Safety Walls -> lên tường ; Do not use walls -> đứng chỗ ngẫu nhiên an toàn
    LPl.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1.2)
        if not farming() then return end
        if _G.EvadeHub_FarmNoWall then task.spawn(randomStandable) else task.spawn(ensureOnWall) end
    end)
end)()

    Flag = "FarmMode",
    Callback = function(option) farmMode = option or "Chờ cứu" end

local musicTabOK, musicTabErr = pcall(function()

local MusicTab = MusicTabEarly or Window:Tab({ Title = "Files/Music", Icon = "folder" })

MusicTab:Section({ Title = "Danh sách và cách sử dụng" })

local SUPPORTED_EXTS = {".mp3", ".ogg", ".wav", ".flac"}
local musicList = {}
local musicPathMap = {}
local currentSound = nil
local autoPlayEnabled = false
local selectedTrack = nil

local gameSoundsBackup = {}
local gameMuted = false

local function muteGameSounds(exceptSound)
    if gameMuted then return end
    gameSoundsBackup = {}
    for _, obj in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj ~= exceptSound then
            if obj.Parent and obj.Parent ~= workspace then
                table.insert(gameSoundsBackup, { sound = obj, volume = obj.Volume })
                obj.Volume = 0
            end
        end
    end
    gameMuted = true
end

local function restoreGameSounds()
    if not gameMuted then return end
    for _, entry in ipairs(gameSoundsBackup) do
        if entry.sound and entry.sound.Parent then
            pcall(function() entry.sound.Volume = entry.volume end)
        end
    end
    gameSoundsBackup = {}
    gameMuted = false
end

local function checkSupport()
    local support = { readFile = false, playMusic = false, permission = false }
    local ok1, files = pcall(listfiles, "")
    if ok1 and type(files) == "table" then support.readFile = true end
    local ok2 = pcall(function() return getcustomasset end)
    if ok2 and type(getcustomasset) == "function" then support.playMusic = true end
    if ok1 and files then support.permission = true end
    return support
end

local support = checkSupport()
local function emoji(bool) return bool and "✔️" or "❌" end

MusicTab:Paragraph({
    Title = "🔎 Hướng dẫn sử dụng",
    Desc = "1. Dropdown để chọn các music để phát nhạc\n" ..
           "2. Button \"Reset list\" để update lại danh sách music trong Dropdown\n" ..
           "3. Toggle \"Auto Start/Music\" để bật music tự động, khi music hết thì tự phát lại, tự nhiên tắt thì phát lại nhạc\n\n" ..
           "Định dạng đuôi file music: mp3, ogg, wav, flac | Không giới hạn thời gian/Dung lượng của file nhạc\n\n" ..
           "❗Lưu ý: các chức năng của client phải được phép đọc file music đồng thời người dùng phải cấp quyền mới có thể phát và đọc file để phát\n\n" ..
           "Functional support:\n" ..

           "• Phát nhạc: " .. emoji(support.playMusic) .. "\n" ..
