MusicTab:Section({ Title = "File Music" })

local FileInfo = MusicTab:Paragraph({
    Title = "📁 File information",
    Desc = "File name: — | —\n" ..
           "Time of music: 00:00 - 00:00\n" ..
           "Memory of music: —",
    Color = "Green",
})

local function formatTime(sec)
    sec = math.floor(sec or 0)
    local m = math.floor(sec / 60)
    local s = sec % 60
    return string.format("%02d:%02d", m, s)
end

local function formatDate(isoString)
    if not isoString or isoString == "" then return "—" end
    local y, m, d = string.match(isoString, "^(%d+)-(%d+)-(%d+)")
    if y and m and d then
        return string.format("%s/%s/%s", d, m, y)
    end
    return "—"
end

local function estimateSize(durationSec)
    local bytes = durationSec * 16 * 1024
    if bytes >= 1024 * 1024 then
        return string.format("%.2f MB", bytes / (1024 * 1024))
    elseif bytes >= 1024 then
        return string.format("%.2f KB", bytes / 1024)
    end
    return bytes .. " B"
end

local function checkSound(assetId, waitSeconds)
    waitSeconds = waitSeconds or 7
    local tempSound = Instance.new("Sound")
    tempSound.SoundId = assetId
    tempSound.Volume = 0
    tempSound.Parent = workspace

    local waited = 0
    while waited < waitSeconds do
        task.wait(0.1)
        waited = waited + 0.1
    end

    local ok = tempSound.IsLoaded and tempSound.TimeLength > 0
    local reason = ""

    if not ok then
        if not tempSound.IsLoaded then
            reason = "Không load được sound (asset có thể bị private, không tồn tại, hoặc sai định dạng)"
        elseif tempSound.TimeLength <= 0 then
            reason = "Sound không có thời lượng (file bị hỏng hoặc định dạng không được Roblox hỗ trợ)"
        else
            reason = "Không xác định được lỗi"
        end
    end

    local timeLength = tempSound.TimeLength
    tempSound:Destroy()

    return ok, reason, timeLength
end

local function checkLocalFile(path, waitSeconds)
    waitSeconds = waitSeconds or 7
    local ok, assetId = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
    if not ok then
        return false, "Không tạo được asset từ file (file không tồn tại hoặc client không hỗ trợ)", 0
    end
    return checkSound(assetId, waitSeconds)
end

local function startFileInfoLoop(sound, filename)
    local nameOnly, ext = string.match(filename, "^(.-)(%.%w+)$")
    nameOnly = nameOnly or filename
    ext = ext or ""

    task.spawn(function()
        local waited = 0
        while sound.TimeLength <= 0 and waited < 3 do
            task.wait(0.1)
            waited = waited + 0.1
        end

        while sound and sound.Parent do
            local cur = sound.TimePosition or 0
            local total = sound.TimeLength or 0
            pcall(function()
                FileInfo:SetDesc(
                    "File name: " .. nameOnly .. " | " .. ext .. "\n" ..
                    "Time of music: " .. formatTime(cur) .. " - " .. formatTime(total) .. "\n" ..
                    "Memory of music: " .. (total > 0 and estimateSize(total) or "—")
                )
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function isSupported(filename)
    local lower = string.lower(filename)
    for _, ext in ipairs(SUPPORTED_EXTS) do
        if string.sub(lower, -#ext) == ext then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local MUSIC_DIR = FOLDER_NAME .. "/Files_Music"

local function refreshMusicList()
    musicList = {}
    musicPathMap = {}
    pcall(function()
        if type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function" then
            if not isfolder(FOLDER_NAME) then makefolder(FOLDER_NAME) end
            if not isfolder(MUSIC_DIR) then makefolder(MUSIC_DIR) end
        end
    end)

    -- 1) tệp nhạc đang nằm ở thư mục workspace -> tự động chuyển vào Aur_Meow Config/Files_Music
    local okRoot, rootFiles = pcall(listfiles, "")
    if okRoot and type(rootFiles) == "table" then
        for _, filePath in ipairs(rootFiles) do
            local filename = string.match(filePath, "([^/\\]+)$") or filePath
            if isSupported(filename) then
                pcall(function()
                    if type(readfile) == "function" and type(writefile) == "function" and type(delfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(filePath) then
                        local dest = MUSIC_DIR .. "/" .. filename
                        writefile(dest, readfile(filePath))
                        if isfile(dest) then delfile(filePath) end -- chỉ xóa bản gốc khi đã ghi thành công
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- 1b) tìm file nhạc trong bộ nhớ điện thoại (Download, ZArchiver, Music, Documents...) và tự chép vào Files_Music
    local externalImported = 0
    do
        local roots = {
            "/storage/emulated/0/Download", "/storage/emulated/0/Downloads", "/storage/emulated/0/Music",
            "/storage/emulated/0/Documents", "/storage/emulated/0/ZArchiver", "/storage/emulated/0/Movies",
            "/storage/emulated/0", "/sdcard/Download", "/sdcard/Music", "/sdcard",
        }
        local visited, budget = {}, { n = 250 }
        local function scanDir(dir, depth)
            if depth > 3 or budget.n <= 0 or visited[dir] then return end
            visited[dir] = true
            budget.n = budget.n - 1
            local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, dir)
            if not ok or type(files) ~= "table" then return end
            for _, p in ipairs(files) do
                local fname = string.match(p, "([^/\\]+)$") or p
                if isSupported(fname) then
                    local dest = MUSIC_DIR .. "/" .. fname
                    local exists = false
                    pcall(function() exists = type(isfile) == "function" and isfile(dest) end)
                    if not exists then
                        pcall(function()
                            writefile(dest, readfile(p)) -- không đọc được thì bỏ qua (client không cho truy cập)
                            if isfile(dest) then externalImported = externalImported + 1 end
                        end)
                    end
                elseif depth < 3 and not string.find(string.lower(p), "/android", 1, true) and not string.match(fname, "^%.") then
                    local isDir = false
                    pcall(function() isDir = type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder(p) end)
                    if isDir then scanDir(p, depth + 1) end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, r in ipairs(roots) do scanDir(r, 1) end
    end
    if externalImported > 0 then
        pcall(function()
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Files/Music", Content = WindUI.Localization.t("Đã nhập ") .. externalImported .. WindUI.Localization.t(" tệp nhạc từ bộ nhớ điện thoại"), Duration = 4 })
        end)
    end

    -- 2) danh sách nhạc lấy từ Files_Music (cộng tệp nào chưa chuyển được ở workspace)
    local function addFrom(dir)
        local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, dir)
        if ok and type(files) == "table" then
            for _, filePath in ipairs(files) do
                local filename = string.match(filePath, "([^/\\]+)$") or filePath
                if isSupported(filename) and not musicPathMap[filename] then
                    table.insert(musicList, filename)
                    musicPathMap[filename] = filePath
                end
            end
        end
    end
    addFrom(MUSIC_DIR)
    addFrom("")

    if #musicList == 0 then
        table.insert(musicList, "Không tìm thấy tệp nhạc")
    end
    return musicList
end

local function stopCurrent()
    if currentSound then
        pcall(function() currentSound:Stop() end)
        pcall(function() currentSound:Destroy() end)
        currentSound = nil
    end
    restoreGameSounds()
end

local function playSelected()
    if not selectedTrack or selectedTrack == "Không tìm thấy tệp nhạc" then return end
    local path = musicPathMap[selectedTrack]
    if not path then return end

    local ok, assetId = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
    if not ok then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "❌ Checkout Sound",
            Content = "Không tải được file: " .. selectedTrack,
            Duration = 3,
        })
        return
    end

    stopCurrent()

    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = assetId
    sound.Volume = 0.5
    sound.Looped = false
    sound.Parent = workspace
    currentSound = sound

    muteGameSounds(sound)
    sound:Play()

    startFileInfoLoop(sound, selectedTrack)

    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        if autoPlayEnabled and currentSound == sound then
            task.wait(0.3)
            if autoPlayEnabled then
                sound:Play()
            end
        else
            restoreGameSounds()
        end
    end)

    sound:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(function()
        if autoPlayEnabled and currentSound == sound and not sound.Playing then
            task.wait(1)
            if autoPlayEnabled and currentSound == sound and not sound.Playing then
                pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            end
        elseif not autoPlayEnabled and currentSound == sound and not sound.Playing then
            restoreGameSounds()
        end
    end)
end

local checkingFile = false

local function updateFileInfo(filename, path)
    if not filename then
        FileInfo:SetDesc(
            "File name: — | —\n" ..
            "Time of music: 00:00 - 00:00\n" ..
            "Memory of music: —"
        )
        return
    end
    local nameOnly, ext = string.match(filename, "^(.-)(%.%w+)$")
    nameOnly = nameOnly or filename
    ext = ext or ""
    local totalTime = 0
    if path then
        local ok, assetId = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
        if ok then
            local tempSound = Instance.new("Sound")
            tempSound.SoundId = assetId
            tempSound.Parent = workspace
            local waited = 0
            while tempSound.TimeLength <= 0 and waited < 3 do
                task.wait(0.1)
                waited = waited + 0.1
            end
            totalTime = tempSound.TimeLength or 0
            tempSound:Destroy()
        end
    end
    FileInfo:SetDesc(
        "File name: " .. nameOnly .. " | " .. ext .. "\n" ..
        "Time of music: " .. formatTime(0) .. " - " .. formatTime(totalTime) .. "\n" ..
        "Memory of music: " .. (totalTime > 0 and estimateSize(totalTime) or "—")
    )
end

local function onSelectMusic(option)
    if checkingFile then return end
    if not option or option == "Không tìm thấy tệp nhạc" then
        selectedTrack = nil
        updateFileInfo(nil)
        return
    end

    checkingFile = true
    selectedTrack = option
    local path = musicPathMap[option]

    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "🔎 Checking & filtering music",
        Content = "Đang tải và kiểm tra " .. option .. "...",
        Duration = 3,
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, reason, timeLength = checkLocalFile(path, 7)

        if not ok then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "❌ Checkout Sound",
                Content = "Không phát được: " .. option .. "\nLý do: " .. reason,
                Duration = 3,
            })
            checkingFile = false
            return
        end

        local nameOnly, ext = string.match(option, "^(.-)(%.%w+)$")
        nameOnly = nameOnly or option
        ext = ext or ""
        FileInfo:SetDesc(
            "File name: " .. nameOnly .. " | " .. ext .. "\n" ..
            "Time of music: 00:00 - " .. formatTime(timeLength) .. "\n" ..
            "Memory of music: " .. estimateSize(timeLength)
        )

        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "✔️ Checkout Sound",
            Content = "Kiểm tra hoàn tất đợi khoảng 4 giây sẽ phát",
            Duration = 3,
        })

        task.wait(4)

        if autoPlayEnabled then
            playSelected()
        end

        checkingFile = false
    end)
end

local Dropdown = MusicTab:Dropdown({
    Title = "Chọn nhạc",
    Desc = "Danh sách nhạc trong workspace Delta",
    Values = refreshMusicList(),
    Value = nil,
    Multi = false,
    AllowNone = true,
    Callback = onSelectMusic,
})

MusicTab:Button({
    Title = "Reset list",
    Desc = "Quét lại danh sách nhạc trong workspace",
    Callback = function()
        local newList = refreshMusicList()
        Dropdown:Refresh(newList)
        selectedTrack = nil
        updateFileInfo(nil)
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "Reset list",
            Content = WindUI.Localization.t("Đã cập nhật: ") .. #newList .. WindUI.Localization.t(" bài"),
            Duration = 3,
        })
    end,
})

local autoStartToggle, randomToggle
local randomEnabled = false
local randomQueue = {}

-- phát 1 file nhạc 1 lần, hết bài thì gọi onEnded
local function playFileOnce(name, onEnded)
    local path = musicPathMap[name]
    if not path then return false end
    local ok, assetId = pcall(getcustomasset, path)
    if not ok then return false end
    stopCurrent()
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = assetId
    sound.Volume = 0.5
    sound.Looped = false
    sound.Parent = workspace
    currentSound = sound
    muteGameSounds(sound)
    sound:Play()
    startFileInfoLoop(sound, name)
    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        if currentSound == sound and onEnded then onEnded() end
    end)
    return true
end

local function playRandomNext()
    if not randomEnabled then return end
    local name = table.remove(randomQueue, 1)
    if not name then
        -- đã phát hết danh sách: dừng và tắt toggle
        randomEnabled = false
        stopCurrent()
        if randomToggle then randomToggle:Set(false, true) end
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Auto Random MP3", Content = "Đã phát hết danh sách nhạc", Duration = 4 })
        return
    end
    selectedTrack = name
    local started = playFileOnce(name, function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        playRandomNext()
    end)
    if not started then playRandomNext() end -- file lỗi thì bỏ qua, phát bài kế tiếp
end

autoStartToggle = MusicTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Start/Music",
    Desc = "Tự động phát, lặp lại và tự phát lại khi bị gián đoạn",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        autoPlayEnabled = state
        if state then
            if randomToggle and randomToggle.Value then randomToggle:Set(false) end -- 2 chế độ không chạy cùng lúc
            playSelected()
        else
            stopCurrent()
        end
    end,
})

randomToggle = MusicTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Random MP3",
    Desc = "Tự động phát ngẫu nhiên từng bài mp3, hết bài thì đổi bài khác cho đến khi phát hết danh sách",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        randomEnabled = state
        if state then
            if autoStartToggle and autoStartToggle.Value then autoStartToggle:Set(false) end
            autoPlayEnabled = false
            randomQueue = {}
            for _, name in ipairs(musicList) do
                if musicPathMap[name] then table.insert(randomQueue, name) end
            end
            for i = #randomQueue, 2, -1 do -- xáo trộn ngẫu nhiên (Fisher–Yates)
                local j = math.random(1, i)
                randomQueue[i], randomQueue[j] = randomQueue[j], randomQueue[i]
            end
            if #randomQueue == 0 then
                randomEnabled = false
                randomToggle:Set(false, true)
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Auto Random MP3", Content = "Không tìm thấy tệp nhạc", Duration = 3 })
                return
            end
            playRandomNext()
        else
            stopCurrent()
        end
    end,
})

-- ===== Section LinkURL (dưới cùng tab Music): Dropdown + Input + Toggle "Auto download & start" =====
MusicTab:Section({ Title = "LinkURL", Opened = true })
local linkDropdown, linkInput, linkToggle
local linkOn = false
local linkSelected = nil

local function linkFiles()
    local list = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(musicList) do
        if musicPathMap[n] then table.insert(list, n) end
    end
    if #list == 0 then list = { "Không tìm thấy tệp nhạc" } end
    return list
end

linkDropdown = MusicTab:Dropdown({
    Title = "Danh sách nhạc đã có / đã tải",
    Desc = "Tất cả file nhạc đã có sẵn, tìm thấy hoặc tải từ link",
    Values = linkFiles(),
    Callback = function(v) linkSelected = v end,
})

linkInput = MusicTab:Input({
    Title = "Link URL nhạc",
    Desc = "Dán link tải trực tiếp file nhạc (mp3, ogg, wav, flac...)",
    Value = "",
    Placeholder = "https://.../bai-hat.mp3",
    Callback = function() end,
})

-- tên file: MeowSound(N).ext ; N = số kế tiếp chưa dùng ; đuôi theo link, không có thì chọn ngẫu nhiên
local function nextMeowName(url)
    local clean = (url or ""):gsub("[%?#].*$", "")
    local ext = clean:match("%.([%w]+)$")
    ext = ext and string.lower(ext) or nil
    local valid = { mp3 = true, ogg = true, wav = true, flac = true, m4a = true, aac = true, opus = true }
    if not (ext and valid[ext]) then
        local pool = { "mp3", "ogg", "wav", "flac" }
        ext = pool[math.random(1, #pool)]
    end
    local maxN = 0
    local okL, files = pcall(listfiles, MUSIC_DIR)
    if okL and type(files) == "table" then
        for _, p in ipairs(files) do
            local fname = string.match(p, "([^/\\]+)$") or p
            local n = tonumber(string.match(fname, "^MeowSound%((%d+)%)%."))
            if n and n > maxN then maxN = n end
        end
    end
    return "MeowSound(" .. (maxN + 1) .. ")." .. ext
end

local function playLink(name)
    if not linkOn then return end
    local started = playFileOnce(name, function()
        task.wait(0.3)
        playLink(name) -- lặp lại bài khi hết (Looped)
    end)
    if not started then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Không phát được: " .. tostring(name), Duration = 4 })
    end
end

linkToggle = MusicTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto download & start",
    Desc = "Tự tải nhạc từ link (nếu có link), lưu vào Files_Music rồi phát lặp lại; không có link thì phát bài đang chọn",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        linkOn = state
        if not state then
            stopCurrent()
            return
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local url = tostring(linkInput.Value or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
            local name
            if url ~= "" then
                local okH, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
                if not okH or type(body) ~= "string" or #body < 1000 then
                    linkOn = false
                    linkToggle:Set(false, true)
                    WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Không tải được file từ link", Duration = 4 })
                    return
                end
                local head = string.lower(body:sub(1, 20))
                if head:find("<!doctype", 1, true) or head:find("<html", 1, true) then
                    linkOn = false
                    linkToggle:Set(false, true)
                    WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Link không phải file nhạc (đây là trang web)", Duration = 5 })
                    return
                end
                name = nextMeowName(url)
                local path = MUSIC_DIR .. "/" .. name
                local okW = pcall(function() writefile(path, body) end)
                if not okW then
                    linkOn = false
                    linkToggle:Set(false, true)
                    WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Không lưu được file", Duration = 4 })
                    return
                end
                table.insert(musicList, name)
                musicPathMap[name] = path
                pcall(function() linkDropdown:Refresh(linkFiles()) end)
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Đã tải: " .. name, Duration = 4 })
            else
                name = linkSelected
                if not name or not musicPathMap[name] then
                    linkOn = false
                    linkToggle:Set(false, true)
                    WindUI:Notify({ Title = "LinkURL", Content = "Chưa nhập link hoặc chưa chọn bài", Duration = 4 })
                    return
                end
            end
            if autoStartToggle and autoStartToggle.Value then autoStartToggle:Set(false) end
            if randomToggle and randomToggle.Value then randomToggle:Set(false) end
            playLink(name)
        end)
    end,
})


MusicTab:Section({ Title = "ID Music" })

local currentAudio = nil
local autoAudioEnabled = false
local currentAudioId = nil

local AudioInfo = MusicTab:Paragraph({
    Title = "🎶 Audio roblox",
    Desc = "Audio creator: —\n" ..
           "Audio name: —\n" ..
           "Target asset ID: —\n" ..
           "Date created: —\n" ..
           "Audio length: 00:00 - 00:00",
    Color = "Orange",
})

local function updateAudioInfo(id)
    if not id or id == "" then
        AudioInfo:SetDesc(
            "Audio creator: —\n" ..
            "Audio name: —\n" ..
            "Target asset ID: —\n" ..
            "Date created: —\n" ..
            "Audio length: 00:00 - 00:00"
        )
        return
    end

    local targetId = tostring(id)
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, info = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfoAsync(
                tonumber(targetId),
                Enum.InfoType.Asset
            )
        end)

        local creatorName = "—"
        local audioName = "—"
        local dateCreated = "—"
        if ok and info then
            if info.Creator then creatorName = info.Creator.Name or "—" end
            if info.Name then audioName = info.Name end
            if info.Created then dateCreated = formatDate(info.Created) end
        end

        AudioInfo:SetDesc(
            "Audio creator: " .. creatorName .. "\n" ..
            "Audio name: " .. audioName .. "\n" ..
            "Target asset ID: " .. targetId .. "\n" ..
            "Date created: " .. dateCreated .. "\n" ..
            "Audio length: 00:00 - 00:00"
        )
    end)
end

local function startAudioInfoLoop(sound)
    task.spawn(function()
        local waited = 0
        while sound.TimeLength <= 0 and waited < 3 do
            task.wait(0.1)
            waited = waited + 0.1
        end

        local creatorName = "—"
        local audioName = "—"
        local dateCreated = "—"
        local okInfo, info = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfoAsync(
                tonumber(currentAudioId),
                Enum.InfoType.Asset
            )
        end)
        if okInfo and info then
            if info.Creator then creatorName = info.Creator.Name or "—" end
            if info.Name then audioName = info.Name end
            if info.Created then dateCreated = formatDate(info.Created) end
        end

        while sound and sound.Parent do
            local cur = sound.TimePosition or 0
            local total = sound.TimeLength or 0
            pcall(function()
                AudioInfo:SetDesc(
                    "Audio creator: " .. creatorName .. "\n" ..
                    "Audio name: " .. audioName .. "\n" ..
                    "Target asset ID: " .. tostring(currentAudioId) .. "\n" ..
                    "Date created: " .. dateCreated .. "\n" ..
                    "Audio length: " .. formatTime(cur) .. " - " .. formatTime(total)
                )
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAudio()
    if currentAudio then
        pcall(function() currentAudio:Stop() end)
        pcall(function() currentAudio:Destroy() end)
        currentAudio = nil
    end
    restoreGameSounds()
end

local function playAudioById()
    if not currentAudioId or currentAudioId == "" then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "❌ Checkout Sound",
            Content = "Chưa nhập ID sound!",
            Duration = 3,
        })
        return
    end

    local soundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(currentAudioId)
    stopAudio()

    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = soundId
    sound.Volume = 0.5
    sound.Looped = false
    sound.Parent = workspace
    currentAudio = sound

    muteGameSounds(sound)

    local ok, err = pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
    if not ok then
        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "❌ Checkout Sound",
            Content = "ID không hợp lệ hoặc bị private.",
            Duration = 3,
        })
        restoreGameSounds()
        return
    end

    startAudioInfoLoop(sound)

    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        if autoAudioEnabled and currentAudio == sound then
            task.wait(0.3)
            if autoAudioEnabled then
                pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            end
        else
            restoreGameSounds()
        end
    end)

    sound:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(function()
        if autoAudioEnabled and currentAudio == sound and not sound.Playing then
            task.wait(1)
            if autoAudioEnabled and currentAudio == sound and not sound.Playing then
                pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            end
        elseif not autoAudioEnabled and currentAudio == sound and not sound.Playing then
            restoreGameSounds()
        end
    end)
end

local checkingAudio = false
local lastCheckedId = nil

local function onInputId(text)
    currentAudioId = text
    updateAudioInfo(text)

    if checkingAudio then return end
    if not text or text == "" then return end
    if text == lastCheckedId then return end

    lastCheckedId = text
    checkingAudio = true

    WindUI:Notify({
        Title = "🔎 Checking & filtering music",
        Content = "Đang tải và kiểm tra ID " .. text .. "...",
        Duration = 3,
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        local assetId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(text)
        local ok, reason, timeLength = checkSound(assetId, 7)

        if not ok then
            WindUI:Notify({
                Title = "❌ Checkout Sound",
                Content = "Không phát được ID " .. text .. "\nLý do: " .. reason,
                Duration = 3,
            })
            checkingAudio = false
            return
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            local okInfo, info = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfoAsync(
                    tonumber(text),
                    Enum.InfoType.Asset
                )
            end)
            local creatorName = "—"
            local audioName = "—"
            local dateCreated = "—"
            if okInfo and info then
                if info.Creator then creatorName = info.Creator.Name or "—" end
                if info.Name then audioName = info.Name end
                if info.Created then dateCreated = formatDate(info.Created) end
            end
            AudioInfo:SetDesc(
                "Audio creator: " .. creatorName .. "\n" ..
                "Audio name: " .. audioName .. "\n" ..
                "Target asset ID: " .. text .. "\n" ..
                "Date created: " .. dateCreated .. "\n" ..
                "Audio length: 00:00 - " .. formatTime(timeLength)
            )
        end)

        WindUI:Notify({
            Title = "✔️ Checkout Sound",
            Content = "Kiểm tra hoàn tất đợi khoảng 4 giây sẽ phát",
            Duration = 3,
        })

        task.wait(4)

        if autoAudioEnabled then
            playAudioById()
        end

        checkingAudio = false
    end)
end

if not musicTabOK then

-- ===== END TAB FILES/MUSIC =====

-- ============================================================
-- ===== REALTIME UPDATE TEXT (ĐÃ SẮP XẾP CHUẨN) ==============
-- ============================================================
task.spawn(function()
    local lastTickEvent, lastTickLv, lastTickToken = tick(), tick(), tick()
    local lastScanTime, cachedScanText = 0, ""

    while task.wait(0.5) do
        local now = tick()

        if isFarming then
            local delta = now - lastTickEvent
            lastTickEvent = now
            if _G.FarmEventUserActive then farmEventAfkTimer = 0; _G.FarmEventUserActive = false 
            else farmEventAfkTimer = farmEventAfkTimer + delta end
        else lastTickEvent = now end

        if isFarmingLv then
            local delta = now - lastTickLv
            lastTickLv = now
            if _G.FarmLvUserActive then farmLvAfkTimer = 0; _G.FarmLvUserActive = false 
            else farmLvAfkTimer = farmLvAfkTimer + delta end
        else lastTickLv = now end

        if isRevivingActive then
            local delta = now - lastTickToken
            lastTickToken = now
            if _G.FarmTokenUserActive then farmTokenAfkTimer = 0; _G.FarmTokenUserActive = false 
            else farmTokenAfkTimer = farmTokenAfkTimer + delta end
        else lastTickToken = now end

        -- ===== AUTO REJOIN THEO COUNTER AFK =====
        -- Chỉ được tham gia lại khi Counter AFK đang hiển thị đạt 00:18:45 (1125 giây).
        -- Dùng đúng các biến farmEventAfkTimer / farmLvAfkTimer / farmTokenAfkTimer mà panel hiển thị, không đếm riêng.
        if antiAfkRejoinEnabled and not _G.EvadeHub_Rejoining then
            local REJOIN_AT = 1125 -- 00:18:45
            if (isFarming and farmEventAfkTimer >= REJOIN_AT)
                or (isFarmingLv and farmLvAfkTimer >= REJOIN_AT)
                or (isRevivingActive and farmTokenAfkTimer >= REJOIN_AT) then
                _G.EvadeHub_Rejoining = true
                task.delay(30, function() _G.EvadeHub_Rejoining = false end) -- mở khóa lại nếu teleport thất bại
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Auto Rejoin", Content = "Counter AFK đạt 00:18:45, đang chuyển server...", Duration = 5 })
                pcall(function()
                    if type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" then queue_on_teleport(AUTO_EXECUTE_CMD) end
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
                end)
            end
        end

        if now - lastScanTime >= 2 then 
            lastScanTime = now
            cachedScanText = FormatScanEventItemsDisplay() 
        end

        local livePos = nil
        local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then livePos = hrp.Position end
        end

        if uiFarmEventText then
            if isFarming then
                local info = "Target items: " .. (farmEventTargetName or "—")
                if farmEventTargetPos and livePos then 
                    info = info .. string.format("\nLocation: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f) - %.1f studs", 
                        farmEventTargetPos.X, farmEventTargetPos.Y, farmEventTargetPos.Z, (livePos - farmEventTargetPos).Magnitude)
                else 
                    info = info .. "\nLocation: " .. locationDots 
                end
                info = info .. "\nPlayers: " .. playerCountNow
                info = info .. "\nCounter AFK: " .. FormatTime(farmEventAfkTimer)

                info = info .. "\n\n----------------------------------------------------------------"

                info = info .. "\nFPS: " .. currentFps .. " | CPU: " .. currentCpu .. " | Ping: " .. currentPing .. " | GPU: " .. currentGpu

                if currentIsInLobby then
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: ❗Lobby detected - Đang ở lobby chờ joined"
                else
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: " .. farmEventStatusText
                end

                info = info .. "\n\nScan EventItems:\n" .. cachedScanText

                uiFarmEventText:SetDesc(info)
            else 
                uiFarmEventText:SetDesc("...") 
            end
        end

        if uiFarmLvText then
            if isFarmingLv then
                local info = "FPS: " .. currentFps .. " | CPU: " .. currentCpu .. " | Ping: " .. currentPing .. " | GPU: " .. currentGpu
                info = info .. "\nPlayers: " .. playerCountNow
                info = info .. "\nLocation: " .. (livePos and string.format("(%.1f, %.1f, %.1f)", livePos.X, livePos.Y, livePos.Z) or "—")
                info = info .. "\nCounter AFK: " .. FormatTime(farmLvAfkTimer)

                if currentIsInLobby then
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: ❗Lobby detected - Đang ở lobby chờ joined"
                else
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: " .. farmLvStatusText
                end

                uiFarmLvText:SetDesc(info)
            else 
                uiFarmLvText:SetDesc("...") 
            end
        end

        if uiFarmTokenText then
            if isRevivingActive then
                local info = "Target Players: " .. tokenCurrentTargetName
                info = info .. "\nFarm Mode: " .. farmMode
                info = info .. "\nPlayers: " .. playerCountNow
                info = info .. "\nFPS: " .. currentFps .. " | CPU: " .. currentCpu .. " | Ping: " .. currentPing .. " | GPU: " .. currentGpu

                if tokenCurrentTargetPos and livePos then 
                    info = info .. string.format("\nLocation: (%.1f, %.1f, %.1f) - %.1f studs", 
                        tokenCurrentTargetPos.X, tokenCurrentTargetPos.Y, tokenCurrentTargetPos.Z, (livePos - tokenCurrentTargetPos).Magnitude)
                else 
                    info = info .. "\nLocation: " .. locationDots 
                end

                info = info .. "\nCounter AFK: " .. FormatTime(farmTokenAfkTimer)
                info = info .. "\nPlayer Success: " .. tokenStatSuccess
                info = info .. "\nThe user is dead: " .. tokenStatDead

                if currentIsInLobby then
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: ❗Lobby detected - Đang ở lobby chờ joined"
                else
                    info = info .. "\nStatus Farm: " .. farmTokenStatusText
                end

                uiFarmTokenText:SetDesc(info)
            else 
                uiFarmTokenText:SetDesc("...") 
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- ===== GỘP TỪ CODE "OTHER AMENITIES" (đã bỏ phần load WindUI + tạo Window) =====
-- ===== Gồm 3 tab: Other amenities / Server & Teleporting / Notify Webhook =====
-- ============================================================
do
local otherOk, otherErr = pcall(function()
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

-- ============================================================

local SkillsTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Skills & Performance", Icon = "zap" })
local OtherTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Other amenities", Icon = "settings" })
local ServerTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Server & Teleporting", Icon = "globe" })
local NotifyTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Notify Webhook", Icon = "message-circle" })
-- Tab Settings mới (cuối cùng); nếu icon không có thì tạo không icon
-- ===== Skills & Performance: Lag switch (bật toggle = tạo GUI, tắt toggle = phá hủy GUI) =====
;(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local lagState = { gui = nil, conns = {}, lagging = false, thread = nil }

    local function destroyLagGui()
        lagState.lagging = false
        if lagState.thread then pcall(task.cancel, lagState.thread); lagState.thread = nil end
        for _, c in pairs(lagState.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        lagState.conns = {}
        if lagState.gui then pcall(function() lagState.gui:Destroy() end); lagState.gui = nil end
    end
    if _G.EvadeHub_LagDestroy then pcall(_G.EvadeHub_LagDestroy) end
    _G.EvadeHub_LagDestroy = destroyLagGui

    local function createLagGui()
        destroyLagGui()
        local PlayerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

        -- ===== GUI =====
        local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        ScreenGui.Name = "LagSwitchGui"
        ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        ScreenGui.Parent = PlayerGui
        lagState.gui = ScreenGui

        local MainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 130)
        MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, -65)
        MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
        MainFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        MainFrame.Active = true
        MainFrame.Parent = ScreenGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", MainFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local Stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        Stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        Stroke.Thickness = 1
        Stroke.Parent = MainFrame

        local TitleBar = Instance.new("Frame")
        TitleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        TitleBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
        TitleBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        TitleBar.Active = true
        TitleBar.Parent = MainFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", TitleBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

        local TitleFix = Instance.new("Frame")
        TitleFix.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8)
        TitleFix.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -8)
        TitleFix.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
        TitleFix.BorderSizePixel = 0
        TitleFix.Parent = TitleBar

        local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        Title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
        Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
        Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Title.Text = "Lag Switch  |  F"
        Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        Title.TextSize = 12
        Title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        Title.Parent = TitleBar

        local ToggleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        ToggleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38)
        ToggleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 38)
        ToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)
        ToggleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        ToggleBtn.Text = "OFF"
        ToggleBtn.TextSize = 14
        ToggleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        ToggleBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ToggleBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        ToggleBtn.Parent = MainFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", ToggleBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

        local StatusLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        StatusLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18)
        StatusLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 84)
        StatusLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        StatusLabel.Text = "Idle"
        StatusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 130)
        StatusLabel.TextSize = 11
        StatusLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        StatusLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        StatusLabel.Parent = MainFrame

        -- ===== LOGIC LAG =====
        local function setState(active)
            TweenService:Create(ToggleBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(180, 40, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 48)
            }):Play()
            ToggleBtn.Text = active and "ON" or "OFF"
            StatusLabel.Text = active and "Lagging..." or "Idle"
            StatusLabel.TextColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 130)
        end

        local function startLag()
            lagState.lagging = true
            setState(true)
            lagState.thread = task.spawn(function()
                while lagState.lagging do
                    for i = 1, 250 do
                        task.spawn(function()
                            local n = 0
                            for j = 1, 40000 do
                                n += math.sqrt(j) * math.sin(j) * math.cos(j)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function stopLag()
            lagState.lagging = false
            setState(false)
            if lagState.thread then
                pcall(task.cancel, lagState.thread)
                lagState.thread = nil
            end
        end

        local function toggle()
            if lagState.lagging then stopLag() else startLag() end
        end

        ToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggle)

        -- Phím tắt F
        lagState.conns.hotkey = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if processed then return end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F then
                toggle()
            end
        end)

        -- ===== KÉO THẢ MƯỢT =====
        local dragging = false
        local dragStart = nil
        local startPos = nil

        local function clampPosition(pos)
            local viewport = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
            local frameSize = MainFrame.AbsoluteSize
            local x = math.clamp(pos.X.Offset, -frameSize.X + 40, viewport.X - 40)
            local y = math.clamp(pos.Y.Offset, 0, viewport.Y - 30)
            return UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
        end

        local function beginDrag(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = MainFrame.Position
                MainFrame.ZIndex = 10
            end
        end

        local function updateDrag(input)
            if not dragging then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                local newPos = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale,
                    startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale,
                    startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
                MainFrame.Position = clampPosition(newPos)
            end
        end

        local function endDrag(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
                MainFrame.ZIndex = 1
            end
        end

        TitleBar.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
        lagState.conns.changed = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(updateDrag)
        lagState.conns.ended = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(endDrag)
    end

    _G.EvadeHub_SetLagSwitch = function(value)
        if value then task.spawn(createLagGui) else destroyLagGui() end
    end

    local skillsSection = SkillsTab:Section({ Title = "Skills", Opened = true })
    skillsSection:Toggle({
        Title = "Lag switch",
        Desc = "Bật = tạo GUI Lag Switch (kéo thả được, phím tắt F), tắt = xóa GUI",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            if _G.EvadeHub_SetLagSwitch then _G.EvadeHub_SetLagSwitch(value) end
        end,
    })
end)()


-- ===== Section Notify (tab Esp & Notify & Camera): Notify Players / Next-Bot / EventItems =====
;(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local notifySection = _G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Section({ Title = "Notify", Opened = true, Order = 200, NoDivider = true })

    local function myHrp()
        local c = LP.Character
        return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    -- Distance = khoảng cách 3D, Radius = bán kính theo mặt phẳng ngang (X, Z)
    local function distances(pos)
        local hrp = myHrp()
        if not hrp then return nil, nil end
        local d = (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude
        local r = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X - pos.X, 0, hrp.Position.Z - pos.Z).Magnitude
        return d, r
    end
    local function fmt(n) return n and string.format("%.1f studs", n) or "--" end

    -- ================== NOTIFY PLAYERS ==================
    local playersOn, playersGen = false, 0
    local pState = {}
    local STATUS_TEXT = { alive = "✔️ Survive...", downed = "❌ downed...", dead = "🚫 Dead..." }

    local function playerStatus(plr)
        local char = plr.Character
        if not char or not char.Parent then return "dead" end            -- chết: không có character
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then return "downed" end              -- bị hạ gục: giống Farm Token (Health <= 0)
        return "alive"                                                   -- sống sót: kiểm tra HP
    end
    local function playerContent(plr, status)
        local root = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local d, r = nil, nil
        if root then d, r = distances(root.Position) end
        return string.format("Players name: %s - @%s\nDistance: %s | Radius: %s\nPlayer status: %s",
            plr.DisplayName, plr.Name, fmt(d), fmt(r), STATUS_TEXT[status])
    end
    local function notifyPlayer(plr, status, forceNew)
        local st = pState[plr]
        if not st then st = {}; pState[plr] = st end
        local content = playerContent(plr, status)
        local img = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. tostring(plr.UserId) .. "&w=150&h=150"
        if st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive() and not forceNew then
            st.handle:Update({ Title = "👤 Notify Players", Content = content, Image = img, Duration = 6 }) -- đổi chữ ✔️ -> ❌ ngay trên thông báo
        else
            st.handle = WindUI:Notify({ Title = "👤 Notify Players", Content = content, Image = img, Duration = 6 })
        end
        st.status = status
    end

    -- ================== NOTIFY NEXT-BOT ==================
    local botOn, botGen = false, 0
    local bState = {}
    local function scanBots()
        local found = {}
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:GetAttribute("Team") == "Nextbot" then found[obj] = true end
        end
        return found
    end
    local function botRoot(m)
        return m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or m:FindFirstChild("Torso") or m.PrimaryPart
    end
    local function botContent(m, present)
        local root = present and botRoot(m)
        local d, r = nil, nil
        if root then d, r = distances(root.Position) end
        return string.format("Distance: %s | Radius: %s\nStatus Next-Bot: %s", fmt(d), fmt(r),
            present and "✔️ There is next-bot..." or "❌ No next-bot...")
    end
    local function notifyBot(m, present, forceNew)
        local st = bState[m]
        if not st then st = {}; bState[m] = st end
        local title = "🤖 Next-Bot name: " .. tostring(m.Name)
        local content = botContent(m, present)
        if st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive() and not forceNew then
            st.handle:Update({ Title = title, Content = content, Duration = 6 })
        else
            st.handle = WindUI:Notify({ Title = title, Content = content, Icon = "🤖", Duration = 6 })
        end
        st.present = present
    end

    -- ================== NOTIFY EVENTITEMS ==================
    local itemOn, itemGen = false, 0
    local iState = {}
    local function scanItems()
        local found = {}
        local match = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(GetValidEventItemNames()) do match[string.lower(n)] = n end
        for _, n in ipairs(GetValidSpecialItemNames()) do match[string.lower(n)] = n end
        if next(match) == nil then return found end
        local myChar = LP.Character
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) and not (myChar and obj:IsDescendantOf(myChar)) then
                local lower = string.lower(obj.Name)
                local matched = match[lower]
                if not matched then
                    for key, real in pairs(match) do
                        if string.find(lower, key, 1, true) then matched = real break end
                    end
                end
                if matched then
                    local part = obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if part and part.Parent then found[part] = matched end
                end
            end
        end
        return found
    end
    local function itemContent(st, present)
        local p = st.pos or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        local d = distances(p)
        return string.format("EventItem name: %s\nLocation: %.1f, %.1f, %.1f | Distance: %s\nStatus EventItems: %s",
            tostring(st.name), p.X, p.Y, p.Z, fmt(d),
            present and "✔️ Spawning EventItems..." or "❌ EventItems Disappear...")
    end
    local function notifyItem(part, present, forceNew)
        local st = iState[part]
        if not st then return end
        if present and part.Parent then st.pos = part.Position end
        local content = itemContent(st, present)
        if st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive() and not forceNew then
            st.handle:Update({ Title = "🎁 Notify EventItems", Content = content, Duration = 6 })
        else
            st.handle = WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🎁 Notify EventItems", Content = content, Icon = "🎁", Duration = 6 })
        end
        st.present = present
    end

    -- ================== TOGGLES ==================
    notifySection:Toggle({
        Title = "Notify Players",
        Desc = "Tự động thông báo tất cả người chơi, sau đó báo khi họ sống / bị hạ gục / chết",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            playersOn = v
            playersGen = playersGen + 1
            local myGen = playersGen
            pState = {}
            if not v then return end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do -- bật toggle: thông báo TẤT CẢ người chơi (trừ bản thân)
                if plr ~= LP then notifyPlayer(plr, playerStatus(plr), true) end
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local tickN = 0
                while playersOn and playersGen == myGen do
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    tickN = tickN + 1
                    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if plr ~= LP then
                            local status = playerStatus(plr)
                            local st = pState[plr]
                            if not st then
                                notifyPlayer(plr, status, true)               -- người chơi mới vào
                            elseif st.status ~= status then
                                notifyPlayer(plr, status, false)              -- đổi trạng thái: sống / bị hạ / chết
                            elseif tickN % 2 == 0 and st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive() then
                                st.handle:Update({ Content = playerContent(plr, status), NoExtend = true }) -- làm mới khoảng cách
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    for plr in pairs(pState) do
                        if not plr.Parent then pState[plr] = nil end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end,
    })

    notifySection:Toggle({
        Title = "Notify Next-Bot",
        Desc = "Tự động thông báo tất cả Next-Bot, sau đó báo khi có Next-Bot mới xuất hiện hoặc biến mất",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            botOn = v
            botGen = botGen + 1
            local myGen = botGen
            bState = {}
            if not v then return end
            local first = scanBots()
            local any = false
            for m in pairs(first) do
                any = true
                bState[m] = {}
                notifyBot(m, true, true)
            end
            if not any then
                WindUI:Notify({
                    Title = "🤖 Next-Bot name: --",
                    Content = "Distance: -- | Radius: --\nStatus Next-Bot: ❌ No next-bot...",
                    Icon = "🤖", Duration = 5,
                })
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                while botOn and botGen == myGen do
                    task.wait(1)
                    local found = scanBots()
                    for m in pairs(found) do
                        if not bState[m] then
                            bState[m] = {}
                            notifyBot(m, true, true)                           -- Next-Bot mới xuất hiện
                        elseif bState[m].handle and bState[m].handle:IsAlive() then
                            bState[m].handle:Update({ Content = botContent(m, true), NoExtend = true })
                        end
                    end
                    for m, st in pairs(bState) do
                        if st.present and not found[m] then
                            notifyBot(m, false, false)                         -- Next-Bot biến mất
                        end
                        if st.present == false and not (st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive()) then
                            bState[m] = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end,
    })

    notifySection:Toggle({
        Title = "Notify EventItems",
        Desc = "Tự động thông báo tất cả vật phẩm sự kiện, sau đó báo khi có vật phẩm mới xuất hiện hoặc biến mất",
        Value = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            itemOn = v
            itemGen = itemGen + 1
            local myGen = itemGen
            iState = {}
            if not v then return end
            for part, name in pairs(scanItems()) do
                iState[part] = { name = name, pos = part.Position }
                notifyItem(part, true, true)
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local tickN = 0
                while itemOn and itemGen == myGen do
                    task.wait(1)
                    tickN = tickN + 1
                    local found = scanItems()
                    for part, name in pairs(found) do
                        if not iState[part] then
                            iState[part] = { name = name, pos = part.Position }
                            notifyItem(part, true, true)                       -- vật phẩm mới xuất hiện
                        elseif tickN % 2 == 0 and iState[part].handle and iState[part].handle:IsAlive() then
                            iState[part].handle:Update({ Content = itemContent(iState[part], true), NoExtend = true })
                        end
                    end
                    for part, st in pairs(iState) do
                        if st.present and not found[part] then
                            notifyItem(part, false, false)                     -- vật phẩm biến mất
                        end
                        if st.present == false and not (st.handle and st.handle:IsAlive()) then
                            iState[part] = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end,
    })
end)()

_G.EvadeHub_HeadlessCreate = function()
-- ==========================================
-- MAIN SERVICES
-- ==========================================
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local InsertService = game:GetService("InsertService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

-- ==========================================
-- GUI ROOT
-- ==========================================
local VisualKorless = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
VisualKorless.Name = "Fake Headless & Korblox"
VisualKorless.DisplayOrder = 99
VisualKorless.ResetOnSpawn = false
VisualKorless.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
VisualKorless.Parent = playerGui

-- ==========================================
-- STATE + CACHE
-- ==========================================
local KORBLOX_LEFT_LEG  = 139607718
local KORBLOX_RIGHT_LEG = 139607673

local Cache = {}
local cacheReady = false
local cacheFailed = false
local alive = true
local faceCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

-- Chọn chế độ: nil = chưa chọn, "Headless", "Korblox", "Both"
local SelectedMode = nil

-- ==========================================
-- HELPERS
-- ==========================================
local function getAllRigs()
	local list = {}
	local container = workspace:FindFirstChild("Rigs")
	if container then
		for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Model") and child.Name == player.Name then
				local hum = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if hum then table.insert(list, { Rig = child, Humanoid = hum }) end
			end
		end
	end
	if #list == 0 and not container then
		local character = player.Character
		local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then table.insert(list, { Rig = character, Humanoid = hum }) end
	end
	return list
end

local function detectRigType(rig, hum)
	if not rig or not hum then return nil end
	if hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then return "R15" end
	if hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then return "R6" end
	local hasUpper = rig:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg") ~= nil
	local hasR6Leg = rig:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") ~= nil
	if hasUpper then return "R15" end
	if hasR6Leg then return "R6" end
	return nil
end

local function isHeadlessApplied(rig)
	local head = rig:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if not head then return false end
	return head.Transparency >= 1
end

local function isKorbloxApplied(rig, hum)
	if hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
		local src = Cache.KorbloxR15
		if not src then return false end
		local dst = rig:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
		local srcPart = src:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
		if not dst or not srcPart then return false end
		return dst.MeshId == srcPart.MeshId and dst.MeshId ~= ""
	else
		local leg = rig:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
		if not leg then return false end
		return leg:FindFirstChild("KorbloxRight") ~= nil
	end
end

local function applyHeadlessToRig(rig)
	local head = rig:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if not head then return false end
	local face = head:FindFirstChild("face") or head:FindFirstChild("Decal")
	head.Transparency = 1
	if face then
		faceCache[rig] = face:Clone()
		face:Destroy()
	end
	return true
end

local function setMeshProp(part, prop, value)
	if pcall(function() part[prop] = value end) then return "direct" end
	if sethiddenproperty and pcall(sethiddenproperty, part, prop, value) then return "sethiddenproperty" end
	return nil
end

local function applyMeshById(part, meshId)
	local method = setMeshProp(part, "MeshId", meshId)
	if method then return method end
	local okNew, newMesh = pcall(function()
		return InsertService:CreateMeshPartAsync(meshId, Enum.CollisionFidelity.Box, Enum.RenderFidelity.Automatic)
	end)
	if okNew and newMesh then
		local okApply = pcall(function() part:ApplyMesh(newMesh) end)
		newMesh:Destroy()
		if okApply then return "ApplyMesh" end
	end
	return nil
end

local function applyLook(dst, src)
	if not (dst:IsA("MeshPart") and src:IsA("MeshPart")) then return false end
	local meshMethod = "same"
	if dst.MeshId ~= src.MeshId then meshMethod = applyMeshById(dst, src.MeshId) end
	local texMethod = "same"
	if dst.TextureID ~= src.TextureID then texMethod = setMeshProp(dst, "TextureID", src.TextureID) end
	if not meshMethod or not texMethod then return false end
	for _, child in ipairs(dst:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then child:Destroy() end
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(src:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then child:Clone().Parent = dst end
	end
	pcall(function()
		dst.Color = src.Color
		dst.Material = src.Material
		dst.Transparency = src.Transparency
	end)
	return true
end

local function applyKorbloxToRig(rig, hum)
	local isR15 = hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
	local src = isR15 and Cache.KorbloxR15 or Cache.KorbloxR6
	if not src then return false end

	if isR15 then
		local applied = 0
		for _, suffix in ipairs({ "UpperLeg", "LowerLeg", "Foot" }) do
			local dst = rig:FindFirstChild("Right" .. suffix)
			local srcPart = src:FindFirstChild("Right" .. suffix)
			if dst and srcPart and applyLook(dst, srcPart) then applied = applied + 1 end
		end
		return applied > 0
	end

	local legName = "Right Leg"
	local oldLeg = rig:FindFirstChild(legName)
	local joints = rig:FindFirstChild("Joints")
	local torso = rig:FindFirstChild("Torso")
	local hipMotor = (joints and joints:FindFirstChild(legName)) or (torso and torso:FindFirstChild("Right Hip")) or rig:FindFirstChild("Right Hip", true)
	local srcLeg = src:FindFirstChild(legName)
	if not (oldLeg and hipMotor and srcLeg) then return false end

	if oldLeg:FindFirstChild("KorbloxRight") then return true end

	local newLeg = srcLeg:Clone()
	for _, v in ipairs(newLeg:GetChildren()) do if v:IsA("Motor6D") then v:Destroy() end end

	for _, mesh in ipairs(rig:GetChildren()) do
		if mesh:IsA("CharacterMesh") and mesh.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then mesh:Destroy() end
	end
	for _, mesh in ipairs(src:GetChildren()) do
		if mesh:IsA("CharacterMesh") and mesh.BodyPart == Enum.BodyPart.RightLeg then mesh:Clone().Parent = rig end
	end

	local tag = Instance.new("StringValue")
	tag.Name = "KorbloxRight"
	tag.Parent = newLeg

	newLeg.Name = "Right Leg"
	newLeg.Parent = rig
	hipMotor.Part1 = newLeg
	newLeg.Massless = true
	newLeg.CanCollide = false
	newLeg.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0, 0, 0, 0)
	if hipMotor.Parent == oldLeg then hipMotor.Parent = newLeg end

	local colors = rig:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
	if colors then newLeg.Color = colors.RightLegColor3 end

	oldLeg:Destroy()
	return true
end

local function applyHeadlessOnly()
	local rigs = getAllRigs()
	for _, entry in ipairs(rigs) do
		if not isHeadlessApplied(entry.Rig) then
			applyHeadlessToRig(entry.Rig)
		end
	end
end

local function applyKorbloxOnly()
	local rigs = getAllRigs()
	for _, entry in ipairs(rigs) do
		if not isKorbloxApplied(entry.Rig, entry.Humanoid) then
			applyKorbloxToRig(entry.Rig, entry.Humanoid)
		end
	end
end

-- ==========================================
-- BUILD CACHE
-- ==========================================
local function buildCache()
	if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
	local waited = 0
	while alive and waited < 10 do
		if #getAllRigs() > 0 then break end
		task.wait(0.2)
		waited = waited + 0.2
	end
	if not alive then return end

	local okDesc, desc = pcall(function()
		return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(player.UserId)
	end)
	if not okDesc or not desc then
		warn("[Korless] Fetch avatar failed")
		cacheFailed = true
		return
	end

	local korbloxDesc = desc:Clone()
	korbloxDesc.LeftLeg  = KORBLOX_LEFT_LEG
	korbloxDesc.RightLeg = KORBLOX_RIGHT_LEG

	local function make(key, description, rigType)
		local model = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(description, rigType)
		model.Name = "Cache_" .. key
		Cache[key] = model
	end

	local okAll, err = pcall(function()
		make("OriR15",     desc,        Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
		make("OriR6",      desc,        Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
		make("KorbloxR15", korbloxDesc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
		make("KorbloxR6",  korbloxDesc, Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6)
	end)

	if okAll then
		cacheReady = true
		warn("[Korless] Cache ready")
	else
		warn("[Korless] Cache error: " .. tostring(err))
		cacheFailed = true
	end
end

	Panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Panel.Parent = sg
	Instance.new("UICorner", Panel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

	local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
	stroke.Thickness = 1
	stroke.Parent = Panel

	local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	Title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 36)
	Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 18)
	Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Title.Text = "Chọn chế độ áp dụng"
	Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245)
	Title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	Title.TextSize = 18
	Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	Title.Parent = Panel

	local Desc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	Desc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 18)
	Desc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 56)
	Desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	Desc.Text = "Chọn 1 trong 3 lựa chọn bên dưới"
	Desc.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 175)
	Desc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	Desc.TextSize = 12
	Desc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	Desc.Parent = Panel

	-- Hàm tạo nút
	local function makeButton(text, yPos, color, onClick)
		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 44)
		btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, yPos)
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = color
		btn.Text = text
		btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245)
		btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		btn.TextSize = 14
		btn.Parent = Panel
		Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(
					math.min(255, color.R * 255 + 20),
					math.min(255, color.G * 255 + 20),
					math.min(255, color.B * 255 + 20)
				),
			}):Play()
		end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundColor3 = color }):Play()
		end)
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			onClick()
		end)
		return btn
	end

	-- Nút 1: Headless
	makeButton("1. Headless", 90, Color3.fromRGB(60, 120, 200), function()
		SelectedMode = "Headless"
		applyHeadlessOnly()
		sg:Destroy()
		warn("[Korless] Mode: Headless only"); if _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify then _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify() end
	end)

	-- Nút 2: Korblox
	makeButton("2. Korblox", 142, Color3.fromRGB(150, 80, 200), function()
		SelectedMode = "Korblox"
		applyKorbloxOnly()
		sg:Destroy()
		warn("[Korless] Mode: Korblox only"); if _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify then _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify() end
	end)

	-- Nút 3: Both
	makeButton("3. Apply Headless + Korblox", 194, Color3.fromRGB(46, 160, 90), function()
		SelectedMode = "Both"
		applyHeadlessOnly()
		task.wait(0.15)
		applyKorbloxOnly()
		sg:Destroy()
		warn("[Korless] Mode: Headless + Korblox"); if _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify then _G.EvadeHub_ModeNotify() end
	end)
end

		local LogScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
		LogScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -8)
		LogScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 4)
		LogScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		LogScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0

		LogScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
		LogScroll.Parent = LogFrame
		local logLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		logLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
		logLayout.Parent = LogScroll

		local Credit = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		Credit.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 16)
		Credit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, 248)
		Credit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		Credit.Text = "Script remake: 4.1.2"
		Credit.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 125)
		Credit.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		Credit.TextSize = 10
		Credit.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		Credit.Parent = Loading

		loadingGui = Loading

		local MAX_LOG = 9
		local logLines = {}

		local function pushLog(text, color)
			local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12)
			l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			l.Text = "▸ " .. tostring(text)
			l.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(130, 180, 130)
			l.Font = Enum.Font.Code
			l.TextSize = 10
			l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			l.Parent = LogScroll
			table.insert(logLines, l)
			if #logLines > MAX_LOG then
				local old = table.remove(logLines, 1)
				if old then old:Destroy() end
			end
			task.defer(function()
				if LogScroll and LogScroll.Parent then
					LogScroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, logLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y - LogScroll.AbsoluteSize.Y))
				end
			end)
		end

		local function setProgress(percent, text)
			PercentLabel.Text = string.format("%d%%", math.floor(percent * 100))
			TweenService:Create(BarFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				Size = UDim2.new(percent, 0, 1, 0),
			}):Play()
			if text then
				TweenService:Create(Status, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
				task.delay(0.2, function()
					Status.Text = text
					TweenService:Create(Status, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { TextTransparency = 0 }):Play()
				end)
			end
		end

		return {
			Loading = Loading,
			PercentLabel = PercentLabel,
			BarFill = BarFill,
			Status = Status,
			pushLog = pushLog,
			setProgress = setProgress,
		}
	end

		{ "state: State table ready ✓" },

		{ "cache: builder thread spawned ✓" },

		{ "avatar: waiting character..." },
		{ "avatar: character found ✓" },
		{ "avatar: GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId" },

		{ "avatar: cloning description ✓" },

		{ "model: building KorbloxR6 ✓" },

		{ "rig: scanning Workspace.Rigs" },
		{ "rig: matching player.Name" },
		{ "rig: checking Humanoid" },
		{ "rig: character validated ✓" },
		{ "rigtype: detectRigType() ready" },
		{ "rigtype: Humanoid.RigType check ✓" },
		{ "rigtype: R15 part fallback ✓" },
		{ "rigtype: R6 part fallback ✓" },
		{ "mesh: setMeshProp() ready" },
		{ "mesh: applyMeshById() ready" },
		{ "mesh: applyLook() ready" },
		{ "mesh: all mesh handlers ready ✓" },
