-- ==========================================================
-- SWAP ENGINE (Lớp 1 -> Lớp 2 -> Lớp 3 -> Lớp 4 ConfirmEmote)
-- ==========================================================
local function NewSwap(config)
    local Swap = {}
    local CATEGORY = config.Category
    local PATHS = config.Paths or {}
    local NOUN = config.Noun or "item"
    local entries, byInstance, swapped, originals = {}, {}, {}, {}

    local function SpacedName(name) return (name:gsub("(%l)(%u)", "%1 %2")) end
    local loaded, source = false, "none"

    local function AddEntry(instance, id)
        if byInstance[instance] then return end
        local info
        local ok, result = pcall(require, instance)
        if ok and type(result) == "table" then info = result end
        local display = info and info.AppearanceInfo and info.AppearanceInfo.Name
        local header = info and info.UIInfo and info.UIInfo.Header
        local entry = { Name = instance.Name, Display = type(display) == "string" and display or SpacedName(instance.Name), Header = type(header) == "string" and header or "Other", Id = id, Instance = instance }
        table.insert(entries, entry)
        byInstance[instance] = entry
    end

    local function ResolvePath(path)
        local current = game
        for _, name in ipairs(string.split(path, ".")) do
            if name == "ReplicatedStorage" then current = ReplicatedStorage
            else current = current:FindFirstChild(name) end
            if not current then return nil end
        end
        return current
    end

    -- LỚP 1: Registry
    local function LoadFromRegistry()
        local items = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local registryModule = items and items:FindFirstChild("Registry")
        if not registryModule then return false end
        local ok, Registry = pcall(require, registryModule)
        if not ok or type(Registry) ~= "table" or type(Registry.GetByCategory) ~= "function" then return false end
        local okList, list = pcall(Registry.GetByCategory, CATEGORY)
        if not okList or type(list) ~= "table" then return false end
        for id, record in pairs(list) do local module = type(record) == "table" and record.Module; if typeof(module) == "Instance" then AddEntry(module, id) end end
        return #entries > 0
    end

    -- LỚP 2: GameFolder (DaftarPath)
    local function LoadFromPaths()
        for _, path in ipairs(PATHS) do
            local instance = ResolvePath(path)
            if instance then AddEntry(instance) end
        end
    end

    -- LỚP 3: Lấy danh sách quét được (Không ghi đè)
    local function GetScanList()
        local items = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items")
        if not items then return {} end
        local found = {}
        for _, descendant in ipairs(items:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("ModuleScript") then
                local inCategory = false
                local parent = descendant.Parent
                while parent and parent ~= items do 
                    if parent:IsA("Folder") and parent.Name == CATEGORY then 
                        inCategory = true; break 
                    end
                    parent = parent.Parent 
                end
                if inCategory then table.insert(found, descendant) end
            end
        end
        return found
    end

    -- LỚP 4: ConfirmEmote (Đối chiếu Lớp 2 và Lớp 3)
    local function ConfirmEmote()
        local scannedList = GetScanList()
        local addedCount = 0
        local currentInstances = {}
        
        for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
            currentInstances[entry.Instance] = true
        end
        
        for _, instance in ipairs(scannedList) do
            if not currentInstances[instance] then
                AddEntry(instance)
                addedCount = addedCount + 1
            end
        end
        
        print(string.format("[ConfirmEmote] Phát hiện thêm %d emote bị thiếu từ GameFolder và đã tự động bổ sung.", addedCount))
        source = "paths + scan (Confirmed)"
    end

    function Swap.Load()
        if loaded then return #entries end
        loaded = true
        if not ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Items") then pcall(function() ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Items", 8) end) end
        
        -- LỚP 1
        if LoadFromRegistry() then 
            source = "registry"
            print("[Load] Tải thành công bằng Registry (Lớp 1).")
        else
            table.clear(entries); table.clear(byInstance)
            
            -- LỚP 2
            LoadFromPaths()
            print(string.format("[Load] Lớp 1 thất bại. Đã tải %d emote từ GameFolder (Lớp 2).", #entries))
            
            -- LỚP 3 + 4
            ConfirmEmote() 
            
            if #entries == 0 then 
                source = "scan" 
                print("[Load] Cả Lớp 2 và Lớp 3 đều không tìm thấy gì.")
            end
        end
        
        table.sort(entries, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.Display) < string.lower(b.Display) end)
        return #entries
    end

    function Swap.GetSource() return source end
    function Swap.Reload() loaded = false; table.clear(entries); table.clear(byInstance); return Swap.Load() end
    function Swap.GetEntries() return entries end
    function Swap.GetPartner(entry) local partner = entry and swapped[entry.Instance]; return partner and byInstance[partner] or nil end
    function Swap.GetState(base, target)
        if not base or not target or base == target then return 1 end
        if swapped[base.Instance] == target.Instance then return 3 end
        if swapped[base.Instance] ~= nil or swapped[target.Instance] ~= nil then return 1 end
        return 2
    end

    local function CacheOriginal(item) if not originals[item] then originals[item] = {}; for _, child in ipairs(item:GetChildren()) do table.insert(originals[item], child:Clone()) end end end
    local function InjectContents(targetItem, sourceItem) for _, child in ipairs(targetItem:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end; for _, child in ipairs(originals[sourceItem]) do child.Clone().Parent = targetItem end end

    function Swap.Swap(base, target)
        if Swap.GetState(base, target) ~= 2 then return false, "Pick valid Base and Target." end
        local ok = pcall(function() CacheOriginal(base.Instance); CacheOriginal(target.Instance); InjectContents(base.Instance, target.Instance); InjectContents(target.Instance, base.Instance) end)
        if not ok then return false, "Swap failed." end
        swapped[base.Instance] = target.Instance; swapped[target.Instance] = base.Instance
        return true, base.Display .. " ⇌ " .. target.Display
    end

    function Swap.Revert(base, target)
        if Swap.GetState(base, target) ~= 3 then return false, "Not swapped." end
        local ok = pcall(function() InjectContents(base.Instance, base.Instance); InjectContents(target.Instance, target.Instance) end)
        if not ok then return false, "Revert failed." end
        swapped[base.Instance] = nil; swapped[target.Instance] = nil
        return true, "Restored."
    end

    function Swap.RevertAll()
        local restored, touched = 0, {}
        for instance, partner in pairs(table.clone(swapped)) do
            if swapped[instance] == partner then
                local entry, other = byInstance[instance], byInstance[partner]
                if entry and other and Swap.Revert(entry, other) then restored = restored + 1; table.insert(touched, entry); table.insert(touched, other) end
            end
        end
        return restored, touched
    end
    return Swap
end

-- ==========================================================
-- PREVIEW (EMOTE)
-- ==========================================================
local Preview = {}
do
    local TRACK_TIMEOUT = 5
    local SETTLE_FRAMES = 2
    local POSE_TIME = 0.5
    local MAX_HOSTS = 3
    local COLLECT_GAP = 0.12
    local POSE_FILE = (_G.EvadeHub_ConfigRoot or "Aur_Meow Configs") .. "/FakeEmote_Poses.json"
    local POSE_VERSION = 1

    local alive = true
    local hostGui = nil
    local hosts = {}
    local cache, inflight, inflightPose = {}, {}, {}
    local poses = nil
    local poseToken = 0

    local function HeartbeatWait(frames) for _ = 1, frames or 1 do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end end
    local function IsR15() local ok, rt = pcall(function() return Players.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RigType") end); if ok and rt ~= nil then return rt == "R15" end; return true end
    local function AssetItem(name) local assets = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Assets"); local items = assets and assets:FindFirstChild("Items"); return items and items:FindFirstChild(name) end
    local function RigTemplate(r15) return AssetItem(r15 and "VisualRigR15Emote" or "VisualRigClassic") end

    function Preview.Available() return RigTemplate(IsR15()) ~= nil end

    local function LoadPoses()
        if poses then return poses end
        poses = {}
        if readfile and isfile and isfile(POSE_FILE) then
            local ok, decoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(POSE_FILE)) end)
            if ok and type(decoded) == "table" and decoded.Version == POSE_VERSION and type(decoded.Poses) == "table" then poses = decoded.Poses end
        end
        return poses
    end

    local function FlushPoses()
        poseToken = poseToken + 1
        if not (poses and writefile) then return end
        pcall(function() writefile(POSE_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode({ Version = POSE_VERSION, Poses = poses })) end)
    end

    local function SavePosesSoon() poseToken = poseToken + 1; local mine = poseToken; task.delay(3, function() if mine == poseToken then FlushPoses() end end) end

    local function JointPath(rig, joint)
        local names = {}
        local node = joint
        while node and node ~= rig do table.insert(names, 1, node.Name); node = node.Parent end
        return table.concat(names, "/")
    end

    local IDENTITY = { 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1 }
    local function IsIdentity(components) for i, v in ipairs(IDENTITY) do if math.abs((components[i] or 0) - v) > 0.0001 then return false end end; return true end
    local function CapturePose(rig) local p = {}; for _, d in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("Motor6D") then local c = { d.Transform:GetComponents() }; if not IsIdentity(c) then p[JointPath(rig, d)] = c end end end; return p end
    local function HasMotion(rig) for _, d in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("Motor6D") and not IsIdentity({ d.Transform:GetComponents() }) then return true end end; return false end
    local function ApplyPose(rig, pose) for _, d in ipairs(rig:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("Motor6D") then local c = pose[JointPath(rig, d)]; d.Transform = c and CFrame.new(table.unpack(c)) or CFrame.new() end end end

    local function EnsureGui()
        if hostGui and hostGui.Parent then return hostGui end
        local parent; pcall(function() parent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end); if not parent then parent = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        hostGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui"); hostGui.Name = "AurMeowPreview"; hostGui.ResetOnSpawn = false; hostGui.DisplayOrder = -10; hostGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true; hostGui.Parent = parent
        table.clear(hosts); return hostGui
    end

    local function AcquireHost()
        while alive do
            local gui = EnsureGui()
            for _, e in ipairs(hosts) do if not e.InUse and e.Frame.Parent then e.InUse = true; return e end end
            if #hosts < MAX_HOSTS then
                local f = Instance.new("ViewportFrame"); f.Name = "Host" .. (#hosts + 1); f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8,8); f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0,0); f.BackgroundTransparency = 1; f.ImageTransparency = 0.98; f.Parent = gui
                local e = { Frame = f, InUse = true }; table.insert(hosts, e); return e
            end
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function Variant(item) local s = item:FindFirstChild("Selection"); if s then return s:GetChildren()[1] or item end; return item end
    local function FindEmoteAnimation(item, r15)
        if item:FindFirstChild("Animations") or item:FindFirstChild("Selection") then
            local c = item:FindFirstChild("Selection") and Variant(item) or item
            local a = c:FindFirstChild("Animations")
            local f = a and a:FindFirstChild(r15 and "R15" or "R6")
            local an = f and f:FindFirstChild("Animation")
            if an and an.AnimationId ~= "" then return an end
            return nil
        end
        return item:FindFirstChild(r15 and "Animation" or "AnimationClassic")
    end
    local function PoseJobs(entry, r15)
        local jobs = {}
        local an = FindEmoteAnimation(entry.Instance, r15)
        if an then table.insert(jobs, { Name = "Rig", Animation = an, Prefix = r15 and "R15:" or "R6:", Template = RigTemplate(r15) }) end
        return jobs
    end
    local function PoseKey(job) return job.Prefix .. tostring(job.Animation.AnimationId) end
    local function RootOffset(model)
        local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not (root and root:IsA("BasePart")) then return 0 end
        local lowest = math.huge
        for _, name in ipairs({ "LeftFoot", "RightFoot", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }) do
            local p = model:FindFirstChild(name)
            if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
                local h = p.Size / 2
                for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do for _, sy in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do for _, sz in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do lowest = math.min(lowest, (p.CFrame * Vector3.new(sx * h.X, sy * h.Y, sz * h.Z)).Y) end end end
            end
        end
        if lowest == math.huge then return 0 end
        return root.Position.Y - lowest - 2.75
    end
    local function AttachProps(rig, item, r15)
        local t = Variant(item):FindFirstChild(r15 and "Character" or "CharacterClassic"); if not t then return end
        local ok, cp = pcall(function() return t:Clone() end); if not ok or not cp then return end
        local em = cp:FindFirstChild("EmoteModel"); if not em then cp:Destroy(); return end
        local pr = rig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) or rig.PrimaryPart
        local tg = rig:FindFirstChild("R15Visual") or rig
        local off = CFrame.new(0, RootOffset(cp), 0)
        em.Parent = tg
        for _, ch in ipairs(em:GetChildren()) do
            if (ch:IsA("Motor6D") or ch:IsA("Weld")) and ch.Part0 and ch.Part0.Parent == cp then
                if ch.Part0.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then ch.Part0 = pr; ch.C0 = off * ch.C0 else ch.Part0 = tg:FindFirstChild(ch.Part0.Name) end
            end
        end
        cp:Destroy()
    end
    local function GetAnimator(rig) local c = rig:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true) or rig:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid", true); if not c then return nil end; return c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or (function() local a = Instance.new("Animator"); a.Parent = c; return a end)() end

    local function SolveJoints(container, solved)
        if not solved then local root = container:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) or container.PrimaryPart; if not root then return false end; solved = { [root] = true } end
        local joints = {}
        for _, d in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("Weld") then if d.Part0 and d.Part1 then table.insert(joints, d) end end end
        local progress = true
        while progress do
            progress = false
            for _, j in ipairs(joints) do
                local p0, p1 = j.Part0, j.Part1; local t = j:IsA("Motor6D") and j.Transform or CFrame.new()
                if solved[p0] and not solved[p1] then p1.CFrame = p0.CFrame * j.C0 * t * j.C1:Inverse(); solved[p1] = true; progress = true
                elseif solved[p1] and not solved[p0] then p0.CFrame = p1.CFrame * j.C1 * t:Inverse() * j.C0:Inverse(); solved[p0] = true; progress = true end
            end
        end
        return true
    end

    local function Orient(model)
        local pr = model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true); if not pr then return end
        local inv = pr.CFrame:Inverse(); local bs = CFrame.new(pr.Position)
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.CFrame = bs * (inv * d.CFrame) end end
    end

    local STRIP_CLASSES = { "Motor6D", "Weld", "ManualWeld", "WeldConstraint", "Snap", "Constraint", "ParticleEmitter", "Trail", "Beam", "Fire", "Smoke", "Sparkles", "Explosion", "Highlight", "ForceField", "PointLight", "SpotLight", "SurfaceLight", "WrapTarget", "WrapLayer", "AnimationController", "Humanoid", "Animator", "LuaSourceContainer", "Sound" }
    local function ShouldStrip(i) for _, c in ipairs(STRIP_CLASSES) do if i:IsA(c) then return true end end; return i:IsA("Attachment") and not i:IsA("Bone") end
    local function Freeze(source, name)
        local cp = source:Clone(); cp.Name = name
        for _, d in ipairs(cp:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Anchored = true; d.CastShadow = false; d.CanCollide = false; d.CanQuery = false; d.CanTouch = false end end
        for _, d in ipairs(cp:GetDescendants()) do if ShouldStrip(d) then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end end
        Orient(cp); return cp
    end

    local function BuildEmoteModel(entry, r15, poseOf)
        local t = RigTemplate(r15); if not t then return nil, "rig not found" end
        local rig = t:Clone(); AttachProps(rig, entry.Instance, r15)
        if poseOf.Rig then ApplyPose(rig, poseOf.Rig) end
        SolveJoints(rig)
        local m = Freeze(rig, entry.Display); pcall(function() rig:Destroy() end); return m
    end

    local function EnsurePose(job)
        local key = PoseKey(job); local store = LoadPoses(); if store[key] then return store[key] end
        while inflightPose[key] do task.wait(0.03); if not alive then return nil, "unloaded" end end
        if store[key] then return store[key] end; inflightPose[key] = true
        local hostE = AcquireHost(); if not hostE then inflightPose[key] = nil; return nil, "unloaded" end
        local pose, fail, world
        local ok = pcall(function()
            world = Instance.new("WorldModel"); world.Parent = hostE.Frame
            local rig = job.Template:Clone(); rig.Parent = world
            local anim = GetAnimator(rig); if not anim then fail = "no animator"; return end
            local tr = anim:LoadAnimation(job.Animation); tr.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3; tr:Play(0); tr:AdjustSpeed(0)
            local limit = os.clock() + TRACK_TIMEOUT
            while alive and os.clock() < limit and tr.Length <= 0 and not HasMotion(rig) do HeartbeatWait(1) end
            if tr.Length <= 0 and not HasMotion(rig) then fail = "anim load failed"; return end
            tr.TimePosition = math.min(POSE_TIME, tr.Length); HeartbeatWait(SETTLE_FRAMES); pose = CapturePose(rig)
        end)
        if world then pcall(function() world:Destroy() end) end
        hostE.InUse = false; inflightPose[key] = nil
        if not ok then return nil, "error" end; if not pose then return nil, fail end
        store[key] = pose; SavePosesSoon(); return pose
    end

    function Preview.Get(entry) return entry and cache[entry.Instance] or nil end
    function Preview.PoseKnown(entry) local store = LoadPoses(); for _, j in ipairs(PoseJobs(entry, IsR15())) do if not store[PoseKey(j)] then return false end end; return true end
    function Preview.Invalidate(entry) local m = entry and cache[entry.Instance]; if m then cache[entry.Instance] = nil; pcall(function() m:Destroy() end) end end
    function Preview.Preload(entries) local r15 = IsR15(); local store = LoadPoses(); local list = {}; for _, e in ipairs(entries) do for _, j in ipairs(PoseJobs(e, r15)) do if not store[PoseKey(j)] then table.insert(list, j.Animation) end end end; if #list == 0 then return end; task.spawn(function() pcall(function() game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync(list) end) end) end

    function Preview.Bake(entry)
        if not alive then return nil, "unloaded" end
        local cached = cache[entry.Instance]; if cached then return cached end
        local r15 = IsR15()
        while inflight[entry.Instance] do task.wait(0.03); if not alive then return nil, "unloaded" end end
        cached = cache[entry.Instance]; if cached then return cached end; inflight[entry.Instance] = true
        local model, fail
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local poseOf = {}
            for _, j in ipairs(PoseJobs(entry, r15)) do local p, r = EnsurePose(j); if not p then fail = r; return end; poseOf[j.Name] = p end
            model, fail = BuildEmoteModel(entry, r15, poseOf)
        end)
        inflight[entry.Instance] = nil
        if not ok then return nil, "error" end; if not model then return nil, fail end
        if not alive then model:Destroy(); return nil, "unloaded" end
        cache[entry.Instance] = model; return model
    end

    function Preview.Collect(entries)
        local r15 = IsR15(); local store = LoadPoses(); local cnt = 0
        for _, e in ipairs(entries) do if not alive then break end; for _, j in ipairs(PoseJobs(e, r15)) do if not store[PoseKey(j)] then if EnsurePose(j) then cnt = cnt + 1 end; task.wait(COLLECT_GAP) end end end
        return cnt
    end
end

-- ==========================================================
-- PAGE BUILDER & UI LOGIC (EMOTE)
-- ==========================================================
local PageArea = CreateUI()
local PageLayout = BuildPageLayout(PageArea)
local PaneHolder, StatusLabel = PageLayout.PaneHolder, PageLayout.StatusLabel

local LoadingBtn = MakeButton(PageLayout.BottomBar, "Loading Emote", false, 130)
LoadingBtn.Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5); LoadingBtn.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)
local RevertAllBtn = MakeButton(PageLayout.BottomBar, "Remove all", false, 140)
RevertAllBtn.Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5); RevertAllBtn.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -154, 0.5, 0)
local SwapBtn = MakeButton(PageLayout.BottomBar, "Change Emote", true, 130)
SwapBtn.Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5); SwapBtn.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -304, 0.5, 0)

local HEADER_ORDER = { "All", "Normal", "Gamepasses", "Halloween", "Winter Holidays", "Summer", "Spring", "Valentines", "Event", "Special", "Other" }
local function BuildGroups(entries)
    local byHeader = { ["All"] = {} }
    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
        table.insert(byHeader["All"], entry)
        local path = entry.Instance:GetFullName()
        local header = "Other"
        if string.find(path, "BaseItems.Emotes") or string.find(path, "Base.DailyShop") or string.find(path, "Base.LevelShop") or string.find(path, "Base.NewShop") or string.find(path, "Base.Special") then header = "Normal"
        elseif string.find(path, "Gamepass") then header = "Gamepasses"
        elseif string.find(path, "Halloween") or string.find(path, "Hlwn") then header = "Halloween"
        elseif string.find(path, "Xmas") or string.find(path, "XMAS") then header = "Winter Holidays"
        elseif string.find(path, "StPatricks") or string.find(path, "Valentines") then header = "Valentines"
        elseif string.find(path, "Summer") then header = "Summer"
        elseif string.find(path, "Spring") then header = "Spring"
        elseif string.find(path, "Collab") or string.find(path, "Integration") then header = "Special"
        end
        byHeader[header] = byHeader[header] or {}
        table.insert(byHeader[header], entry)
    end
    local order, seen = {}, {}
    for _, header in ipairs(HEADER_ORDER) do if byHeader[header] and #byHeader[header] > 0 then table.insert(order, header); seen[header] = true end end
    local rest = {}
    for header, list in pairs(byHeader) do if not seen[header] and #list > 0 then table.insert(rest, header) end end
    table.sort(rest)
    for _, header in ipairs(rest) do table.insert(order, header) end
    local groups = {}
    for _, header in ipairs(order) do table.insert(groups, { Title = header, Entries = byHeader[header] }) end
    return groups
end

local LOOK_DIR = Vector3.new(4.25, 1.7, -8.5).Unit
local CELL_H, TITLE_H, GAP = 108, 20, 6

local function BuildEmoteUI(Swap)
    if Swap.Load() == 0 then
        Label(PaneHolder, { Text = "No emotes found.", TextWrapped = true, TextColor3 = THEME.SubText, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
        SwapBtn.Frame.Visible = false; RevertAllBtn.Frame.Visible = false; LoadingBtn.Frame.Visible = false
        return
    end
    local groups = BuildGroups(Swap.GetEntries())
    local sides, failedPrev = {}, {}
    local curMusic = nil
    local statusToken = 0
    local Refresh, RefreshVisible

    local function TitleOf(entry) local p = Swap.GetPartner(entry); return p and (entry.Display .. " (" .. p.Display .. ")") or entry.Display end
    local function ShowHint() local b, t = sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected; local st = Swap.GetState(b, t); if st == 3 then StatusLabel.Text = b.Display .. " looks like " .. t.Display .. " (swapped)"; elseif st == 2 then StatusLabel.Text = "Ready: " .. b.Display .. " will look like " .. t.Display; else StatusLabel.Text = "Pick a Base and a Target emote." end; StatusLabel.TextColor3 = THEME.SubText; if PageLayout.StatusDot then PageLayout.StatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Accent end end
    local function SetStatus(text, color) statusToken = statusToken + 1; local mine = statusToken; StatusLabel.Text = text; StatusLabel.TextColor3 = color or THEME.SubText; if PageLayout.StatusDot then PageLayout.StatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = color or THEME.Accent end; task.delay(15.5, function() if mine == statusToken then ShowHint() end end) end
    local function PreviewsOn() return Preview.Available() end
    local function AttachView(tile)
        if tile.View then return end
        local m = Preview.Get(tile.Entry); if not m then return end
        local v = New("ViewportFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190), LightColor = Color3.new(1,1,1) }, tile.Thumb)
        local cp = m:Clone(); cp.Parent = v
        local cf, sz = cp:GetBoundingBox(); local rad = math.max(sz.X, sz.Y, sz.Z) / 2; local dist = rad / math.tan(math.rad(15)) * 1.1
        local cam = New("Camera", { FieldOfView = 30 }, v); cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cf.Position + LOOK_DIR * dist, cf.Position); v.CurrentCamera = cam
        tile.View = v; tile.Placeholder.Visible = false
    end
    local function DetachView(tile) if tile.View then tile.View:Destroy(); tile.View = nil end; tile.Placeholder.Visible = true end
    local function PaintTile(side, tile, hov)
        local on = side.Selected == tile.Entry
        local sw = Swap.GetPartner(tile.Entry) ~= nil
        tile.Button.BackgroundColor3 = sw and THEME.Swapped or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        tile.Button.BackgroundTransparency = sw and (on and 0.68 or (hov and 0.74 or 0.8)) or (on and 0.82 or (hov and 0.9 or 0.95))
        tile.Badge.Visible = on
        tile.Stroke.Color = on and THEME.Accent or (sw and THEME.Swapped or THEME.Stroke)
        tile.Stroke.Transparency = on and 0.2 or (sw and 0.35 or (hov and 0.7 or THEME.StrokeAlpha))
        tile.Stroke.Thickness = (on or sw) and 1.5 or 1
        if tile.MusicBtn then tile.MusicBtn.Visible = on end
    end
    local function StopMusic() if curMusic then curMusic:Destroy(); curMusic = nil end end
    local function PlayMusic(entry) StopMusic(); local function FindSound(inst) if inst:IsA("Sound") and inst.SoundId ~= "" then return inst end; for _, c in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do local f = FindSound(c); if f then return f end end; return nil end; local snd = FindSound(entry.Instance); if snd then curMusic = snd:Clone(); curMusic.Parent = SoundService; curMusic.Looped = false; curMusic:Play() else SetStatus("No sound.", THEME.Danger) end end
    local function Pick(side, entry) side.Selected = (side.Selected ~= entry) and entry or nil; if not side.Selected then StopMusic() end; Refresh() end

    local function MakeTile(side, entry)
        local btn = New("TextButton", { Name = entry.Name, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.95 }, side.Scroll)
        Round(btn, 10)
        local st = Stroke(btn, THEME.StrokeAlpha)
        local thumb = New("Frame", { Name = "Thumb", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, CELL_H - 10 - TITLE_H), BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Field, BackgroundTransparency = THEME.FieldAlpha, ClipsDescendants = true, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, btn)
        Round(thumb, 7)
        local ph = Label(thumb, { Text = "…", TextSize = 18, TextColor3 = THEME.Muted, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
        local nm = Label(btn, { Text = TitleOf(entry), Font = FONT_LABEL, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, CELL_H - TITLE_H - 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, TITLE_H) })
        local bd = New("Frame", { Name = "Badge", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -9, 0, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 3 }, btn)
        Round(bd, 9); CheckGlyph(bd, 12, THEME.OnAccent)
        local mBtn = New("TextButton", { Name = "MusicBtn", Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Accent2, BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Visible = false, ZIndex = 4 }, btn)
        Round(mBtn, 6); PlayGlyph(mBtn, 14, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
        mBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() PlayMusic(entry); SetStatus("Playing " .. entry.Display, THEME.Success) end)
        local tile = { Entry = entry, Button = btn, Stroke = st, Thumb = thumb, Placeholder = ph, Name = nm, Badge = bd, MusicBtn = mBtn }
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() PaintTile(side, tile, true) end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() PaintTile(side, tile, false) end)
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Pick(side, entry) end)
        return tile
    end

    local function DestroyTile(tile) DetachView(tile); tile.Button:Destroy() end
    local function IsNear(side, tile) local s = side.Scroll; local top = s.CanvasPosition.Y; local bot = top + s.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y; local y = tile.Button.AbsolutePosition.Y - s.AbsolutePosition.Y + top; local m = 120; return y + tile.Button.AbsoluteSize.Y >= top - m and y <= bot + m end
    local function UpdateVisible(side) for _, t in ipairs(side.TileList) do if IsNear(side, t) then AttachView(t) elseif t.View then DetachView(t) end end end
    local function OnBaked(entry) for _, s in pairs(sides) do local t = s.TileByEntry[entry]; if t and IsNear(s, t) then AttachView(t) end end end
    local WORKERS = 3
    local function Bake(side)
        if not PreviewsOn() then return end
        side.BakeToken = side.BakeToken + 1; local mine = side.BakeToken
        local pending = {}
        for _, t in ipairs(side.TileList) do if IsNear(side, t) and not Preview.Get(t.Entry) and not failedPrev[t.Entry] then table.insert(pending, t.Entry) end end
        if #pending == 0 then return end
        Preview.Preload(pending)
        local nextIdx = 1
        local function Worker()
            while mine == side.BakeToken and nextIdx <= #pending do
                local e = pending[nextIdx]; nextIdx = nextIdx + 1
                local m, r = Preview.Bake(e)
                if m then OnBaked(e) else failedPrev[e] = true end
            end
        end
        for _ = 1, math.min(WORKERS, #pending) do task.spawn(Worker) end
    end
    RefreshVisible = function(side) UpdateVisible(side); Bake(side) end

    local function RenderSide(side)
        local other = side == sides.Base and sides.Target or sides.Base
        local q = string.lower(side.Query)
        local ents = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(groups[side.Group].Entries) do if e ~= other.Selected and (q == "" or string.find(string.lower(e.Display), q, 1, true)) then table.insert(ents, e) end end
        local list, map = {}, {}
        for i, e in ipairs(ents) do local tile = side.TileByEntry[e] or MakeTile(side, e); tile.Button.LayoutOrder = i; tile.Name.Text = TitleOf(e); list[i] = tile; map[e] = tile end
        for e, tile in pairs(side.TileByEntry) do if not map[e] then DestroyTile(tile) end end
        side.TileList, side.TileByEntry = list, map
        for _, tile in ipairs(list) do PaintTile(side, tile, false) end
        local sel = side.Selected
        side.SelectedLabel.Text = sel and TitleOf(sel) or "None selected"
        side.SelectedLabel.TextColor3 = sel and THEME.Text or THEME.Muted
    end

    Refresh = function()
        local b, t = sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected
        if t == b then sides.Target.Selected = nil end
        if b == t then sides.Base.Selected = nil end
        RenderSide(sides.Base); RenderSide(sides.Target)
        local st = Swap.GetState(b, t)
        SwapBtn.Label.Text = st == 3 and "Revert" or "Change Emote"
        SetEnabled(SwapBtn, st ~= 1)
        ShowHint()
        for _, s in pairs(sides) do task.defer(function() RefreshVisible(s) end) end
    end

    local function Field(parent, pos, size, class, props)
        local m = { BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Field, BackgroundTransparency = THEME.FieldAlpha, Position = pos, Size = size }
        for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do m[k] = v end
        local f = New(class, m, parent); Round(f, 9); Stroke(f, 0.9); return f
    end

    local function NewSide(name, title)
        local side = { Name = name, Group = 1, Query = "", Selected = nil, TileList = {}, TileByEntry = {}, BakeToken = 0 }
        sides[name] = side
        local fr = New("Frame", { Name = name, LayoutOrder = name == "Base" and 1 or 2, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = THEME.Card, BackgroundTransparency = THEME.CardAlpha, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, PaneHolder)
        Round(fr, 12); Stroke(fr, 0.9)
        local inr = New("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, fr)
        New("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 12), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 12) }, inr)
        
        -- FIX: Thêm TextTruncate để không bị đè chữ
        Label(inr, { Text = title, Font = FONT_TITLE, TextSize = 15, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 20), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
        
        side.SelectedLabel = Label(inr, { Text = "None selected", TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = THEME.Muted, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 20) })
        local catBtn = Field(inr, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false })
        local catLbl = Label(catBtn, { Text = groups[1].Title, Font = FONT_LABEL, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 1, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
        local chv = New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, catBtn)
        ChevronGlyph(chv, 14, THEME.SubText)
        local srch = Field(inr, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 64), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), "TextBox", { Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search emote...", PlaceholderColor3 = THEME.Muted, TextColor3 = THEME.Text, Font = FONT_BODY, TextSize = 13, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ClearTextOnFocus = false })
        New("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10) }, srch)
        side.Scroll = New("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 104), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -104), ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = THEME.Muted, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y }, inr)
        New("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 3), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 3), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, side.Scroll)
        ResponsiveGrid(side.Scroll, GAP, CELL_H, 88, function() RefreshVisible(side) end)
        
        local DRAWER_Y = 62
        local drawerHeight = math.min(#groups * 36 + 10, 150)
        local drawer = Field(inr, UDim2.fromOffset(0, DRAWER_Y), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, drawerHeight), "Frame", { ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false })
        local list = New("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = THEME.Muted, AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y }, drawer)
        New("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4) }, list)
        New("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, list)

        local rows = {}
        local function PaintRow(idx, hov)
            local r = rows[idx]; local on = idx == side.Group
            r.Button.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.78 or (hov and 0.92 or 1)
            r.Check.Visible = on
            r.Stroke.Color = on and THEME.Accent or THEME.Stroke
            r.Stroke.Transparency = on and 0.45 or (hov and 0.78 or THEME.StrokeAlpha)
        end
        local drawerOpen = false
        local function Layout(open)
            drawerOpen = open; drawer.Visible = open
            Tween(chv, { Rotation = open and 180 or 0 }, 0.2)
            local sY = open and (DRAWER_Y + drawerHeight + 6) or 64
            local gY = sY + 40
            Tween(srch, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, sY) }, 0.2)
            Tween(side.Scroll, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, gY), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -gY) }, 0.2)
            task.delay(0.25, function() RefreshVisible(side) end)
        end

        for i, g in ipairs(groups) do
            local b = New("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32) }, list)
            Round(b, 8); Gradient(b, 0, AURORA); local st = Stroke(b, THEME.StrokeAlpha)
            Label(b, { Text = g.Title, Font = FONT_LABEL, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
            Label(b, { Text = tostring(#g.Entries), TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = THEME.Muted, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, 0) })
            local cs = New("Frame", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, b)
            CheckGlyph(cs, 14, THEME.Accent)
            rows[i] = { Button = b, Stroke = st, Check = cs }
            b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() PaintRow(i, true) end)
            b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() PaintRow(i, false) end)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() side.Group = i; catLbl.Text = g.Title; for ri = 1, #rows do PaintRow(ri, false) end; side.Scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero; for e in pairs(failedPrev) do failedPrev[e] = nil end; Layout(false); Refresh() end)
        end
        for ri = 1, #rows do PaintRow(ri, false) end
        catBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Layout(not drawerOpen) end)
        srch:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() side.Query = srch.Text; side.Scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero; Refresh() end)
        local scrollToken = 0
        side.Scroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function() scrollToken = scrollToken + 1; local mine = scrollToken; task.delay(0.08, function() if mine == scrollToken then RefreshVisible(side) end end) end)
    end

    NewSide("Base", "Base Emote")
    NewSide("Target", "Target Emote")

    local function Forget(entries) for _, e in ipairs(entries) do Preview.Invalidate(e); failedPrev[e] = nil; for _, s in pairs(sides) do local t = s.TileByEntry[e]; if t then DetachView(t) end end end end

    SwapBtn.Hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local b, t = sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected
        local st = Swap.GetState(b, t)
        if st == 1 then SetStatus("Pick valid emotes.", THEME.Danger); return end
        local pair = { b, t }
        local ok, msg
        if st == 2 then
            ok, msg = Swap.Swap(b, t)
            if ok then SetStatus("✔️ The emote animation has been changed", THEME.Success) else SetStatus(msg, THEME.Danger) end
        else
            ok, msg = Swap.Revert(b, t)
            if ok then SetStatus("Restored.", THEME.Success) else SetStatus(msg, THEME.Danger) end
        end
        if ok then Forget(pair) end
        sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected = nil, nil
        StopMusic(); Refresh()
    end)

    RevertAllBtn.Hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local cnt, touched = Swap.RevertAll()
        Forget(touched); sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected = nil, nil; StopMusic(); Refresh()
        SetStatus(cnt > 0 and "Restored " .. cnt .. " pair(s)." or "Nothing to restore.", cnt > 0 and THEME.Success or THEME.SubText)
    end)

    -- FIX: Không xóa toàn bộ UI nữa, chỉ reset và render lại danh sách
    LoadingBtn.Hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        SetStatus("Loading...", THEME.Accent); task.wait(0.5)
        local ok, cnt = pcall(function() return Swap.Reload() end)
        if ok then
            SetStatus("Found " .. cnt .. " emotes.", THEME.Success)
            for _, s in pairs(sides) do 
                s.Group = 1
                s.Selected = nil
                s.Query = ""
                s.Scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
                for _, tile in pairs(s.TileByEntry) do
                    tile.Button:Destroy()
                end
                s.TileList = {}
                s.TileByEntry = {}
            end
            sides.Base.Selected, sides.Target.Selected = nil, nil
            StopMusic()
            
            -- Render lại danh sách mới mà không vẽ đè toàn bộ UI
            Refresh() 
        else 
            SetStatus("Load failed!", THEME.Danger) 
        end
    end)

    Refresh()
    task.spawn(function()
        if not PreviewsOn() then return end
        local list, missing = {}, 0
        for _, g in ipairs(groups) do for _, e in ipairs(g.Entries) do table.insert(list, e); if not Preview.PoseKnown(e) then missing = missing + 1 end end end
        if missing == 0 then return end
        Preview.Collect(list)
    end)
end

local Swap = NewSwap({ Category = "Emotes", Noun = "emote", Paths = GameFolder })
BuildEmoteUI(Swap)
end

_G.EvadeHub_CarryCreate = function()
-- Aur_Meow (Carry) - Standalone (Lớp 1: Registry -> Lớp 2: GameFolder -> Lớp 3: Scan -> Lớp 4: ConfirmEmote)
local SCRIPT_NAME = "Aur_Meow (Carry)"
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local function rgb(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
local THEME = {
    Background = rgb(8, 10, 18), BackgroundAlpha = 0.04,
    Card = rgb(255, 255, 255), CardAlpha = 0.94,
    Stroke = rgb(255, 255, 255), StrokeAlpha = 0.86,
    Field = rgb(2, 3, 8), FieldAlpha = 0.42,
    Popup = rgb(10, 12, 20), PopupAlpha = 0.05,
    Text = rgb(250, 250, 255), SubText = rgb(198, 202, 220), Muted = rgb(138, 142, 162),
    Accent = rgb(140, 220, 255), Accent2 = rgb(180, 150, 255), OnAccent = rgb(10, 12, 22),
    Success = rgb(90, 235, 165), Danger = rgb(255, 90, 110), Swapped = rgb(255, 205, 80),
}
local GRADIENT_STOPS = { rgb(255, 255, 255), rgb(224, 234, 255), rgb(255, 247, 226), rgb(255, 224, 160) }
local AURORA_STOPS = { rgb(140, 220, 255), rgb(180, 150, 255), rgb(255, 150, 210), rgb(255, 205, 120) }

local function Linear(stops)
    local points = {}
    for i, color in ipairs(stops) do points[i] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((i - 1) / (#stops - 1), color) end
    return ColorSequence.new(points)
end
local GRADIENT = Linear(GRADIENT_STOPS)
local AURORA = Linear(AURORA_STOPS)
local CYCLE = (function()
    local mirrored = table.clone(AURORA_STOPS)
    for i = #AURORA_STOPS - 1, 1, -1 do table.insert(mirrored, AURORA_STOPS[i]) end
    return Linear(mirrored)
end)()
local FONT_TITLE, FONT_LABEL, FONT_BODY = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, Enum.Font.GothamMedium

local function New(class, props, parent) local i = Instance.new(class); for k,v in pairs(props) do i[k]=v end; i.Parent=parent; return i end
local function Round(i, r) return New("UICorner", {CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,r)}, i) end
local function Stroke(i, t, th, c) return New("UIStroke", {Color=c or THEME.Stroke, Transparency=t, Thickness=th or 1, ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, i) end
local function Gradient(p, r, s) return New("UIGradient", {Rotation=r or 0, Color=s or GRADIENT}, p) end
local function Label(p, props) local m={BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Font=FONT_BODY,TextColor3=THEME.Text,TextSize=13,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left}; for k,v in pairs(props) do m[k]=v end; return New("TextLabel",m,p) end
local function Tween(i, p, d, s) TweenService:Create(i, TweenInfo.new(d or 0.2, s or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), p):Play() end

local function Glyph(p, s) return New("Frame", {AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(s,s),BackgroundTransparency=1}, p) end
local function Bar(r, l, t, rot, x, y, c) local b=New("Frame",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(x,y),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(l,t),Rotation=rot,BackgroundColor3=c,BorderSizePixel=0},r); Round(b,t); return b end
local function ChevronGlyph(p, s, c) local r=Glyph(p,s); local l=s*0.56; return r,{Bar(r,l,2,45,0.31,0.5,c),Bar(r,l,2,-45,0.69,0.5,c)} end
local function CheckGlyph(p, s, c) local r=Glyph(p,s); Bar(r,s*0.34,2,42,0.3,0.63,c); Bar(r,s*0.64,2,-48,0.63,0.51,c); return r end
local function CloseGlyph(p, s, c) local r=Glyph(p,s); return {Bar(r,s*0.78,2,45,0.5,0.5,c),Bar(r,s*0.78,2,-45,0.5,0.5,c)} end
local function MinusGlyph(p, s, c) local r=Glyph(p,s); return {Bar(r,s*0.7,2,0,0.5,0.5,c)} end

local function MakeButton(p, text, pri, w)
    local pill=New("Frame",{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,32),BackgroundColor3=pri and Color3.new(1,1,1) or THEME.Card,BackgroundTransparency=pri and 0 or THEME.CardAlpha,BorderSizePixel=0},p)
    Round(pill,10)
    if pri then Gradient(pill,45,AURORA); local g=Stroke(pill,0.55,3,THEME.Accent2); Gradient(g,45,CYCLE) else Stroke(pill,THEME.StrokeAlpha) end
    local lab=Label(pill,{Text=text,Font=FONT_TITLE,TextColor3=pri and THEME.OnAccent or THEME.Text,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)})
    local hit=New("TextButton",{Text="",BackgroundTransparency=1,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),ZIndex=3},pill)
    local btn={Frame=pill,Label=lab,Hit=hit,Primary=pri,Width=w}
    hit.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() Tween(pill,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w-6,28)},0.08) end)
    hit.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() Tween(pill,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,32)},0.3,Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
    hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(pill,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,32)},0.2) end)
    if not pri then
        hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(pill,{BackgroundTransparency=0.82},0.15) end)
        hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(pill,{BackgroundTransparency=THEME.CardAlpha},0.2) end)
    end
    return btn
end

local function SetEnabled(btn, en)
    btn.Frame.BackgroundTransparency = en and (btn.Primary and 0 or THEME.CardAlpha) or 0.6
    btn.Label.TextTransparency = en and 0 or 0.45
end

local function ResponsiveGrid(scroll, gap, cellHeight, minCell, onChange)
    local layout=New("UIGridLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,CellSize=UDim2.new(1/3,-math.ceil(gap*2/3),0,cellHeight),CellPadding=UDim2.fromOffset(gap,gap)},scroll)
    local columns,token=3,0
    local function Update()
        local width=scroll.AbsoluteWindowSize.X-9
        if width<=0 then return end
        local count=math.max(2,math.floor((width+gap)/(minCell+gap)))
        if count==columns then return end
        columns=count
        layout.CellSize=UDim2.new(1/count,-math.ceil(gap*(count-1)/count),0,cellHeight)
        if onChange then token=token+1; local mine=token; task.delay(0.15,function() if mine==token then onChange() end end) end
    end
    scroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteWindowSize"):Connect(Update)
    Update()
    return layout
end

local BAR_H=58
local function BuildPageLayout(frame)
    local pane=New("Frame",{Name="Panes",Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-BAR_H),BackgroundTransparency=1},frame)
    New("UIPadding",{PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,14),PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,14),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,14),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,10)},pane)
    New("UIListLayout",{FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,10)},pane)
    local bot=New("Frame",{Name="BottomBar",AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,1),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0,1),Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,BAR_H),BackgroundTransparency=1},frame)
    local div=New("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BorderSizePixel=0},bot)
    local dg=Gradient(div,0,AURORA); dg.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.95),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.55),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.95)})
    TweenService:Create(dg,TweenInfo.new(14,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,Enum.EasingDirection.Out,-1),{Rotation=360}):Play()
    local dot=New("Frame",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0,18,0.5,0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(6,6),BackgroundColor3=THEME.Accent,BorderSizePixel=0},bot)
    New("UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)},dot); Stroke(dot,0.35,2,THEME.Accent2)
    local lab=Label(bot,{Position=UDim2.fromOffset(32,0),Size=UDim2.new(1,-470,1,0),TextColor3=THEME.SubText,TextTruncate=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd})
    return {PaneHolder=pane,BottomBar=bot,StatusLabel=lab,StatusDot=dot}
end

local TOPBAR=56
local MIN_WIDTH,MIN_HEIGHT,MAX_WIDTH,MAX_HEIGHT=340,340,1200,760

local function CreateUI()
    local conns,tweens={},{}
    local opened=true
    local function Spin(g,s) local t=TweenService:Create(g,TweenInfo.new(s,Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,Enum.EasingDirection.Out,-1),{Rotation=g.Rotation+360}); t:Play(); table.insert(tweens,t) end
    local par; pcall(function() par=(gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end); if not par then par=Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    local Gui=New("ScreenGui",{Name="AurMeowCarry",ResetOnSpawn=false,IgnoreGuiInset=true,DisplayOrder=10000,ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling},par)
    _G.EvadeHub_CarryCleanup = function() for _,c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; for _,t in ipairs(tweens) do pcall(function() t:Cancel() end) end; pcall(function() Gui:Destroy() end) end
    local Main=New("Frame",{Name="Main",AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(680,470),BackgroundTransparency=1},Gui)
    local MainScale=New("UIScale",{Scale=1},Main)
    local Pop=New("Frame",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1},Main)
    local PopScale=New("UIScale",{Scale=0.9},Pop)
    local rings={}
    for _,l in ipairs({{0,3,0.55},{3,7,0.8},{10,14,0.92}}) do
        local p,t,tr=l[1],l[2],l[3]
        local r=New("Frame",{Name="Glow",AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),Size=UDim2.new(1,p*2,1,p*2),BackgroundTransparency=1},Pop)
        Round(r,18+p)
        local st=Stroke(r,1,t); Spin(Gradient(st,0,CYCLE),10)
        table.insert(rings,{Stroke=st,Base=tr})
    end
    local Win=New("CanvasGroup",{Name="Window",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundColor3=THEME.Background,BackgroundTransparency=THEME.BackgroundAlpha,GroupTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0},Pop)
    Round(Win,18)
    local wash=New("Frame",{Name="Wash",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundColor3=rgb(60,90,160),BorderSizePixel=0},Win)
    Round(wash,18)
    local wg=Gradient(wash,135,AURORA); wg.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.92),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55,0.985),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.94)})
    local sh=New("Frame",{Name="Sheen",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BorderSizePixel=0},Win)
    Round(sh,18)
    New("UIGradient",{Rotation=20,Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.93),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35,1),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.97)})},sh)
    local te=New("Frame",{Name="TopEdge",Size=UDim2.new(1,-32,0,1),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(16,1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BackgroundTransparency=0.78,BorderSizePixel=0},Win)
    local bor=New("Frame",{Name="Border",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1,ZIndex=5},Pop)
    Round(bor,18)
    local bs=Stroke(bor,1,1.6); Spin(Gradient(bs,0,CYCLE),10)
    table.insert(rings,{Stroke=bs,Base=0.28})
    local Top=New("Frame",{Name="Topbar",Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,TOPBAR),BackgroundTransparency=1},Win)
    local br=New("Frame",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0,18,0.5,0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(32,32),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BorderSizePixel=0,ClipsDescendants=true},Top)
    Round(br,10)
    Gradient(br,45,AURORA)
    local bg=Stroke(br,0.55,2,THEME.Accent); Gradient(bg,45,CYCLE)
    Label(br,{Text="C",Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack,TextSize=18,TextColor3=THEME.OnAccent,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)})
    local tr=New("Frame",{Position=UDim2.fromOffset(60,8),Size=UDim2.new(1,-200,0,22),BackgroundTransparency=1},Top)
    New("UIListLayout",{FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center,Padding=UDim.new(0,8)},tr)
    local tl=Label(tr,{Text="Aur_Meow",Font=FONT_TITLE,TextSize=16,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,20),LayoutOrder=1})
    Gradient(tl,0,GRADIENT)
    local vp=New("Frame",{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,16),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BorderSizePixel=0,LayoutOrder=2},tr)
    Round(vp,8)
    New("UIPadding",{PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,8),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,8)},vp)
    Gradient(vp,45,AURORA)
    Label(vp,{Text="Carry",Font=FONT_TITLE,TextSize=11,TextColor3=THEME.OnAccent,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,16)})
    Label(Top,{Text="by Aur_Meow  ·  K to hide or show",TextSize=12,TextColor3=THEME.SubText,Position=UDim2.fromOffset(60,30),Size=UDim2.new(1,-200,0,14)})
    local function CtrlBtn(off, gFn, hCol)
        local btn=New("TextButton",{Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,BorderSizePixel=0,BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,0.5),Position=UDim2.new(1,off,0.5,0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(34,34)},Top)
        Round(btn,10)
        local bars=gFn(btn,20,THEME.SubText)
        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() Tween(btn,{BackgroundTransparency=0.86},0.15); for _,b in ipairs(bars) do Tween(b,{BackgroundColor3=hCol or THEME.Text},0.15) end end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() Tween(btn,{BackgroundTransparency=1},0.2); for _,b in ipairs(bars) do Tween(b,{BackgroundColor3=THEME.SubText},0.2) end end)
        return btn
    end
    local CloseBtn=CtrlBtn(-14,CloseGlyph,THEME.Danger)
    local MinBtn=CtrlBtn(-54,MinusGlyph)
    local tline=New("Frame",{Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,TOPBAR),Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),BorderSizePixel=0},Win)
    local tlg=Gradient(tline,0,AURORA); tlg.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.92),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.6),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.92)})
    local Body=New("Frame",{Name="Body",Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,TOPBAR+1),Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,-(TOPBAR+1)),BackgroundTransparency=1},Win)
    local PageArea=New("Frame",{Name="Pages",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1},Body)
    local function VPS() local c=workspace.CurrentCamera; return c and c.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280,720) end
    local function Relayout()
        local w,h=680,470
        local s=VPS()
        local sc=math.clamp(math.min(1,(s.X-24)/w,(s.Y-24)/h),0.45,1)
        MainScale.Scale=sc
        Tween(Main,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,h)},0.3,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
    end
    Relayout()
    local dragging,dStart,dPos=false,nil,nil
    Top.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging,dStart,dPos=true,i.Position,Main.Position end end)
    local grip=New("TextButton",{Name="ResizeGrip",Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,BackgroundTransparency=1,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(1,1),Position=UDim2.new(1,-6,1,-6),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(22,22),ZIndex=8},Pop)
    Bar(grip,14,2,-45,0.58,0.58,THEME.Muted); Bar(grip,9,2,-45,0.72,0.72,THEME.Muted); Bar(grip,5,2,-45,0.84,0.84,THEME.Muted)
    local rsz,rStart,rSize,rPos=false,nil,nil,nil
    grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then rsz,rStart,rSize,rPos=true,i.Position,Main.Size,Main.Position end end)
    local prs,mvd,pStart,pPos=false,false,nil,nil
    local OpenBtn=New("TextButton",{Name="OpenButton",Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,BorderSizePixel=0,AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.new(0,40,0.5,0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(54,54),BackgroundColor3=THEME.Popup,BackgroundTransparency=THEME.PopupAlpha,Visible=false,ClipsDescendants=true},Gui)
    New("UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)},OpenBtn)
    local OpenScale=New("UIScale",{Scale=1},OpenBtn)
    Spin(Gradient(Stroke(OpenBtn,0.12,2,THEME.Accent),0,CYCLE),6)
    Spin(Gradient(Stroke(OpenBtn,0.68,7,THEME.Accent2),0,CYCLE),6)
    local om=Label(OpenBtn,{Text="C",Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack,TextSize=22,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)})
    Gradient(om,45,AURORA)
    local openDrag,oStart,oPos=false,nil,nil
    OpenBtn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch then openDrag,oStart,oPos=true,i.Position,OpenBtn.Position; prs,mvd,pStart,pPos=true,false,i.Position,OpenBtn.Position end end)
    table.insert(conns,UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        local isM=i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        if not isM then return end
        local sc=MainScale.Scale
        if dragging then local d=(i.Position-dStart)/sc; Main.Position=UDim2.new(dPos.X.Scale,dPos.X.Offset+d.X,dPos.Y.Scale,dPos.Y.Offset+d.Y) end
        if rsz then
            local s=VPS(); local d=(i.Position-rStart)/sc
            local mW=math.max(MIN_WIDTH,math.min(MAX_WIDTH,(s.X-24)/sc))
            local mH=math.max(MIN_HEIGHT,math.min(MAX_HEIGHT,(s.Y-24)/sc))
            local w=math.clamp(rSize.X.Offset+d.X,MIN_WIDTH,mW)
            local h=math.clamp(rSize.Y.Offset+d.Y,MIN_HEIGHT,mH)
            Main.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,h)
            Main.Position=UDim2.new(rPos.X.Scale,rPos.X.Offset+(w-rSize.X.Offset)/2,rPos.Y.Scale,rPos.Y.Offset+(h-rSize.Y.Offset)/2)
        end
        if openDrag then local d=(i.Position-oStart); if d.Magnitude>6 then mvd=true end; OpenBtn.Position=UDim2.new(oPos.X.Scale,oPos.X.Offset+d.X,oPos.Y.Scale,oPos.Y.Offset+d.Y) end
    end))
    local function SetOpen(st)
        if st==opened then return end
        opened=st; OpenScale.Scale=MainScale.Scale
        if st then
            Main.Visible=true; PopScale.Scale=0.9
            Tween(PopScale,{Scale=1},0.5,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint); Tween(Win,{GroupTransparency=0},0.3)
            for _,r in ipairs(rings) do Tween(r.Stroke,{Transparency=r.Base},0.4) end
            Tween(OpenBtn,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,0)},0.18)
            task.delay(0.18,function() if opened then OpenBtn.Visible=false end end)
        else
            Tween(PopScale,{Scale=0.92},0.3,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint); Tween(Win,{GroupTransparency=1},0.24)
            for _,r in ipairs(rings) do Tween(r.Stroke,{Transparency=1},0.2) end
            task.delay(0.3,function() if not opened then Main.Visible=false end end)
            OpenBtn.Visible=true; Tween(OpenBtn,{Size=UDim2.fromOffset(54,54)},0.5,Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
        end
    end
    table.insert(conns,UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and i.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end; dragging,rsz,openDrag=false,false,false; if prs then prs=false; if not mvd then SetOpen(true) end end end))
    MinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() SetOpen(false) end)
    table.insert(conns,UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(i,proc) if proc or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end; if i.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.K then SetOpen(not opened) end end))
    
    local dlgOp=false
    local function ConfirmClose()
        if dlgOp then return end; dlgOp=true
        local ov=New("TextButton",{Name="Dialog",Text="",AutoButtonColor=false,BorderSizePixel=0,Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0),BackgroundTransparency=1,ZIndex=50},Win)
        Tween(ov,{BackgroundTransparency=0.42},0.25)
        local cd=New("CanvasGroup",{AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0.5,0.5),Position=UDim2.fromScale(0.5,0.5),Size=UDim2.new(0,340,0,0),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,BackgroundColor3=THEME.Popup,BackgroundTransparency=THEME.PopupAlpha,GroupTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Active=true,ZIndex=51},ov)
        Round(cd,16)
        local ol=Stroke(cd,1,1.6,Color3.new(1,1,1)); Spin(Gradient(ol,45,CYCLE),8)
        New("UIPadding",{PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,18),PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,18),PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,18),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,18)},cd)
        New("UIListLayout",{SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,10)},cd)
        local cs=New("UIScale",{Scale=0.9},cd); Tween(cs,{Scale=1},0.4,Enum.EasingStyle.Quint); Tween(cd,{GroupTransparency=0},0.25); Tween(ol,{Transparency=0.3},0.25)
        local ti=Label(cd,{Text="Close "..tl.Text.."?",Font=FONT_TITLE,TextSize=16,TextColor3=Color3.new(1,1,1),TextWrapped=true,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),LayoutOrder=1})
        Gradient(ti,0,AURORA)
        Label(cd,{Text="The interface will be closed and everything you swapped or applied will be put back to normal. Run the script again to reopen it.",TextSize=14,TextColor3=THEME.SubText,TextWrapped=true,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),LayoutOrder=2})
        local rw=New("Frame",{Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,36),BackgroundTransparency=1,LayoutOrder=3},cd)
        New("UIListLayout",{FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal,HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,Padding=UDim.new(0,8)},rw)
        local function CloseDlg() Tween(cs,{Scale=0.93},0.2); Tween(cd,{GroupTransparency=1},0.2); Tween(ol,{Transparency=1},0.2); Tween(ov,{BackgroundTransparency=1},0.2); task.delay(0.22,function() ov:Destroy(); dlgOp=false end) end
        local function DBtn(text,dng,ord,cb)
            local btn=New("TextButton",{Text=text,Font=FONT_TITLE,TextSize=13,TextColor3=dng and THEME.OnAccent or THEME.Text,AutoButtonColor=false,BorderSizePixel=0,AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X,Size=UDim2.fromOffset(0,36),BackgroundColor3=dng and THEME.Danger or THEME.Card,BackgroundTransparency=dng and 0 or 0.84,LayoutOrder=ord},rw)
            Round(btn,10); New("UIPadding",{PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,18),PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,18)},btn); btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(cb)
        end
        DBtn("Cancel",false,1,CloseDlg)
        DBtn("Close",true,2,function() for _,c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; for _,t in ipairs(tweens) do pcall(function() t:Cancel() end) end; pcall(function() Gui:Destroy() end) end)
    end
    CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(ConfirmClose)
    Tween(PopScale,{Scale=1},0.45,Enum.EasingStyle.Back); Tween(Win,{GroupTransparency=0},0.35)
    for _,r in ipairs(rings) do Tween(r.Stroke,{Transparency=r.Base},0.45) end
    return PageArea
end
