-- ============================================================
-- FLOAT PLATFORM + FREE CAMERA SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local function createPlatform(y)
    if platform and platform.Parent then return platform end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil end
    platform = Instance.new("Part")
    platform.Anchored = true
    platform.CanCollide = true
    platform.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    platform.Transparency = 0.4
    platform.Size = PLATFORM_SIZE
    platform.CastShadow = false
    platform.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, y - OFFSET, hrp.Position.Z)
    platform.Parent = workspace
    return platform
end

local function destroyPlatform()
    if platform then
        platform:Destroy()
        platform = nil
    end
end

local function followPlatform()
    if followConn then return end
    followConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if floating and platform then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and humanoid then
                if humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
                    savedY = hrp.Position.Y
                end
                platform.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, savedY - OFFSET, hrp.Position.Z)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function stopFollow()
    if followConn then
        followConn:Disconnect()
        followConn = nil
    end
end

freecamGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
freecamGui.Name = "FreecamJoystick"
freecamGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
freecamGui.Enabled = false
freecamGui.Parent = PlayerGui

local joystickContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
joystickContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
joystickContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
joystickContainer.Parent = freecamGui

local fcSizeOuter = 120
local fcSizeInner = 50

local fcOuter = Instance.new("Frame")
fcOuter.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(fcSizeOuter, fcSizeOuter)
fcOuter.Position = UDim2.new(0.15, 0, 0.75, 0)
fcOuter.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
fcOuter.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
fcOuter.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
fcOuter.Parent = joystickContainer
fcOuter.ZIndex = 1
fcOuter.Active = true
Instance.new("UICorner", fcOuter).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local fcOuterStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", fcOuter)
fcOuterStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
fcOuterStroke.Transparency = 0.7
fcOuterStroke.Thickness = 1.5

local fcInner = Instance.new("Frame")
fcInner.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(fcSizeInner, fcSizeInner)
fcInner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -fcSizeInner/2, 0.5, -fcSizeInner/2)
fcInner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
fcInner.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
fcInner.Parent = fcOuter
fcInner.ZIndex = 2
Instance.new("UICorner", fcInner).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local fcInnerStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", fcInner)
fcInnerStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
fcInnerStroke.Transparency = 0.8
fcInnerStroke.Thickness = 2

local fcRadius = fcSizeOuter / 2
local fcCenter
local fcDragging = false
local fcActiveTouch

local function fcToV2(pos) return Vector2.new(pos.X, pos.Y) end

local function fcUpdateCenter()
    fcCenter = Vector2.new(
        fcOuter.AbsolutePosition.X + fcRadius,
        fcOuter.AbsolutePosition.Y + fcRadius
    )
end

local function fcMoveInner(posV2)
    local dir = posV2 - fcCenter
    local dist = math.min(dir.Magnitude, fcRadius)
    local offset = dir.Magnitude > 0 and dir.Unit * dist or Vector2.new(0,0)
    fcInner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, offset.X - fcSizeInner/2, 0.5, offset.Y - fcSizeInner/2)
    _G.JoystickData.DraggingLevel = math.floor((dist / fcRadius) * 100)
    _G.JoystickData.Direction = Vector3.new(offset.X / fcRadius, 0, offset.Y / fcRadius)
end

fcOuter.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
    or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        if not fcDragging then
            fcUpdateCenter()
            fcDragging = true
            fcActiveTouch = input
            fcMoveInner(fcToV2(input.Position))
        end
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if fcDragging and fcActiveTouch and input == fcActiveTouch then
        fcMoveInner(fcToV2(input.Position))
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if fcDragging and fcActiveTouch and input == fcActiveTouch then
        fcDragging = false
        fcActiveTouch = nil
        fcInner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -fcSizeInner/2, 0.5, -fcSizeInner/2)
        _G.JoystickData.DraggingLevel = 0
        _G.JoystickData.Direction = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
    end
end)

movePart = Instance.new("Part")
movePart.Size = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
movePart.Anchored = true
movePart.Transparency = 1
movePart.CanCollide = false
movePart.Parent = workspace
movePart.CFrame = Camera.CFrame
movePart.Name = "CameraPart"

currentPos = movePart.Position
origMinZoom = LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance
origMaxZoom = LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance

local function setDefaultControlsVisible(state)
    local touchGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
    if touchGui then
        touchGui.Enabled = state
    end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = state and 16 or 0
        humanoid.JumpPower = state and 50 or 0
    end
end

local function applyFreecam(state)
    if state then
        Camera.CameraSubject = movePart
        Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0
        LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0
        setDefaultControlsVisible(false)
    else
        local h = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or nil
        Camera.CameraSubject = h
        LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = origMinZoom
        LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = origMaxZoom
        Camera.FieldOfView = DEFAULT_FOV
        setDefaultControlsVisible(true)
    end
end

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not freecamEnabled then return end

    -- FIX: giữ freecam sống khi hết round / respawn / game đổi camera
    -- (miễn toggle còn bật thì freecam luôn hoạt động, tắt toggle mới thoát)
    local liveCam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if liveCam and liveCam ~= Camera then Camera = liveCam end
    if not movePart or not movePart.Parent then
        movePart = Instance.new("Part")
        movePart.Size = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        movePart.Anchored = true
        movePart.Transparency = 1
        movePart.CanCollide = false
        movePart.Name = "CameraPart"
        movePart.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos or Camera.CFrame.Position)
        movePart.Parent = workspace
    end
    if Camera.CameraSubject ~= movePart then Camera.CameraSubject = movePart end
    if Camera.CameraType ~= Enum.CameraType.Custom then Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom end
    if LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance ~= 0 then LocalPlayer.CameraMinZoomDistance = 0 end
    if LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance ~= 0 then LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 0 end
    local fcPg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if freecamGui then
        if fcPg and freecamGui.Parent ~= fcPg then freecamGui.Parent = fcPg end
        if not freecamGui.Enabled then freecamGui.Enabled = true end
    end
    local fcNow = os.clock()
    if fcNow - (_G.JoystickData.lastEnforce or 0) > 0.5 then
        _G.JoystickData.lastEnforce = fcNow
        pcall(setDefaultControlsVisible, false)
    end

    local dir = _G.JoystickData.Direction
    local level = _G.JoystickData.DraggingLevel

    if level > 1 then
        local moveSpeed = (level * 0.02) * speedMultiplier
        local camCF = Camera.CFrame
        local forward = camCF.LookVector
        local right = camCF.RightVector
        local moveDir = (forward * -dir.Z) + (right * dir.X)
        moveDir += Vector3.new(0, dir.Y, 0)
        if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
            moveDir = moveDir.Unit
            currentPos = currentPos + moveDir * moveSpeed * dt * 60
        end
    end

    local camLook = Camera.CFrame.LookVector
    local yaw = math.atan2(camLook.X, camLook.Z)
    movePart.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
end)

-- ============================================================
-- TAB OTHER AMENITIES
-- ============================================================
_G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Section({ Title = "Góc nhìn", Opened = true, Order = 400, NoDivider = true })
;(function()
    local PROPS = { "Ambient", "OutdoorAmbient", "Brightness", "ClockTime", "FogEnd", "GlobalShadows", "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", "EnvironmentSpecularScale" }
    local FORCED = {
        Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Brightness = 2,
        ClockTime = 14, FogEnd = 100000, GlobalShadows = false, EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1, EnvironmentSpecularScale = 1,
    }
    local gameValues = {} -- giá trị mà GAME đang đặt (cập nhật khi game tự đổi lúc đang bật Full Bright)
    local fbOn = false
    local fbGen = 0

    _G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Full Bright",
        Desc = "Bật ánh sáng toàn map",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "FullBright",
        Callback = function(value)
            fbOn = value
            fbGen = fbGen + 1
            local myGen = fbGen
            if value then
                for _, p in ipairs(PROPS) do gameValues[p] = Lighting[p] end
                for _, p in ipairs(PROPS) do pcall(function() Lighting[p] = FORCED[p] end) end
                task.spawn(function()
                    while fbOn and fbGen == myGen do
                        for _, p in ipairs(PROPS) do
                            local cur = Lighting[p]
                            if cur ~= FORCED[p] then
                                -- game vừa tự đổi giá trị này (đổi ngày/đêm, đổi map...): nhớ làm mặc định hiện tại của game rồi ép sáng lại
                                gameValues[p] = cur
                                pcall(function() Lighting[p] = FORCED[p] end)
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.2)
                    end
                end)
            else
                -- tắt: trả về đúng mặc định HIỆN TẠI của game (tối thì tối, sáng thì sáng), không phải giá trị lúc bật
                for _, p in ipairs(PROPS) do
                    if gameValues[p] ~= nil then pcall(function() Lighting[p] = gameValues[p] end) end
                end
            end
        end,
    })
end)()

-- Remove fog: tự phát hiện sương mù rồi xóa, tắt toggle thì khôi phục lại (bọc trong hàm riêng để không thêm biến local)
;(function()
    local fogOn = false
    local fogGen = 0
    local backup = { start = nil, finish = nil, atm = {} }
    local CLEAR_END = 1000000

    local function clearFog()
        -- Fog kiểu cũ (FogStart/FogEnd): FogEnd < 100000 nghĩa là đang có sương mù
        if Lighting.FogEnd < 100000 then
            backup.start, backup.finish = Lighting.FogStart, Lighting.FogEnd
            Lighting.FogEnd = CLEAR_END
            Lighting.FogStart = 100000
        end
        -- Atmosphere (Density / Haze)
        for _, obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Atmosphere") and (obj.Density > 0 or obj.Haze > 0) then
                backup.atm[obj] = { Density = obj.Density, Haze = obj.Haze }
                obj.Density = 0
                obj.Haze = 0
            end
        end
    end

    local function restoreFog()
        pcall(function()
            if backup.finish then
                Lighting.FogStart = backup.start
                Lighting.FogEnd = backup.finish
            end
            for obj, v in pairs(backup.atm) do
                if obj and obj.Parent then
                    obj.Density = v.Density
                    obj.Haze = v.Haze
                end
            end
        end)
        backup.start, backup.finish, backup.atm = nil, nil, {}
    end

    _G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Remove fog",
        Desc = "Tự phát hiện và xóa sương mù, tắt đi sẽ khôi phục lại",
        Value = false,
        Flag = "RemoveFog",
        Callback = function(value)
            fogOn = value
            fogGen += 1
            local myGen = fogGen
            if value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while fogOn and fogGen == myGen do
                        pcall(clearFog)
                        task.wait(0.3)
                    end
                end)
            else
                restoreFog()
            end
        end,
    })
end)()

local fpsGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
fpsGui.Name = "FPSDisplay" fpsGui.ResetOnSpawn = false fpsGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true fpsGui.Enabled = false fpsGui.Parent = PlayerGui

local fpsFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
fpsFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0) fpsFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.11, 0, 0.065, 0) fpsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
fpsFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) fpsFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 fpsFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0 fpsFrame.Parent = fpsGui
local fpsCorner = Instance.new("UICorner") fpsCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) fpsCorner.Parent = fpsFrame
local fpsStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke") fpsStroke.Thickness = 2 fpsStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0) fpsStroke.Parent = fpsFrame
local strokeGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
strokeGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
})
strokeGradient.Parent = fpsStroke
task.spawn(function() while fpsGui.Parent do task.wait(0.05) strokeGradient.Rotation = (strokeGradient.Rotation + 5) % 360 end end)

local fpsLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
fpsLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0) fpsLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0)
fpsLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1 fpsLabel.Text = "FPS: --" fpsLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
fpsLabel.TextScaled = true fpsLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold fpsLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center fpsLabel.Parent = fpsFrame

local frameCount = 0
local lastTime = tick()
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not fpsGui.Enabled then return end
    frameCount = frameCount + 1
    local now = tick()
    if now - lastTime >= 0.5 then
        currentFPSValue = math.floor(frameCount / (now - lastTime))
        frameCount = 0
        lastTime = now
        local color
        if currentFPSValue >= 50 then color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)
        elseif currentFPSValue >= 30 then color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
        else color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0) end
        fpsLabel.Text = "FPS: " .. currentFPSValue
        fpsLabel.TextColor3 = color
    end
end)

local fpsDragging = false
local fpsDragStart
local fpsStartPos
fpsFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        fpsDragging = true fpsDragStart = input.Position fpsStartPos = fpsFrame.Position
    end
end)
fpsFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if fpsDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - fpsDragStart
        fpsFrame.Position = UDim2.new(fpsStartPos.X.Scale, fpsStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X, fpsStartPos.Y.Scale, fpsStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then fpsDragging = false end
end)

-- Toggle "Show FPS" đã được chuyển sang cạnh "Round Timer" (tab Character); hàm bật/tắt đặt ở _G để toggle bên đó gọi
_G.EvadeHub_SetFPS = function(value)
    fpsGui.Enabled = value
end

-- Display Ping: bật toggle thì tự tạo GUI (giống Display Time Round, viền đen-trắng), tắt thì xóa
;(function()
    local ping = { sg = nil, conn = nil }

    local function destroyPing()
        if ping.conn then ping.conn:Disconnect(); ping.conn = nil end
        if ping.sg then pcall(function() ping.sg:Destroy() end); ping.sg = nil end
    end

    local function createPing()
        if ping.sg then return end
        local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or PlayerGui

        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "PingDisplay_GG_EVADE_Lol"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.DisplayOrder = 2
        sg.Parent = pg

        local container = Instance.new("Frame")
        container.Name = "PingContainer"
        container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.065, 20)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(0.11, 0, 0.065, 0)
        container.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        container.Parent = sg
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        corner.Parent = container
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Thickness = 3
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        stroke.Parent = container

        -- Viền gradient Trắng -> Đen -> Trắng
        local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new{
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
        }
        grad.Parent = stroke

        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -4)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 2)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = "Ping: --ms"
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.TextScaled = true
        label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        label.Parent = container

        -- Kéo thả giống Display Time Round
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
        container.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = container.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        container.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                container.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)

        local acc = 0.5
        ping.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            grad.Rotation = (grad.Rotation + 90 * dt) % 360
            acc = acc + dt
            if acc >= 0.5 then
                acc = 0
                local ms = GetPing()
                if ms <= 0 then
                    pcall(function() ms = math.floor(LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000) end)
                end
                label.Text = "Ping: " .. ms .. "ms"
            end
        end)
        ping.sg = sg
    end

    _G.EvadeHub_SetPing = function(value)
        if value then createPing() else destroyPing() end
    end
end)()

-- Display CPU / Display GPU: GUI giống Display FPS, viền 3D khác màu (CPU tím-đỏ, GPU trắng-đen)
;(function()
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local StatsService = game:GetService("Stats")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

    local hbMs = 0
    RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt) hbMs = hbMs * 0.9 + (dt * 1000) * 0.1 end)

    local function readStat(name)
        local ok, v = pcall(function() return StatsService.PerformanceStats[name]:GetValue() end)
        if ok and type(v) == "number" then return v end
        return nil
    end

    local function makeStatGui(guiName, prefix, colorA, colorB, yScale, yOffset, getValue)
        local obj = { gui = nil }
        function obj.destroy()
            if obj.gui then pcall(function() obj.gui:Destroy() end); obj.gui = nil end
        end
        function obj.create()
            obj.destroy()
            local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
            if not pg then return end
            local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui.Name = guiName
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            gui.Parent = pg
            obj.gui = gui

            -- lớp bóng phía sau tạo hiệu ứng 3D
            local shadow = Instance.new("Frame")
            shadow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            shadow.Size = UDim2.new(0.11, 0, 0.065, 0)
            shadow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10 + 3, yScale, yOffset + 3)
            shadow.BackgroundColor3 = colorB
            shadow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
            shadow.BorderSizePixel = 0
            shadow.Parent = gui
            Instance.new("UICorner", shadow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
            frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
            frame.Size = UDim2.new(0.11, 0, 0.065, 0)
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, yScale, yOffset)
            frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame.Parent = gui
            Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Thickness = 2
            stroke.Color = colorA
            stroke.Parent = frame
            local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, colorA),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, colorB),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, colorA),
            })
            grad.Parent = stroke

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0)
            label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 0)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = prefix .. "--"
            label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            label.TextScaled = true
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            label.Parent = frame

            -- kéo thả (khung + bóng đi cùng nhau)
            local dragging, dragStart, startPos, shadowStart = false, nil, nil, nil
            frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging = true
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = frame.Position
                    shadowStart = shadow.Position
                end
            end)
            frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local d = input.Position - dragStart
                    frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                    shadow.Position = UDim2.new(shadowStart.X.Scale, shadowStart.X.Offset + d.X, shadowStart.Y.Scale, shadowStart.Y.Offset + d.Y)
                end
            end)
            frame.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                local acc = 0
                while gui.Parent do
                    local dt = task.wait(0.05)
                    grad.Rotation = (grad.Rotation + 5) % 360
                    acc = acc + (dt or 0.05)
                    if acc >= 0.5 then
                        acc = 0
                        local v = getValue()
                        label.Text = v and (prefix .. string.format("%.1f", v) .. "ms") or (prefix .. "N/A")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        return obj
    end

    local cpuGui = makeStatGui("CPUDisplayGui", "CPU: ", Color3.fromRGB(150, 60, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40), 0.13, 30, function()
        return readStat("CPU") or hbMs
    end)
    local gpuGui = makeStatGui("GPUDisplayGui", "GPU: ", Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.195, 40, function()
        return readStat("GPU")
    end)

    if _G.EvadeHub_CPUDestroy then pcall(_G.EvadeHub_CPUDestroy) end
    if _G.EvadeHub_GPUDestroy then pcall(_G.EvadeHub_GPUDestroy) end
    _G.EvadeHub_CPUDestroy = cpuGui.destroy
    _G.EvadeHub_GPUDestroy = gpuGui.destroy
    _G.EvadeHub_SetCPU = function(v) if v then cpuGui.create() else cpuGui.destroy() end end
    _G.EvadeHub_SetGPU = function(v) if v then gpuGui.create() else gpuGui.destroy() end end
end)()


OtherTab:Section({ Title = "Troll" })

local function SendChat(message)
    pcall(function()
        local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            local channel = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
            if channel then channel:SendAsync(message) return end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").DefaultChatSystemChatEvents.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(message, "All")
    end)
end

local function OnPlayerChatted(plr, message)
    if not chatMimic then return end
    if plr == LocalPlayer then return end
    if not message or message == "" then return end
    SendChat(message)
end

local function ConnectAllPlayers()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
            if not mimicConnections[plr] then
                mimicConnections[plr] = plr.Chatted:Connect(function(msg) OnPlayerChatted(plr, msg) end)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function DisconnectAllPlayers()
    for plr, conn in pairs(mimicConnections) do if conn then conn:Disconnect() end end
    mimicConnections = {}
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    if chatMimic and plr ~= LocalPlayer then
        task.wait(1)
        if not mimicConnections[plr] then
            mimicConnections[plr] = plr.Chatted:Connect(function(msg) OnPlayerChatted(plr, msg) end)
        end
    end
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    if mimicConnections[plr] then mimicConnections[plr]:Disconnect() mimicConnections[plr] = nil end
end)

OtherTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Chat Mimic",
    Desc = "Chat lại y chang người khác",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "ChatMimic",
    Callback = function(value)
        chatMimic = value
        if value then ConnectAllPlayers() WindUI:Notify({ Title = "✅ Chat Mimic BẬT", Content = "Chat lại y chang người khác", Duration = 3 })
        else DisconnectAllPlayers() WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Chat Mimic TẮT", Content = "Đã dừng chat lại", Duration = 3 }) end
    end,
})

OtherTab:Section({ Title = "Dance support", MobileOnly = true })

local function FlashInputGreen()
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.05)
        local focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
        if focused then focused.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) end
    end)
end

OtherTab:Input({
    Title = "Joystick size",
    Value = "",
    Placeholder = "Nhập size joystick / D-pad...",
    Callback = function(text)
        local num = tonumber(text)
        if num and num >= 20 then
            joystickSize = num
            if applySizeChanges then
                outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(joystickSize, joystickSize)
                dpadContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(joystickSize, joystickSize)
                maxDistance = (joystickSize - sizeInner) / 2
            end
            FlashInputGreen()
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "✅ Joystick size", Content = "Đã đặt size: " .. joystickSize, Duration = 2 })
        end
    end,
})

OtherTab:Input({
    Title = "Jump button size",
    Value = "",
    Placeholder = "Nhập size nút nhảy...",
    Callback = function(text)
        local num = tonumber(text)
        if num and num >= 20 then
            jumpSize = num
            if applySizeChanges then jumpBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(jumpSize, jumpSize) end
            FlashInputGreen()
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "✅ Jump size", Content = "Đã đặt size: " .. jumpSize, Duration = 2 })
        end
    end,
})

OtherTab:Button({
    Title = "Switch to support operation",
    Callback = function()
        if supportMode == "joystick" then
            supportMode = "dpad"
            outer.Visible = false
            dpadContainer.Visible = true
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Đã đổi chế độ", Content = "Chế độ: Nút di chuyển (D-pad)", Duration = 3 })
        else
            supportMode = "joystick"
            outer.Visible = true
            dpadContainer.Visible = false
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Đã đổi chế độ", Content = "Chế độ: Joystick", Duration = 3 })
        end
    end,
})

OtherTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Apply changes",
    Desc = "Tự động áp dụng size mới",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "ApplyChanges",
    Callback = function(value)
        applySizeChanges = value
        if value then
            if joystickSize and joystickSize >= 20 then
                outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(joystickSize, joystickSize)
                dpadContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(joystickSize, joystickSize)
                maxDistance = (joystickSize - sizeInner) / 2
            end
            if jumpSize and jumpSize >= 20 then jumpBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(jumpSize, jumpSize) end
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "✅ Apply changes BẬT", Content = "Nhập size vào input để tự động áp dụng", Duration = 3 })
        else
            outer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(defaultJoystickSize, defaultJoystickSize)
            dpadContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(defaultJoystickSize, defaultJoystickSize)
            jumpBtn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(defaultJumpSize, defaultJumpSize)
            maxDistance = (defaultJoystickSize - sizeInner) / 2
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Apply changes TẮT", Content = "Đã quay về size mặc định", Duration = 3 })
        end
    end,
})

    Title = "Supporting ingredients",
    Desc = "Hiện joystick và các nút hỗ trợ",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "SupportingIngredients",
    Callback = function(value)
        hideControls = value
        screenGui.Enabled = value
        if value then EnforceHide()
        else
            adjustJoystick = false
            transformActive = false
            transformScreenGui.Enabled = false
            emoteRunning = false
            if crouchActive then
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
                crouchActive = false
            end
            for keyCode, _ in pairs(activeKeys) do VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game) end
            activeKeys = {}
            currentDiagonal = nil
            -- Reset multi-touch table
            activeButtons = {}
            jumpHolding = false
            for _, btn in ipairs(dpadButtons) do 
                btn.Active = true 
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
            end
            jumpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
            local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                local tg = pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
                if tg then tg.Enabled = true end
                local cm = pg:FindFirstChild("ControlModule")
                if cm then pcall(function() require(cm):Enable() end) end
            end
        end
    end,
})

_G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Section({ Title = "Float Platform & Camera", Order = 300, NoDivider = true })

_G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Toggle({
    Title = "☁️ Float",
    Desc = "Tạo platform bay theo nhân vật",
    Value = false,
    Flag = "FloatPlatform",
    Callback = function(value)
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        if value then
            savedY = hrp.Position.Y
            floating = true
            createPlatform(savedY)
            followPlatform()
        else
            floating = false
            stopFollow()
            destroyPlatform()
        end
    end,
})

        speedMultiplier = value
    end,
})

_G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Button({
    Title = "📍 Teleporting character",
    Callback = function()
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        if c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local camCF = Camera.CFrame
            local _, yaw, _ = camCF:ToOrientation()
            local uprightCFrame = CFrame.new(camCF.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
            c:PivotTo(uprightCFrame)
        end
    end,
})

_G.EvadeHub_EspTab:Button({
    Title = "🎥 Teleporting camera",
    Callback = function()
        local c = LocalPlayer.Character
        if c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local rootCF = c.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            local _, yaw, _ = rootCF:ToOrientation()
            local camCF = CFrame.new(rootCF.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
            currentPos = camCF.Position
            movePart.CFrame = camCF
            Camera.CFrame = camCF
        end
    end,
})

        if value then
            task.spawn(function()
                if savedJobId == "" then WindUI:Notify({ Title = "⚠️ Chưa nhập JobID", Content = "Vui lòng nhập JobID trước", Duration = 5 }) joinServerSpam = false
                elseif savedJobId == game.JobId then WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Từ chối joined", Content = "JobID trùng server hiện tại", Duration = 5 }) joinServerSpam = false
                else

                    task.wait(3)
                    if joinServerSpam then pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, savedJobId, LocalPlayer) end) end
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

ServerTab:Section({ Title = "Server Type" })

local function GetStableServer()
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
    end)
    if ok and result and result.data then
        local servers = {}
        for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
            if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing >= 1 and server.playing <= 10 and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                table.insert(servers, server.id)
            end
        end
        if #servers > 0 then return servers[math.random(1, #servers)] end
    end
    return nil
end

                if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then table.insert(servers, server.id) end

            else TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer) end

    end,
})

            else

ServerTab:Button({
    Title = "Joined Region Server",
    Callback = function()
        local placeId = game.PlaceId
        local success, result = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")) end)
        if success and result and result.data then
            local servers = {}
            for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
                if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then table.insert(servers, server) end
            end
            if #servers > 0 then
                table.sort(servers, function(a, b) return a.playing < b.playing end)
                local topServers = {}
                for i = 1, math.min(5, #servers) do table.insert(topServers, servers[i].id) end
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, topServers[math.random(1, #topServers)], LocalPlayer)
            else TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer) end
        else TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer) end
    end,
})

    end,

local function ProcessChatCommand(message)
    if not chatShortcut then return end
    local cleanMsg = string.gsub(message, "^/", "")
    local lowerMsg = string.lower(cleanMsg)
    if lowerMsg == "exit" or lowerMsg == "bye" or lowerMsg == "cút" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "👋 Thoát server", Content = "Đang rời khỏi server...", Duration = 2 })
        task.wait(2)
        LocalPlayer:Kick("Bạn đã thoát server bằng lệnh chat")
        return
    end
    local function DoHop(minP, maxP)
        local placeId = game.PlaceId
        local success, result = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")) end)
        if success and result and result.data then
            local servers = {}
            for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
                if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing >= minP and server.playing <= maxP and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
                    table.insert(servers, server.id)
                end
            end
            if #servers > 0 then TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, servers[math.random(1, #servers)], LocalPlayer) end
        end
    end
    if lowerMsg == "rejoin" or lowerMsg == "rejoined" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Rejoin", Content = "Đang vào lại server", Duration = 3 })
        task.wait(1)
        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
    elseif lowerMsg == "hop" or lowerMsg == "serverhop" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Server Hop", Content = "Đang đổi server", Duration = 3 })
        task.wait(1) DoHop(0, 100)
    elseif lowerMsg == "small" or lowerMsg == "smallserver" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Small Server", Content = "Đang tìm server ít người", Duration = 3 })
        task.wait(1) DoHop(1, 5)
    elseif lowerMsg == "dead" or lowerMsg == "deadserver" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Dead Server", Content = "Đang tìm server chết", Duration = 3 })
        task.wait(1) DoHop(0, 1)
    elseif lowerMsg == "random" or lowerMsg == "randomserver" then
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🔄 Random Server", Content = "Đang đổi server ngẫu nhiên", Duration = 3 })
        task.wait(1) DoHop(0, 100)
    else
        local jobIdMatch = string.match(message, '[Jj]ob[Ii][Dd]"([^"]+)"')
        if jobIdMatch then
            local jobId = string.gsub(jobIdMatch, "%s+", "")
            if jobId == game.JobId then WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Từ chối joined", Content = "JobID trùng server hiện tại", Duration = 5 })
            else
                WindUI:Notify({ Title = "✔️ Đang joined server...", Content = "Đang vào server qua JobID", Duration = 3 })
                task.wait(3)
                pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, jobId, LocalPlayer) end)
            end
        end
    end
end

LocalPlayer.Chatted:Connect(function(message) ProcessChatCommand(message) end)

local function RefreshCache()
    safePartsCache = {}
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    local originPos = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and char.HumanoidRootPart.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            if obj.CanCollide and obj.Material ~= Enum.Material.Water and obj.Size.Y >= 1 and (obj.Position - originPos).Magnitude <= 500 then
                table.insert(safePartsCache, obj)
            end
        end
    end
end
RefreshCache()
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if not absoluteSafety then task.wait(0.1) continue end
        if #safePartsCache == 0 then RefreshCache() task.wait(0.5) continue end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then task.wait(0.1) continue end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then task.wait(0.1) continue end
        local part = safePartsCache[math.random(1, #safePartsCache)]
        checkedCount = checkedCount + 1
        local standPos = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, part.Size.Y / 2 + 3, 0)
        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(standPos)
        safeCount = safeCount + 1
        absStats.Text = string.format("Đã quét: %d\nAn toàn: %d", checkedCount, safeCount)
    end
end)
task.spawn(function() while task.wait(5) do if absoluteSafety then RefreshCache() end end end)

    end,
})

NotifyTab:Section({ Title = "Report Errors" })

NotifyTab:Input({
    Title = "Nhập lỗi cần báo cáo",
    Value = "",
    Placeholder = "Mô tả lỗi...",
    Callback = function(text) errorInput = text end,
})

NotifyTab:Button({
    Title = "Send a message",
    Callback = function()
        if errorInput == "" then WindUI:Notify({ Title = "⚠️ Chưa nhập lỗi", Content = "Vui lòng nhập mô tả lỗi", Duration = 3 }) return end
        local content = string.format(
            "❗ *Thông báo có lỗi*\n👤 *Username:* %s - @%s\n📊 *Error Notes:* %s\n🕑 *Error time:* %s\n-----Server-----\n🎮 *Game:* %s - JobID: `%s`\n👤 *Players:* %s",
            LocalPlayer.DisplayName, LocalPlayer.Name, errorInput, GetTimeReal(), GetGameName(), game.JobId, GetPlayerCount()
        )
        local ok, err = SendTelegram(content)
        if ok then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "👑 Admin", Content = "🫙🐿️ Đang bận lọ tí làm nhé bro...", Duration = 10 })
            lastReportTime = tick()
            errorInput = ""
        else WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Lỗi gửi", Content = tostring(err), Duration = 5 }) end
    end,
})

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(5)
    while task.wait(3) do
        local updates = GetTelegramUpdates()
        for _, update in ipairs(updates) do
            lastUpdateId = math.max(lastUpdateId, update.update_id)
            if update.message and update.message.text then
                local msg = string.lower(update.message.text)
                if tick() - lastReportTime < 300 then
                    if msg == "no" then
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "👑 Admin", Content = "😡 Thằng cha mày báo lỗi cc làm gì có lỗi", Duration = 10 })
                        SendTelegram("✔️ Đã nhận sẽ được gửi notify đến người chơi...")
                    elseif msg == "ok" then
                        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "👑 Admin", Content = "🤑 Admin đã nhận đợi fix nhé. Cảm ơn bro", Duration = 10 })
                        SendTelegram("✔️ Đã nhận sẽ được gửi notify đến người chơi...")
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

NotifyTab:Section({ Title = "Gợi ý thêm chức năng" })

NotifyTab:Input({
    Title = "Nhập gợi ý chức năng",
    Value = "",
    Placeholder = "Mô tả chức năng bạn muốn thêm...",
    Callback = function(text) suggestInput = text end,
})

NotifyTab:Button({
    Title = "Gửi lệnh thêm chức năng",
    Callback = function()
        if suggestInput == "" or string.match(suggestInput, "^%s*$") then
            WindUI:Notify({ Title = "⚠️ Không hợp lệ", Content = "Vui lòng nhập gợi ý chức năng trước khi gửi", Duration = 5 })
            return
        end
        WindUI:Notify({ Title = "🕑 Đang xử lý...", Content = "🐧 Đợi admin xem và thêm nhé bro", Duration = 5 })
        local gameName = GetGameName()
        local placeInfo = GetPlaceInfo()
        local devName = "Unknown"
        pcall(function()
            if placeInfo and placeInfo.Creator then
                devName = placeInfo.Creator.Name or "Unknown"
                if placeInfo.Creator.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.Group then devName = devName .. " (Group)" end
            end
        end)
        local ping = GetPing()
        local region = regionCache or "Unknown"
        local memMB = math.floor(Stats:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb())
        local lifetime = FormatLifetime(tick() - serverStartTime)
        local content = string.format(
            "👤 *Thông tin người dùng báo cáo* 🔔\n\n*Username:* %s | @%s\n*Time of sending:* %s | %s\n*Report notes:* %s\n\n📊 *Thông tin server*\n\n🎮 *Game:* %s\n👨‍💻 *Developer:* %s\n👥 *Players:* %d/%d\n⚡ *Server Performance:* %d%%\n📍 *Location version:* %s\n🌍 *Server location:* %s\n📡 *Server update latency:* %dms\n🔑 *JobID:* `%s`\n🆔 *Place ID:* %d\n📌 *Location version:* %s\n🕐 *Startup time:* %s\n💾 *Use memory:* %d MB\n⚙️ *Engine version:* Client\n⏱️ *Server lifetime:* %s\n🎯 *FPS:* %d | *Ping:* %dms\n🌐 *Region:* %s",
            LocalPlayer.DisplayName, LocalPlayer.Name, GetTimeReal(), GetDateReal(), suggestInput,
            gameName, devName, #Players:GetPlayers(), Players.MaxPlayers, math.min(100, math.floor((currentFPSValue / 60) * 100)),
            tostring(game.PlaceVersion or "Unknown"), region, ping, game.JobId, game.PlaceId,
            tostring(game.PlaceVersion or "Unknown"), os.date("%H:%M:%S"), memMB, lifetime, currentFPSValue, ping, region
        )
        local ok, err = SendTelegram(content)
        if ok then suggestInput = ""
        else WindUI:Notify({ Title = "❌ Lỗi gửi", Content = tostring(err), Duration = 5 }) end
    end,
})

-- ============================================================
-- RESPAWN HANDLER
-- ============================================================
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
    floating = false
    stopFollow()
    destroyPlatform()
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(2) do
        if autoSend and game.JobId ~= lastJobId then
            lastJobId = game.JobId
            SendAutoMessage("🔄 Đã đổi server", "JobID mới: `" .. game.JobId .. "`")
        end
    end
end)
end)
if not otherOk then
    warn("[Other amenities] lỗi khi load: " .. tostring(otherErr))
    pcall(function() WindUI:Notify({ Title = "Other amenities", Content = "Lỗi load: " .. tostring(otherErr), Duration = 8 }) end)
end
end


-- ============================================================
-- ===== CONFIG LOAD & SAVE TỰ ĐỘNG ====================
-- ============================================================
pcall(function()
    if myConfig and type(myConfig.Load) == "function" then
        myConfig:Load()
    end
end)

pcall(function()
    Window:OnClose(function()
        if myConfig and type(myConfig.Save) == "function" then
            myConfig:Save()
        end
    end)
end)
